```
1→"""Contextual reminders with decay."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import UTC
     7→from datetime import datetime as _dt
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
    11→    _FEEDBACK_URL,
    12→    _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD,
    13→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state import StateModel, get_strict_score, path_scoped_findings
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _compute_fp_rates(findings: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
    21→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical findings."""
    22→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    23→    for finding in findings.values():
    24→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    25→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    26→            detector = "structural"
    27→        zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    28→        key = (detector, zone)
    29→        if key not in counts:
    30→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    31→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    32→        if finding.get("status") == "false_positive":
    33→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    34→
    35→    rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = {}
    36→    for key, stat in counts.items():
    37→        if stat["total"] >= 5 and stat["fp"] > 0:
    38→            rates[key] = stat["fp"] / stat["total"]
    39→    return rates
    40→
    41→
    42→def _auto_fixer_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    43→    auto_fix_actions = [
    44→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    45→    ]
    46→    if not auto_fix_actions:
    47→        return []
    48→    total = sum(action.get("count", 0) for action in auto_fix_actions)
    49→    if total <= 0:
    50→        return []
    51→    first_cmd = auto_fix_actions[0].get("command", "desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run")
    52→    return [
    53→        {
    54→            "type": "auto_fixers_available",
    55→            "message": f"{total} findings are auto-fixable. Run `{first_cmd}`.",
    56→            "command": first_cmd,
    57→        }
    58→    ]
    59→
    60→
    61→def _rescan_needed_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    62→    if command not in {"fix", "resolve", "ignore"}:
    63→        return []
    64→    return [
    65→        {
    66→            "type": "rescan_needed",
    67→            "message": "Rescan to verify — cascading effects may create new findings.",
    68→            "command": "desloppify scan",
    69→        }
    70→    ]
    71→
    72→
    73→def _badge_reminder(strict_score: float | None, badge: dict) -> list[dict]:
    74→    eligible_for_badge = (
    75→        strict_score is not None
    76→        and strict_score >= 90
    77→        and badge.get("generated")
    78→        and not badge.get("in_readme")
    79→    )
    80→    if not eligible_for_badge:
    81→        return []
    82→    badge_path = str(badge.get("path") or "scorecard.png")
    83→    return [
    84→        {
    85→            "type": "badge_recommendation",
    86→            "message": (
    87→                "Score is above 90! Add the scorecard to your README: "
    88→                f'<img src="{badge_path}" width="100%">'
    89→            ),
    90→            "command": None,
    91→        }
    92→    ]
    93→
    94→
    95→def _wontfix_debt_reminders(state: StateModel, debt: dict, command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    96→    reminders: list[dict] = []
    97→    if debt.get("trend") == "growing":
    98→        reminders.append(
    99→            {
   100→                "type": "wontfix_growing",
   101→                "message": "Wontfix debt is growing. Review stale decisions: `desloppify show --status wontfix`.",
   102→                "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   103→            }
   104→        )
   105→
   106→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   107→    if scan_count < 20 or command != "scan":
   108→        return reminders
   109→
   110→    stale_wontfix = []
   111→    for finding in state.get("findings", {}).values():
   112→        if finding.get("status") != "wontfix":
   113→            continue
   114→        resolved_at = finding.get("resolved_at")
   115→        if not resolved_at:
   116→            continue
   117→        try:
   118→            age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - _dt.fromisoformat(resolved_at)).days
   119→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   120→            log_best_effort_failure(
   121→                logger, f"parse wontfix timestamp {resolved_at!r}", exc
   122→            )
   123→            continue
   124→        if age_days > 60:
   125→            stale_wontfix.append(finding)
   126→
   127→    if stale_wontfix:
   128→        reminders.append(
   129→            {
   130→                "type": "wontfix_stale",
   131→                "message": (
   132→                    f"{len(stale_wontfix)} wontfix item(s) are >60 days old. "
   133→                    f"Has anything changed? Review with: "
   134→                    f'`desloppify show "*" --status wontfix`'
   135→                ),
   136→                "command": 'desloppify show "*" --status wontfix',
   137→            }
   138→        )
   139→    return reminders
   140→
   141→
   142→def _ignore_suppression_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   143→    """Nudge when ignores/suppression are high enough to mask signal quality."""
   144→    integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {}) or {}
   145→    ignored = int(integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   146→    suppressed_pct = float(integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if ignored < 10 and suppressed_pct < 30.0:
   148→        return []
   149→    return [
   150→        {
   151→            "type": "ignore_suppression_high",
   152→            "message": (
   153→                f"Ignore suppression is high ({ignored} ignored, {suppressed_pct:.1f}% suppressed). "
   154→                "Revisit broad ignore patterns and resolve stale suppressions."
   155→            ),
   156→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   157→        }
   158→    ]
   159→
   160→
   161→def _stagnation_reminders(dimensions: dict) -> list[dict]:
   162→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   163→    for dim in dimensions.get("stagnant_dimensions", []):
   164→        strict = dim.get("strict", 0)
   165→        if strict >= 99:
   166→            message = (
   167→                f"{dim['name']} has been at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   168→                f"The remaining items may be worth marking as wontfix if they're intentional."
   169→            )
   170→        else:
   171→            message = (
   172→                f"{dim['name']} has been stuck at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
   173→                f"Try tackling it from a different angle — run `desloppify next` to find the right entry point."
   174→            )
   175→        reminders.append(
   176→            {
   177→                "type": "stagnant_nudge",
   178→                "message": message,
   179→                "command": None,
   180→            }
   181→        )
   182→    return reminders
   183→
   184→
   185→def _dry_run_reminder(actions: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   186→    if not actions or actions[0].get("type") != "auto_fix":
   187→        return []
   188→    return [
   189→        {
   190→            "type": "dry_run_first",
   191→            "message": "Always --dry-run first, review changes, then apply.",
   192→            "command": None,
   193→        }
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→
   197→def _zone_classification_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   198→    zone_dist = state.get("zone_distribution")
   199→    if not zone_dist:
   200→        return []
   201→    non_prod = sum(value for key, value in zone_dist.items() if key != "production")
   202→    if non_prod <= 0:
   203→        return []
   204→    total = sum(zone_dist.values())
   205→    parts = [
   206→        f"{value} {key}"
   207→        for key, value in sorted(zone_dist.items())
   208→        if key != "production" and value > 0
   209→    ]
   210→    return [
   211→        {
   212→            "type": "zone_classification",
   213→            "message": (
   214→                f"{non_prod} of {total} files classified as non-production "
   215→                f"({', '.join(parts)}). "
   216→                f"Override with `desloppify zone set <file> production` "
   217→                f"if any are misclassified."
   218→            ),
   219→            "command": "desloppify zone show",
   220→        }
   221→    ]
   222→
   223→
   224→def _fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float]) -> list[dict]:
   225→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   226→    for (detector, zone), rate in fp_rates.items():
   227→        if rate <= 0.3:
   228→            continue
   229→        pct = round(rate * 100)
   230→        reminders.append(
   231→            {
   232→                "type": f"fp_calibration_{detector}_{zone}",
   233→                "message": (
   234→                    f"{pct}% of {detector} findings in {zone} zone are false positives. "
   235→                    f"Consider reviewing detection rules for {zone} files."
   236→                ),
   237→                "command": None,
   238→            }
   239→        )
   240→    return reminders
   241→
   242→
   243→def _review_queue_reminders(
   244→    state: StateModel,
   245→    scoped_findings: dict,
   246→    command: str | None,
   247→    strict_score: float | None,
   248→) -> list[dict]:
   249→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   250→    open_review = [
   251→        finding
   252→        for finding in scoped_findings.values()
   253→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   254→    ]
   255→    if open_review:
   256→        uninvestigated = [
   257→            finding
   258→            for finding in open_review
   259→            if not finding.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   260→        ]
   261→        if uninvestigated:
   262→            reminders.append(
   263→                {
   264→                    "type": "review_findings_pending",
   265→                    "message": f"{len(uninvestigated)} review finding(s) need investigation. "
   266→                    f"Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue.",
   267→                    "command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   268→                }
   269→            )
   270→
   271→    if command == "resolve" and state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   272→        reminders.append(
   273→            {
   274→                "type": "rereview_needed",
   275→                "message": "Subjective results may be stale after resolve. Re-run "
   276→                "`desloppify review --prepare` to refresh, or reset with "
   277→                "`desloppify scan --reset-subjective` before a clean rerun.",
   278→                "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   283→    if not review_cache.get("files"):
   284→        current_strict = strict_score or 0
   285→        if current_strict >= 80:
   286→            reminders.append(
   287→                {
   288→                    "type": "review_not_run",
   289→                    "message": (
   290→                        "Mechanical checks look good! Run a subjective design review "
   291→                        "to catch issues linters miss: desloppify review --prepare"
   292→                    ),
   293→                    "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   294→                }
   295→            )
   296→
   297→    return reminders
   298→
   299→
   300→def _review_staleness_reminder(state: StateModel, config: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   301→    review_max_age = (config or {}).get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   302→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   303→    if review_max_age <= 0 or not review_cache.get("files"):
   304→        return []
   305→    try:
   306→        oldest_str = min(
   307→            review["reviewed_at"]
   308→            for review in review_cache["files"].values()
   309→            if review.get("reviewed_at")
   310→        )
   311→        oldest = _dt.fromisoformat(oldest_str)
   312→        age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - oldest).days
   313→    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   314→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "parse oldest review timestamp", exc)
   315→        return []
   316→    if age_days <= review_max_age:
   317→        return []
   318→    return [
   319→        {
   320→            "type": "review_stale",
   321→            "message": f"Design review is {age_days} days old — run: desloppify review --prepare",
   322→            "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   323→        }
   324→    ]
   325→
   326→
   327→def _feedback_reminder(
   328→    state: StateModel,
   329→    phase: str,
   330→    command: str | None,
   331→    fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float],
   332→) -> list[dict]:
   333→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   334→    if scan_count < 2 or command != "scan":
   335→        return []
   336→    high_fp_dets = [
   337→        detector for (detector, _zone), rate in fp_rates.items() if rate > 0.3
   338→    ]
   339→    if high_fp_dets:
   340→        nudge_msg = (
   341→            f"Some detectors have high false-positive rates ({', '.join(high_fp_dets)}). "
   342→            f"If patterns are being misclassified, file an issue at "
   343→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} with the file and expected behavior — "
   344→            f"it helps calibrate detection for everyone."
   345→        )
   346→    elif phase == "stagnation":
   347→        nudge_msg = (
   348→            f"Score has plateaued — if you suspect desloppify is missing patterns "
   349→            f"or not capturing something it should, file an issue at "
   350→            f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} describing what you expected. "
   351→            f"Gaps in detection are a common cause of stagnation."
   352→        )
   353→    else:
   354→        nudge_msg = (
   355→            f"If you notice patterns desloppify doesn't detect, false positives, "
   356→            f"or findings that seem off, file an issue at {_FEEDBACK_URL} — "
   357→            f"include the file and what you expected. It helps improve the tool."
   358→        )
   359→    return [
   360→        {
   361→            "type": "feedback_nudge",
   362→            "message": nudge_msg,
   363→            "command": None,
   364→        }
   365→    ]
   366→
   367→
   368→_REMINDER_METADATA: dict[str, tuple[int, str]] = {
   369→    # Highest urgency operational nudges.
   370→    "rescan_needed": (1, "high"),
   371→    "ignore_suppression_high": (1, "high"),
   372→    "review_findings_pending": (1, "high"),
   373→    "rereview_needed": (1, "high"),
   374→    # Medium urgency quality/process nudges.
   375→    "wontfix_growing": (2, "medium"),
   376→    "wontfix_stale": (2, "medium"),
   377→    "stagnant_nudge": (2, "medium"),
   378→    "review_stale": (2, "medium"),
   379→    "auto_fixers_available": (2, "medium"),
   380→    "zone_classification": (2, "medium"),
   381→    "fp_calibration": (2, "medium"),
   382→    # Informational nudges.
   383→    "badge_recommendation": (3, "low"),
   384→    "dry_run_first": (3, "low"),
   385→    "feedback_nudge": (3, "low"),
   386→    "review_not_run": (3, "low"),
   387→    "report_scores": (3, "low"),
   388→}
   389→
   390→
   391→def _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   392→    """Attach stable priority/severity metadata and sort by priority."""
   393→    decorated: list[dict] = []
   394→    for reminder in reminders:
   395→        reminder_type = str(reminder.get("type", ""))
   396→        key = reminder_type
   397→        if reminder_type.startswith("fp_calibration_"):
   398→            key = "fp_calibration"
   399→        priority, severity = _REMINDER_METADATA.get(key, (3, "low"))
   400→        clone = dict(reminder)
   401→        clone.setdefault("priority", priority)
   402→        clone.setdefault("severity", severity)
   403→        decorated.append(clone)
   404→    decorated.sort(key=lambda item: (int(item.get("priority", 3)), str(item.get("type", ""))))
   405→    return decorated
   406→
   407→
   408→def _report_scores_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   409→    if command != "scan":
   410→        return []
   411→    return [
   412→        {
   413→            "type": "report_scores",
   414→            "message": (
   415→                "ALWAYS share ALL scores with the user: overall, objective, and strict, "
   416→                "plus every dimension score (lenient + strict), including subjective dimensions. "
   417→                "The goal is to maximize strict scores."
   418→            ),
   419→            "command": None,
   420→            "no_decay": True,
   421→        }
   422→    ]
   423→
   424→
   425→def _apply_decay(
   426→    reminders: list[dict], reminder_history: dict
   427→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   428→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   429→    for reminder in reminders:
   430→        if reminder.get("no_decay"):
   431→            filtered.append(reminder)
   432→            continue
   433→        count = reminder_history.get(reminder["type"], 0)
   434→        if count < _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD:
   435→            filtered.append(reminder)
   436→
   437→    updated_history = dict(reminder_history)
   438→    for reminder in filtered:
   439→        updated_history[reminder["type"]] = updated_history.get(reminder["type"], 0) + 1
   440→    return filtered, updated_history
   441→
   442→
   443→def _compute_reminders(
   444→    state: StateModel,
   445→    lang: str | None,
   446→    phase: str,
   447→    debt: dict,
   448→    actions: list[dict],
   449→    dimensions: dict,
   450→    badge: dict,
   451→    command: str | None,
   452→    config: dict | None = None,
   453→) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   454→    """Compute context-specific reminders, suppressing those shown too many times."""
   455→    del lang  # Reserved for future language-specific rules.
   456→
   457→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
   458→    reminder_history = state.get("reminder_history", {})
   459→    scoped_findings = path_scoped_findings(
   460→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   461→    )
   462→    fp_rates = _compute_fp_rates(scoped_findings)
   463→
   464→    reminders: list[dict] = []
   465→    reminders.extend(_report_scores_reminder(command))
   466→    reminders.extend(_auto_fixer_reminder(actions))
   467→    reminders.extend(_rescan_needed_reminder(command))
   468→    reminders.extend(_badge_reminder(strict_score, badge))
   469→    reminders.extend(_wontfix_debt_reminders(state, debt, command))
   470→    reminders.extend(_ignore_suppression_reminder(state))
   471→    reminders.extend(_stagnation_reminders(dimensions))
   472→    reminders.extend(_dry_run_reminder(actions))
   473→    reminders.extend(_zone_classification_reminder(state))
   474→    reminders.extend(_fp_calibration_reminders(fp_rates))
   475→    reminders.extend(
   476→        _review_queue_reminders(state, scoped_findings, command, strict_score)
   477→    )
   478→    reminders.extend(_review_staleness_reminder(state, config))
   479→    reminders.extend(_feedback_reminder(state, phase, command, fp_rates))
   480→
   481→    reminders = _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders)
   482→    return _apply_decay(reminders, reminder_history)
   483→
```

```
1→"""Execution strategy engine for narrative lanes and parallelization hints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Iterable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import (
     9→    _DETECTOR_CASCADE,
    10→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE,
    11→)
    12→
    13→
    14→def open_files_by_detector(findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    15→    """Collect file sets of open findings by detector."""
    16→    by_detector: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    17→    for finding in findings.values():
    18→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    19→            continue
    20→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    21→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    22→            detector = "structural"
    23→        file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    24→        if not file_path:
    25→            by_detector.setdefault(detector, set())
    26→            continue
    27→        by_detector.setdefault(detector, set()).add(file_path)
    28→    return by_detector
    29→
    30→
    31→def compute_fixer_leverage(
    32→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    33→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    34→    phase: str,
    35→    _lang: str | None,
    36→) -> dict[str, float | int | str]:
    37→    """Estimate how much value automated fixers would deliver."""
    38→    auto_fixable = sum(
    39→        action.get("count", 0) for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    40→    )
    41→    total = sum(by_detector.values())
    42→    coverage = auto_fixable / total if total > 0 else 0.0
    43→    total_impact = sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in actions)
    44→    auto_impact = sum(
    45→        action.get("impact", 0)
    46→        for action in actions
    47→        if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"
    48→    )
    49→    impact_ratio = auto_impact / total_impact if total_impact > 0 else 0.0
    50→
    51→    if coverage == 0:
    52→        recommendation = "none"
    53→    elif coverage > 0.4 or impact_ratio > 0.3:
    54→        recommendation = "strong"
    55→    elif phase in ("first_scan", "stagnation", "regression") and coverage > 0.15:
    56→        recommendation = "strong"
    57→    elif coverage > 0.1:
    58→        recommendation = "moderate"
    59→    else:
    60→        recommendation = "none"
    61→
    62→    return {
    63→        "auto_fixable_count": auto_fixable,
    64→        "total_count": total,
    65→        "coverage": round(coverage, 3),
    66→        "impact_ratio": round(impact_ratio, 3),
    67→        "recommendation": recommendation,
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _cleanup_lane_actions(actions: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    72→    cascade_rank = {detector: idx for idx, detector in enumerate(_DETECTOR_CASCADE)}
    73→
    74→    def sort_key(action: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, float]:
    75→        detector = action.get("detector", "")
    76→        return (cascade_rank.get(detector, 99), -action.get("impact", 0))
    77→
    78→    return sorted(actions, key=sort_key)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _files_for_actions(
    82→    actions: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]]
    83→) -> set[str]:
    84→    files: set[str] = set()
    85→    for action in actions:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector")
    87→        if detector and detector in files_by_detector:
    88→            files |= files_by_detector[detector]
    89→    return files
    90→
    91→
    92→def _group_by_file_overlap(
    93→    action_files: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]],
    94→) -> list[list[tuple[dict[str, Any], set[str]]]]:
    95→    """Group actions whose file sets overlap using union-find."""
    96→    item_count = len(action_files)
    97→    if item_count == 0:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    parent = list(range(item_count))
   101→
   102→    def find(index: int) -> int:
   103→        while parent[index] != index:
   104→            parent[index] = parent[parent[index]]
   105→            index = parent[index]
   106→        return index
   107→
   108→    def union(left: int, right: int) -> None:
   109→        left_root, right_root = find(left), find(right)
   110→        if left_root != right_root:
   111→            parent[left_root] = right_root
   112→
   113→    for left in range(item_count):
   114→        for right in range(left + 1, item_count):
   115→            if action_files[left][1] & action_files[right][1]:
   116→                union(left, right)
   117→
   118→    grouped_indices: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
   119→    for index in range(item_count):
   120→        grouped_indices.setdefault(find(index), []).append(index)
   121→
   122→    return [
   123→        [action_files[index] for index in indices]
   124→        for indices in grouped_indices.values()
   125→    ]
   126→
   127→
   128→def _refactor_lanes(
   129→    refactor_actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   130→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   131→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   132→    """Split refactor work into independent lanes by file overlap."""
   133→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   134→    action_files = [
   135→        (action, files_by_detector.get(action.get("detector"), set()))
   136→        for action in refactor_actions
   137→    ]
   138→
   139→    test_coverage_actions = [
   140→        (action, files)
   141→        for action, files in action_files
   142→        if action.get("detector") == "test_coverage"
   143→    ]
   144→    other_actions = [
   145→        (action, files)
   146→        for action, files in action_files
   147→        if action.get("detector") != "test_coverage"
   148→    ]
   149→
   150→    groups = _group_by_file_overlap(other_actions)
   151→    for index, group in enumerate(groups):
   152→        lane_name = f"refactor_{index}" if len(groups) > 1 else "refactor"
   153→        lanes[lane_name] = {
   154→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in group],
   155→            "file_count": len(
   156→                _files_for_actions((action for action, _ in group), files_by_detector)
   157→            ),
   158→            "total_impact": round(
   159→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in group), 1
   160→            ),
   161→            "automation": "manual",
   162→            "run_first": False,
   163→        }
   164→
   165→    if test_coverage_actions:
   166→        lanes["test_coverage"] = {
   167→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action, _ in test_coverage_actions],
   168→            "file_count": len(
   169→                _files_for_actions(
   170→                    (action for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), files_by_detector
   171→                )
   172→            ),
   173→            "total_impact": round(
   174→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action, _ in test_coverage_actions), 1
   175→            ),
   176→            "automation": "manual",
   177→            "run_first": False,
   178→        }
   179→
   180→    return lanes
   181→
   182→
   183→def compute_lanes(
   184→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   185→    files_by_detector: dict[str, set[str]],
   186→) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
   187→    """Partition actions into parallelizable work lanes."""
   188→    lanes: dict[str, dict] = {}
   189→
   190→    cleanup_actions = _cleanup_lane_actions(
   191→        [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
   192→    )
   193→    reorganize_actions = [
   194→        action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "reorganize"
   195→    ]
   196→    debt_actions = [action for action in actions if action.get("type") == "debt_review"]
   197→    refactor_actions = [
   198→        action
   199→        for action in actions
   200→        if action.get("type") not in {"auto_fix", "reorganize", "debt_review"}
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    if cleanup_actions:
   204→        cleanup_files = _files_for_actions(cleanup_actions, files_by_detector)
   205→        lanes["cleanup"] = {
   206→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in cleanup_actions],
   207→            "file_count": len(cleanup_files),
   208→            "total_impact": round(
   209→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in cleanup_actions), 1
   210→            ),
   211→            "automation": "full",
   212→            "run_first": False,
   213→        }
   214→
   215→    if reorganize_actions:
   216→        lanes["restructure"] = {
   217→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in reorganize_actions],
   218→            "file_count": len(
   219→                _files_for_actions(reorganize_actions, files_by_detector)
   220→            ),
   221→            "total_impact": round(
   222→                sum(action.get("impact", 0) for action in reorganize_actions), 1
   223→            ),
   224→            "automation": "manual",
   225→            "run_first": False,
   226→        }
   227→
   228→    if refactor_actions:
   229→        lanes.update(_refactor_lanes(refactor_actions, files_by_detector))
   230→
   231→    if debt_actions:
   232→        lanes["debt_review"] = {
   233→            "actions": [action["priority"] for action in debt_actions],
   234→            "file_count": 0,
   235→            "total_impact": 0.0,
   236→            "automation": "manual",
   237→            "run_first": False,
   238→        }
   239→
   240→    if "cleanup" in lanes:
   241→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   242→            if lane_name == "cleanup":
   243→                continue
   244→            lane_files = _files_for_actions(
   245→                (action for action in actions if action["priority"] in lane["actions"]),
   246→                files_by_detector,
   247→            )
   248→            if cleanup_files & lane_files:
   249→                lanes["cleanup"]["run_first"] = True
   250→                break
   251→
   252→    return lanes
   253→
   254→
   255→def _significant_lane(lane_name: str, lane: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   256→    if lane_name == "debt_review" or lane.get("run_first"):
   257→        return False
   258→    return lane.get("file_count", 0) >= 5 or lane.get("total_impact", 0) >= 1.0
   259→
   260→
   261→def compute_strategy_hint(
   262→    fixer_leverage: dict[str, Any],
   263→    lanes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   264→    can_parallelize: bool,
   265→    phase: str,
   266→) -> str:
   267→    """Generate one- or two-sentence execution strategy guidance."""
   268→    recommendation = fixer_leverage.get("recommendation", "none")
   269→    coverage_pct = round(fixer_leverage.get("coverage", 0) * 100)
   270→    lane_count = sum(
   271→        1
   272→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   273→        if lane_name != "debt_review" and not lane.get("run_first")
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    if recommendation == "strong" and can_parallelize:
   277→        return (
   278→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   279→            f"Then {lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   280→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   281→        )
   282→    if recommendation == "strong":
   283→        return (
   284→            f"Run fixers first — they cover {coverage_pct}% of findings. "
   285→            "Then rescan to verify."
   286→        )
   287→    if can_parallelize:
   288→        return (
   289→            f"{lane_count} independent workstreams, safe to parallelize. "
   290→            "Rescan after each phase to verify."
   291→        )
   292→    if phase == "maintenance":
   293→        return "Maintenance mode — address new findings as they appear."
   294→    if phase == "stagnation":
   295→        return "Try a different dimension to break the plateau."
   296→    return "Work through actions in priority order. Rescan after each fix to track progress."
   297→
   298→
   299→def compute_strategy(
   300→    findings: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   301→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   302→    actions: list[dict[str, Any]],
   303→    phase: str,
   304→    lang: str | None,
   305→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   306→    """Orchestrate strategy computation and annotate actions with lanes."""
   307→    files_by_detector = open_files_by_detector(findings)
   308→    fixer_leverage = compute_fixer_leverage(by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   309→    lanes = compute_lanes(actions, files_by_detector)
   310→
   311→    action_lane: dict[int, str] = {}
   312→    for lane_name, lane in lanes.items():
   313→        for priority in lane["actions"]:
   314→            action_lane[priority] = lane_name
   315→    for action in actions:
   316→        action["lane"] = action_lane.get(action["priority"])
   317→
   318→    significant_non_blocked = [
   319→        (lane_name, lane)
   320→        for lane_name, lane in lanes.items()
   321→        if _significant_lane(lane_name, lane)
   322→    ]
   323→    can_parallelize = len(significant_non_blocked) >= 2
   324→
   325→    hint = compute_strategy_hint(fixer_leverage, lanes, can_parallelize, phase)
   326→    review_action = next(
   327→        (action for action in actions if action.get("detector") == "review"), None
   328→    )
   329→    if review_action:
   330→        hint += f" Review: {review_action['count']} finding(s) — `desloppify show review --status open`."
   331→
   332→    return {
   333→        "fixer_leverage": fixer_leverage,
   334→        "lanes": {name: {**lane} for name, lane in lanes.items()},
   335→        "can_parallelize": can_parallelize,
   336→        "hint": hint,
   337→    }
   338→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    return (
    93→        detector == scope
    94→        or filepath == scope
    95→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   100→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   101→
   102→
   103→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   104→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   105→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   106→    target = cleaned.lower()
   107→
   108→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   109→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   110→            return str(dim_key)
   111→    return slugify(cleaned)
   112→
   113→
   114→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   115→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   116→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   117→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   118→    return {
   119→        cleaned.lower(),
   120→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   121→        slugify(cleaned),
   122→        canonical.lower(),
   123→        slugify(canonical),
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   128→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   129→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   130→    if not lang:
   131→        return None
   132→
   133→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   134→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   138→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   142→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   143→        return None
   144→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   145→
   146→
   147→def primary_command_for_finding(
   148→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   149→) -> str:
   150→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   151→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   152→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   153→        available_fixers = [
   154→            fixer
   155→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   156→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   157→        ]
   158→        if available_fixers:
   159→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   160→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   161→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   162→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   163→        return "desloppify show subjective_review --status open"
   164→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   165→
   166→
   167→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   168→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   169→    if not dim_scores:
   170→        return {}
   171→
   172→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   173→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   174→    for entry in entries:
   175→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   176→        if not name:
   177→            continue
   178→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   179→        [REDACTED](name)
   180→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   181→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   182→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   183→            if not key:
   184→                continue
   185→            aliases.add(key)
   186→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   187→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   188→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   189→        for alias in aliases:
   190→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   191→    return scores
   192→
   193→
   194→def build_subjective_items(
   195→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   198→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   199→    if not dim_scores:
   200→        return []
   201→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   202→
   203→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   204→    if not subjective_entries:
   205→        return []
   206→    unassessed_dims = {
   207→        str(name).strip()
   208→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   209→            dim_scores
   210→        )
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   214→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   215→    for finding in findings.values():
   216→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   217→            continue
   218→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   219→        if not dim_key:
   220→            continue
   221→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   222→
   223→    items: list[dict] = []
   224→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   225→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   226→        if not name:
   227→            continue
   228→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   229→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   230→            continue
   231→
   232→        [REDACTED](name)
   233→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   234→        cli_keys = [
   235→            str(key).strip().lower()
   236→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   237→            if str(key).strip()
   238→        ]
   239→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   240→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   241→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   242→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   243→            name in unassessed_dims
   244→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   245→        )
   246→        if is_unassessed:
   247→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   248→        else:
   249→            if open_review > 0:
   250→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   251→            elif cli_keys:
   252→                primary_command = (
   253→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   254→                )
   255→            else:
   256→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   257→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%)"
   258→        items.append(
   259→            {
   260→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   261→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   262→                "file": ".",
   263→                "tier": 4,
   264→                "effective_tier": 4,
   265→                "confidence": "medium",
   266→                "summary": summary,
   267→                "detail": {
   268→                    "dimension_name": name,
   269→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   270→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   271→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   272→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   273→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   274→                },
   275→                "status": "open",
   276→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   277→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   278→            }
   279→        )
   280→    return items
   281→
   282→
   283→__all__ = [
   284→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   285→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   286→    "build_subjective_items",
   287→    "detail_dict",
   288→    "is_review_finding",
   289→    "is_subjective_finding",
   290→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   291→    "review_finding_weight",
   292→    "scope_matches",
   293→    "slugify",
   294→    "status_matches",
   295→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   296→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   297→]
   298→
```

```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    69→    if (
    70→        new.overall is None
    71→        or new.objective is None
    72→        or new.strict is None
    73→        or new.verified is None
    74→    ):
    75→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
    94→        print(
    95→            colorize(
    96→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
    97→                "yellow",
    98→            )
    99→        )
   100→        print(
   101→            colorize(
   102→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   103→                "dim",
   104→            )
   105→        )
   106→    elif status == "fixed":
   107→        print(
   108→            colorize(
   109→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   110→                "yellow",
   111→            )
   112→        )
   113→
   114→
   115→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   116→    *,
   117→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   118→    state: dict,
   119→    all_resolved: list[str],
   120→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   121→) -> None:
   122→    has_review = any(
   123→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   124→        for fid in all_resolved
   125→    )
   126→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   127→        return
   128→
   129→    stale_dims = sorted(
   130→        dim
   131→        for dim in {
   132→            str(
   133→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   134→            ).strip()
   135→            for fid in all_resolved
   136→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   137→        }
   138→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   139→    )
   140→    if not stale_dims:
   141→        return
   142→
   143→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   144→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   145→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   149→            "yellow",
   150→        )
   151→    )
   152→    print(
   153→        colorize(
   154→            "  Next subjective step: "
   155→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   156→            "dim",
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   162→    remaining = sum(
   163→        1
   164→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   165→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   166→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   167→    )
   168→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   169→    if remaining > 0:
   170→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   171→        print(
   172→            colorize(
   173→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   174→                "dim",
   175→            )
   176→        )
   177→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   178→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   179→    print()
   180→    return next_command
   181→
```

```
    34→def _lookup_from_last_query(number: int) -> str | None:
    35→    """Look up finding ID by 1-based index from the last query output."""
    36→    try:
    37→        qf = query_file_path()
    38→        if not qf.exists():
    39→            return None
    40→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
    41→        items = data.get("items") or data.get("findings") or []
    42→        if not items:
    43→            return None
    44→        if number < 1 or number > len(items):
    45→            return None
    46→        item = items[number - 1]
    47→        return item.get("id") or item.get("finding_id") or None
    48→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, KeyError, TypeError):
    49→        return None

    61→    if not finding:
    62→        print(

    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    effective_lang = lang_name or finding.get("lang", "unknown")
    69→

    75→                file=sys.stderr,
    76→            )
    77→            return
    78→        try:

    86→        ok = update_investigation(state, finding_id, text)
    87→        if not ok:

    93→        save_state(state, runtime.state_path)

    95→

   113→                "detail": finding.get("detail", {}),
   114→            },
   115→            "next_command": f'desloppify resolve fixed "{finding_id}" --note "<what you fixed>"',
   116→            "narrative": narrative,
   117→        }
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
   122→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
   123→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   124→
   125→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   126→    if not concerns:
   127→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
   131→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
   132→
   133→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
   134→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
   135→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
   136→        for ev in c.evidence:
   137→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
   138→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
   139→
   140→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
   141→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
   142→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
   143→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
   144→            if reasoning:
   145→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
   146→        print()
   147→
   148→
   149→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   150→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   151→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   152→    state = runtime.state
   153→    config = runtime.config
   154→
   155→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   159→    if stale_warning:
   160→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   161→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   162→    if skill_warning:
   163→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   164→
   165→    # Handle "show concerns" as a special view.
   166→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   167→    notes_file = getattr(args, "notes", None)
   168→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   169→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
   170→        _show_concerns(state, lang.name if lang else None)
   171→        return
   172→
   173→    # Numeric shorthand: look up from last query output.
   174→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().isdigit():
   175→        looked_up = _lookup_from_last_query(int(pattern_raw.strip()))
   176→        if looked_up:
   177→            pattern_raw = looked_up
   178→            # Rewrite args.pattern so downstream sees the resolved ID.
   179→            args.pattern = pattern_raw
   180→
   181→    # Single-finding detail mode.
   182→    if pattern_raw and "::" in pattern_raw:
   183→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
   184→        _show_single_finding(
   185→            state,
   186→            runtime,
   187→            pattern_raw,
   188→            lang.name if lang else None,
   189→            notes_file,
   190→        )
   191→        return
   192→
   193→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   194→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   195→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   196→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   197→        return
   198→
   199→    matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
   200→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   201→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   202→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   203→        state,
   204→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    if not matches:
   208→        print(colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow"))
   209→        write_query(
   210→            {
   211→                "command": "show",
   212→                "query": pattern,
   213→                "status_filter": status_filter,
   214→                "total": 0,
   215→                "findings": [],
   216→                "narrative": narrative,
   217→            }
   218→        )
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    (
   222→        surfaced_matches,
   223→        hidden_by_detector,
   224→        noise_budget,
   225→        global_noise_budget,
   226→        budget_warning,
   227→    ) = resolve_noise(
   228→        config,
   229→        matches,
   230→    )
   231→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   232→
   233→    payload = build_show_payload(
   234→        surfaced_matches,
   235→        pattern,
   236→        status_filter,
   237→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   238→            total_matches=len(matches),
   239→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   240→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   241→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   245→
   246→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   247→    if output_file:
   248→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   249→            return
   250→        raise SystemExit(1)
   251→
   252→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   253→    render_findings(
   254→        surfaced_matches,
   255→        pattern=pattern,
   256→        status_filter=status_filter,
   257→        show_code=show_code,
   258→        top=top,
   259→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   260→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   261→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   262→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   263→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   264→    )
   265→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches)
   266→    show_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→
   272→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   273→
```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )

   155→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:

   158→        state,

   163→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   164→    dim_scores: dict,
   165→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   166→) -> str | None:
   167→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   168→    lowest_name = None
   169→    lowest_score = 101.0
   170→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   171→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   172→        if not ds:
   173→            continue
   174→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   175→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   176→            lowest_score = strict_val
   177→            lowest_name = dim.name
   178→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   179→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   180→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   181→            lowest_score = strict_val
   182→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   183→    return lowest_name

   186→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   187→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   188→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   189→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   190→        return {}
   191→
   192→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   193→    for finding in findings.values():
   194→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   195→            continue
   196→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   197→            continue
   198→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   199→        dimension = ""
   200→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   201→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   202→        if not dimension:
   203→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   204→        if not dimension:
   205→            continue
   206→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   207→    return counts

   337→def show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   338→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   339→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   340→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   341→    if not lowest_name:
   342→        return
   343→
   344→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   345→    lowest_kind = None
   346→    lowest_score = 101.0
   347→    lowest_issues = 0
   348→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   349→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   350→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   351→            if ds:
   352→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   353→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   354→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   355→            break
   356→    else:
   357→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   358→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   359→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   360→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   361→                lowest_issues = 0
   362→                break
   363→
   364→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   365→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   366→            print(
   367→                colorize(
   368→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   369→                    "cyan",
   370→                )
   371→            )
   372→            print()
   373→            return
   374→
   375→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   376→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   377→        if target_dim:
   378→            impact = 0.0
   379→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   380→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   381→                    {

   383→                            "score": v["score"],
   384→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   385→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   386→                        }
   387→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   388→                        if "score" in v
   389→                    },
   390→                    potentials,
   391→                    det,
   392→                    lowest_issues,
   393→                )
   394→                if impact > 0:
   395→                    break
   396→
   397→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   398→            print(
   399→                colorize(
   400→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   401→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→
   407→
   408→def show_subjective_followup(
   409→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, target_strict_score: float
   410→) -> None:
   411→    """Show concrete subjective follow-up commands when applicable."""
   412→    if not dim_scores:
   413→        return
   414→
   415→    subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   416→    if not subjective:
   417→        return
   418→
   419→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   420→        state,
   421→        subjective,
   422→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   423→        max_quality_items=3,
   424→        max_integrity_items=5,
   425→    )
   426→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   427→        print()

   430→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict) -> None:
   431→    """Show concise action plan derived from narrative.actions."""
   432→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   433→    if not actions:
   434→        return
   435→
   436→    print(
   437→        colorize(
   438→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   439→            "yellow",
   440→        )
   441→    )
   442→    top = actions[0]

   445→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   446→        print()
   447→
   448→
   449→def _collect_structural_areas(
   450→    state: dict,
   451→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   452→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   453→
   454→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   455→    """
   456→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   457→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   458→    )
   459→
   460→    structural = [
   461→        f
   462→        for f in findings.values()
   463→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   464→    ]
   465→
   466→    if len(structural) < 5:
   467→        return None
   468→
   469→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   470→    for f in structural:
   471→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   472→        areas[area].append(f)
   473→
   474→    if len(areas) < 2:
   475→        return None
   476→
   477→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   478→
   479→
   480→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   481→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   482→    rows = []
   483→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   484→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   485→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   486→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   487→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   488→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   489→        rows.append(
   490→            [
   491→                area,
   492→                str(len(area_findings)),
   493→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   494→                str(open_count),
   495→                str(debt_count),
   496→                str(weight),
   497→            ]
   498→        )
   499→    return rows
   500→
   501→
   502→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   503→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   504→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   505→    if remaining > 0:
   506→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   507→
   508→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   509→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   510→    print(
   511→        colorize(
   512→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   513→        )
   514→    )
   515→    print(
   516→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   517→    )
   518→    print()
   519→
   520→
   521→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   522→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   523→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   524→    if sorted_areas is None:
   525→        return
   526→
   527→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   528→    print(
   529→        colorize(
   530→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   531→            "dim",
   532→        )
   533→    )
   534→    print()
   535→
   536→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   537→    print_table(
   538→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   539→    )
   540→
   541→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   542→
   543→
   544→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   545→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   546→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   547→    review_open = [
   548→        f
   549→        for f in findings.values()
   550→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   551→    ]
   552→    if not review_open:
   553→        return
   554→    uninvestigated = sum(
   555→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   556→    )
   557→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   558→    if uninvestigated:
   559→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   560→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   561→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   562→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   563→        print(
   564→            colorize(
   565→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   566→                "dim",
   567→            )
   568→        )
   569→    print()
   570→
   571→
   572→__all__ = [
   573→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   574→    "print_scan_completeness",
   575→    "print_scan_metrics",
   576→    "score_summary_lines",
   577→    "show_agent_plan",
   578→    "show_dimension_table",
   579→    "show_focus_suggestion",
   580→    "show_ignore_summary",
   581→    "show_review_summary",
   582→    "show_structural_areas",
   583→    "show_subjective_followup",
   584→    "show_tier_progress_table",
   585→    "write_status_query",
   586→]
   587→
```

```
    13→def _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(
    14→    state: dict, *, threshold: float = 90.0
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Return subjective dimensions with score below threshold and evidence issues."""
    17→    low_dims: list[str] = []
    18→    dimension_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    19→    if not isinstance(dimension_scores, dict):
    20→        return low_dims
    21→
    22→    for dim_name, payload in dimension_scores.items():
    23→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    24→            continue
    25→        score = payload.get("score")
    26→        detectors = payload.get("detectors", {})
    27→        subjective_payload = (
    28→            detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    29→            if isinstance(detectors, dict)
    30→            else None
    31→        )
    32→        if not isinstance(subjective_payload, dict):
    33→            continue
    34→        issues = subjective_payload.get("issues", 0)
    35→        if not isinstance(score, int | float) or not isinstance(
    36→            issues, int | float
    37→        ):
    38→            continue
    39→        if score < threshold and issues > 0:
    40→            low_dims.append(str(dim_name))
    41→    return low_dims

    50→

    52→        risk_flags
    53→    )
    54→    if not has_plan:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    print(colorize("  Narrative Plan:", "cyan"))
    58→    if isinstance(why_now, str) and why_now.strip():
    59→        print(colorize(f"    Why now: {why_now.strip()}", "dim"))
    60→

    62→        command = primary_action.get("command")
    63→        description = primary_action.get("description")

   157→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
   158→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
   159→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   161→        return []

   164→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
   165→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   166→            continue
   167→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
   168→        confidence = coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   169→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   170→            continue
   171→
   172→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
   173→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
   174→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   175→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
   176→
   177→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
   178→        if impact:
   179→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
   180→        if remediation:
   181→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
   182→        warnings.append(line)
   183→    return warnings
```
