   330→
   331→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   332→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   333→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   334→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   335→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   336→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   337→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   338→    confidence_reduced = (
   339→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   340→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   344→        return
   345→
   346→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   347→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   348→    strict_gap = (
   349→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   350→    )
   351→
   352→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   353→    actionable = (
   354→        stats.get("open", 0)
   355→        + wontfix
   356→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   357→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   358→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   359→    )
   360→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   363→
   364→    if wontfix > 5:
   365→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   366→            style = "red"
   367→            msg = (
   368→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   369→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   370→            )
   371→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   372→            style = "yellow"
   373→            msg = (
   374→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   375→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   376→            )
   377→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   378→            style = "yellow"
   379→            msg = (
   380→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   381→                "pts below lenient"
   382→            )
   383→        else:
   384→            style = "dim"
   385→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   386→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   387→
   388→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   389→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   390→        if dim_scores:
   391→            gaps = []
   392→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   393→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   394→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   395→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   396→                if gap > 0:
   397→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   398→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   399→            if gaps:
   400→                top = gaps[:2]
   401→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   402→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   403→
   404→    if ignored > 0:
   405→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   406→        print(
   407→            colorize(
   408→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   409→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   410→                style,
   411→            )
   412→        )
   413→        print(
   414→            colorize(
   415→                "    Suppressed findings still count against strict and verified scores",
   416→                "dim",
   417→            )
   418→        )
   419→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:
   420→        print(
   421→            colorize(
   422→                f"  {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   423→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   424→                "dim",
   425→            )
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    if confidence_reduced:
   429→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   430→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   431→        confidence = coerce_confidence(
   432→            score_confidence.get("confidence"),
   433→            default=1.0,
   434→        )
   435→        dim_text = ""
   436→        if isinstance(impacted_dimensions, list) and impacted_dimensions:
   437→            preview = ", ".join(str(item) for item in impacted_dimensions[:3])
   438→            if len(impacted_dimensions) > 3:
   439→                preview += f", +{len(impacted_dimensions) - 3} more"
   440→            dim_text = f" (dimensions: {preview})"
   441→        print(
   442→            colorize(
   443→                f"  ⚠ Score confidence reduced to {confidence * 100:.0f}%{dim_text}",
   444→                "yellow",
   445→            )
   446→        )
   447→        if isinstance(detectors, list):
   448→            for detector in detectors[:3]:
   449→                if not isinstance(detector, dict):
   450→                    continue
   451→                summary = str(detector.get("summary", "")).strip()
   452→                remediation = str(detector.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   453→                if summary:
   454→                    print(colorize(f"    - {summary}", "dim"))
   455→                if remediation:
   456→                    print(colorize(f"      Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   457→
   458→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 55, "dim"))
   459→    print()
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = ["show_post_scan_analysis", "show_score_integrity"]
   463→

   129→def _default_blind_packet_path() -> Path:
   130→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
   131→
   132→
   133→def _is_sha256_hex(raw: object) -> bool:
   134→    return (
   135→        isinstance(raw, str)
   136→        and len(raw) == 64
   137→        and all(ch in "0123456789abcdefABCDEF" for ch in raw)
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def _hash_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str | None:
   142→    try:
   143→        data = path.read_bytes()
   144→    except OSError:
   145→        return None
   146→    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   147→
   148→
   149→def _resolve_packet_path(raw_path: object) -> Path | None:
   150→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str):
   151→        return None
   152→    text = raw_path.strip()
   153→    if not text:
   154→        return None
   155→    path = Path(text)
   156→    return path if path.is_absolute() else get_project_root() / path
   157→
   158→
   159→def _assessment_provenance_status(
   160→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   161→    *,
   162→    import_file: str,
   163→) -> AssessmentProvenanceModel:
   164→    """Evaluate whether assessments come from a trusted blind batch artifact."""
   165→    provenance = findings_data["provenance"]
   166→    if not provenance:
   167→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   168→            trusted=False,
   169→            reason="missing provenance metadata",
   170→            import_file=import_file,
   171→        )
   172→
   173→    kind = str(provenance.get("kind", "")).strip()
   174→    if kind != _BLIND_PROVENANCE_KIND:
   175→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   176→            trusted=False,
   177→            reason=f"unsupported provenance kind: {kind or '<missing>'}",
   178→            import_file=import_file,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    if provenance.get("blind") is not True:
   182→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   183→            trusted=False,
   184→            reason="provenance is not marked blind=true",
   185→            import_file=import_file,
   186→        )
   187→
   188→    runner = str(provenance.get("runner", "")).strip().lower()
   189→    if runner not in _SUPPORTED_BLIND_REVIEW_RUNNERS:
   190→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   191→            trusted=False,
   192→            reason=f"unsupported runner in provenance: {runner or '<missing>'}",
   193→            import_file=import_file,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→    packet_hash = provenance.get("packet_sha256")
   197→    if not _is_sha256_hex(packet_hash):
   198→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   199→            trusted=False,
   200→            reason="missing or invalid packet_sha256 in provenance",
   201→            import_file=import_file,
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    packet_path = _resolve_packet_path(provenance.get("packet_path"))
   205→    if packet_path is None:
   206→        packet_path = _default_blind_packet_path()
   207→    if not packet_path.exists():
   208→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   209→            trusted=False,
   210→            reason=f"blind packet not found: {packet_path}",
   211→            import_file=import_file,
   212→        )
   213→    observed_hash = _hash_file_sha256(packet_path)
   214→    if observed_hash is None:
   215→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   216→            trusted=False,
   217→            reason=f"unable to hash blind packet: {packet_path}",
   218→            import_file=import_file,
   219→        )
   220→    if observed_hash != packet_hash:
   221→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   222→            trusted=False,
   223→            reason=(
   224→                "blind packet hash mismatch "
   225→                f"(expected {packet_hash[:12]}..., got {observed_hash[:12]}...)"
   226→            ),
   227→            import_file=import_file,
   228→        )
   229→
   230→    return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   231→        trusted=True,
   232→        reason="trusted blind subagent provenance",
   233→        runner=runner,
   234→        packet_path=str(packet_path),
   235→        packet_sha256=packet_hash,
   236→        import_file=import_file,
   237→    )
   238→

   240→def resolve_override_context(
   241→    *,
   242→    manual_override: bool,
   243→    manual_attest: str | None,
   244→    assessment_override: bool,
   245→    assessment_note: str | None,
   246→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   247→    """Support legacy assessment_* flags while preferring manual_* naming."""
   248→    override = bool(manual_override or assessment_override)
   249→    attest = (
   250→        manual_attest
   251→        if isinstance(manual_attest, str) and manual_attest.strip()
   252→        else assessment_note
   253→    )
   254→    if isinstance(attest, str):
   255→        attest = attest.strip()
   256→    return override, attest

   259→def _validate_attested_external_attestation(attest: str | None) -> str | None:
   260→    """Validate and normalize attestation text for attested external imports."""
   261→    if not isinstance(attest, str) or not attest.strip():
   262→        return None
   263→    text = attest.strip()
   264→    lowered = text.lower()
   265→    if all(phrase in lowered for phrase in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_REQUIRED_PHRASES):
   266→        return text
   267→    return None

   270→def _print_import_error_hints(
   271→    errors: list[str],
   272→    *,
   273→    import_file: str,
   274→    colorize_fn,
   275→) -> None:
   276→    """Print actionable retry commands for common import policy failures."""
   277→    joined = " ".join(err.lower() for err in errors)
   278→    quoted_import = shlex.quote(import_file)
   279→    import_cmd = (
   280→        "desloppify review --import "

   284→    validate_cmd = (
   285→        "desloppify review --validate-import "

   288→    )
   289→    findings_only_cmd = f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import}"
   290→
   291→    if "--attested-external requires --attest containing both" in joined:
   292→        print(
   293→            colorize_fn(
   294→                "  Hint: rerun with the required attestation template:",
   295→                "yellow",
   296→            ),
   297→            file=sys.stderr,
   298→        )
   299→        print(colorize_fn(f"    `{import_cmd}`", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   300→        print(
   301→            colorize_fn(
   302→                f"  Preflight without state changes: `{validate_cmd}`",
   303→                "dim",
   304→            ),
   305→            file=sys.stderr,
   306→        )
   307→        return
   308→
   309→    if (
   310→        "--attested-external requires valid blind packet provenance" in joined
   311→        or "supports runner='claude'" in joined
   312→    ):
   313→        print(
   314→            colorize_fn(

   316→                "yellow",
   317→            ),
   318→            file=sys.stderr,
   319→        )
   320→        print(colorize_fn(f"    `{import_cmd}`", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   321→        print(
   322→            colorize_fn(
   323→                f"  Preflight without state changes: `{validate_cmd}`",
   324→                "dim",
   325→            ),
   326→            file=sys.stderr,
   327→        )
   328→        print(

   335→
   336→
   337→def _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   338→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   339→    *,
   340→    import_file: str,
   341→    attested_external: bool,
   342→    attested_attest: str | None,
   343→    manual_override: bool,
   344→    manual_attest: str | None,
   345→    trusted_assessment_source: bool,
   346→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None,
   347→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   348→    """Apply trust gating for assessment imports (findings import always allowed)."""
   349→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   350→    has_assessments = bool(assessments)
   351→    assessment_count = len(assessments) if has_assessments else 0
   352→    provenance_status = _assessment_provenance_status(
   353→        findings_data, import_file=import_file
   354→    )
   355→    policy = AssessmentImportPolicyModel(
   356→        assessments_present=has_assessments,
   357→        assessment_count=int(assessment_count),
   358→        trusted=False,
   359→        mode="none",
   360→        reason="",
   361→        provenance=provenance_status,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→    def _attach_policy(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   365→        normalized = dict(payload)
   366→        normalized[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   367→        return normalized
   368→
   369→    if not has_assessments:
   370→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   371→
   372→    if trusted_assessment_source:
   373→        policy = replace(
   374→            policy,
   375→            mode="trusted_internal",
   376→            trusted=True,
   377→            reason=(trusted_assessment_label or "trusted internal run-batches import"),
   378→        )
   379→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   380→
   381→    if attested_external:
   382→        normalized_attest = _validate_attested_external_attestation(attested_attest)
   383→        if normalized_attest is None:
   384→            return None, [
   385→                "--attested-external requires --attest containing both "
   386→                "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'"
   387→            ]
   388→        if provenance_status.trusted is not True:
   389→            return None, [
   390→                "--attested-external requires valid blind packet provenance "
   391→                f"(current status: {provenance_status.reason or 'untrusted provenance'})"
   392→            ]
   393→        runner = provenance_status.runner.strip().lower()
   394→        if runner not in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_RUNNERS:
   395→            return None, [
   396→                "--attested-external currently supports runner='claude' provenance only"
   397→            ]
   398→        policy = replace(
   399→            policy,
   400→            mode="attested_external",
   401→            trusted=True,
   402→            reason="attested external blind subagent provenance",
   403→            attest=normalized_attest,
   404→        )
   405→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   406→
   407→    if manual_override:
   408→        if not isinstance(manual_attest, str) or not manual_attest.strip():
   409→            return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   410→        policy = replace(
   411→            policy,
   412→            mode="manual_override",
   413→            reason="manual override attested by operator",
   414→            attest=manual_attest.strip(),
   415→        )
   416→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   417→
   418→    policy = replace(policy, mode="findings_only")
   419→    if findings_data["provenance"]:
   420→        provenance_reason = provenance_status.reason.strip()
   421→        if provenance_status.trusted is True:
   422→            policy = replace(
   423→                policy,
   424→                reason=(
   425→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust even when provenance appears valid; "
   426→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   427→                ),
   428→            )
   429→        elif provenance_reason:
   430→            policy = replace(
   431→                policy,
   432→                reason=(
   433→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust "
   434→                    f"({provenance_reason}); run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   435→                ),
   436→            )
   437→        else:
   438→            policy = replace(
   439→                policy,
   440→                reason=(
   441→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust; "
   442→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   443→                ),
   444→            )
   445→    else:
   446→        policy = replace(
   447→            policy,
   448→            reason="missing trusted run-batches source; imported findings only",
   449→        )
   450→    payload = dict(findings_data)
   451→    payload["assessments"] = {}
   452→    payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   453→    return payload, []
   454→
   455→
   456→def _has_non_empty_strings(items: object) -> bool:
   457→    """Return True when ``items`` is a list with at least one non-empty string."""
   458→    return isinstance(items, list) and any(
   459→        isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in items
   460→    )
   461→
   462→
   463→def _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   464→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   465→    *,
   466→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   467→) -> list[str]:
   468→    """Validate strict holistic finding schema expected by issue import."""
   469→    findings = findings_data["findings"]
   470→
   471→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   472→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip():
   473→        _, dimension_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   474→        allowed_dimensions = set(dimension_prompts)
   475→
   476→    errors: list[str] = []
   477→    for idx, entry in enumerate(findings):
   478→        _normalized: ReviewFindingPayload | None
   479→        _normalized, entry_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
   480→            entry,
   481→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
   482→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions or None,
   483→            allow_dismissed=True,
   484→        )
   485→        for message in entry_errors:
   486→            if (
   487→                "is not allowed" in message
   488→                and lang_name
   489→                and "dimension '" in message
   490→            ):
   491→                message = message.replace(
   492→                    "is not allowed",
   493→                    f"is not valid for language '{lang_name}'",
   494→                )
   495→            errors.append(message)
   496→    return errors
   497→
   498→
   499→def _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings: object) -> set[str]:
   500→    """Return dimensions with explicit improvement guidance in findings payload."""
   501→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   502→        return set()
   503→    dims: set[str] = set()
   504→    for entry in findings:
   505→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   506→            continue
   507→        dim = entry.get("dimension")
   508→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   509→            continue
   510→        suggestion = entry.get("suggestion")
   511→        if isinstance(suggestion, str) and suggestion.strip():
   512→            dims.add(dim.strip())
   513→    return dims
   514→
   515→
   516→def _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(dimension_notes: object) -> set[str]:
   517→    """Return dimensions with concrete review evidence in dimension_notes payload."""
   518→    if not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   519→        return set()
   520→    dims: set[str] = set()
   521→    for dim, note in dimension_notes.items():
   522→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   523→            continue
   524→        if not isinstance(note, dict):
   525→            continue
   526→        if not _has_non_empty_strings(note.get("evidence")):
   527→            continue
   528→        dims.add(dim.strip())
   529→    return dims
   530→
   531→
   532→def _validate_assessment_feedback(
   533→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   534→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   535→    """Return dimensions missing required feedback and required low-score findings."""
   536→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   537→    if not assessments:
   538→        return [], []
   539→
   540→    finding_dims = _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings_data["findings"])
   541→    feedback_dims = set(finding_dims)
   542→    feedback_dims.update(
   543→        _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(findings_data["dimension_notes"])
   544→    )
   545→    missing_feedback: list[str] = []
   546→    missing_low_score_findings: list[str] = []
   547→    for dim_name, payload in assessments.items():
   548→        if not isinstance(dim_name, str) or not dim_name.strip():
   549→            continue
   550→        score = coerce_assessment_score(payload)
   551→        if score is None:
   552→            continue
   553→        if score_requires_dimension_finding(score) and dim_name not in finding_dims:
   554→            missing_low_score_findings.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   555→        if score_requires_explicit_feedback(score) and dim_name not in feedback_dims:
   556→            missing_feedback.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   557→    return sorted(missing_feedback), sorted(missing_low_score_findings)
   558→
   559→
   560→def _parse_and_validate_import(
   561→    import_file: str,
   562→    *,
   563→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   564→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   565→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   566→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   567→    attested_external: bool = False,
   568→    manual_override: bool = False,
   569→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   570→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   571→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   572→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   573→    """Parse and validate a review import file (pure function).
   574→
   575→    Returns ``(data, errors)`` where *data* is the normalized payload on
   576→    success, or ``None`` when errors prevent import.
   577→    """
   578→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   579→    if not findings_path.exists():
   580→        return None, [f"file not found: {import_file}"]
   581→    try:
   582→        findings_data = json.loads(findings_path.read_text())
   583→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   584→        return None, [f"error reading findings: {exc}"]
   585→
   586→    if isinstance(findings_data, list):
   587→        findings_data = {"findings": findings_data}
   588→
   589→    if not isinstance(findings_data, dict):
   590→        return None, ["findings file must contain a JSON array or object"]
   591→
   592→    if "findings" not in findings_data:
   593→        return None, ["findings object must contain a 'findings' key"]
   594→    normalized_findings_data, shape_errors = _normalize_import_payload_shape(
   595→        findings_data
   596→    )
   597→    if shape_errors:
   598→        return None, shape_errors
   599→    assert normalized_findings_data is not None
   600→
   601→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   602→        manual_override=manual_override,
   603→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   604→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   605→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   606→    )
   607→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
   608→        return None, [
   609→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
   610→        ]
   611→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
   612→        return None, [
   613→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   614→            "attested score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   615→        ]
   616→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
   617→        return None, [
   618→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   619→            "manual score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   620→        ]
   621→    findings_data, policy_errors = _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   622→        normalized_findings_data,
   623→        import_file=import_file,
   624→        attested_external=attested_external,
   625→        attested_attest=override_attest,
   626→        manual_override=override_enabled,
   627→        manual_attest=override_attest,
   628→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   629→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   630→    )
   631→    if policy_errors:
   632→        return None, policy_errors
   633→    assert findings_data is not None
   634→
   635→    missing_feedback, missing_low_score_findings = _validate_assessment_feedback(
   636→        findings_data
   637→    )
   638→    if missing_low_score_findings:
   639→        if override_enabled:
   640→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   641→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   642→            return findings_data, []
   643→        return None, [
   644→            f"assessments below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include at "
   645→            "least one finding for that same dimension with a concrete suggestion. "
   646→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_low_score_findings)}"
   647→        ]
   648→
   649→    if missing_feedback:
   650→        if override_enabled:
   651→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   652→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   653→            return findings_data, []
   654→        return None, [
   655→            f"assessments below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include explicit feedback "
   656→            "(finding with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, or "
   657→            "dimension_notes evidence for that dimension). "
   658→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_feedback)}"
   659→        ]
   660→
   661→    schema_errors = _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   662→        findings_data,
   663→        lang_name=lang_name,
   664→    )
   665→    if schema_errors and not allow_partial:
   666→        visible_errors = schema_errors[:10]
   667→        remaining = len(schema_errors) - len(visible_errors)
   668→        errors = [
   669→            "findings schema validation failed for holistic import. "
   670→            "Fix payload or rerun with --allow-partial to continue."
   671→        ]
   672→        errors.extend(visible_errors)
   673→        if remaining > 0:
   674→            errors.append(f"... {remaining} additional schema error(s) omitted")
   675→        return None, errors
   676→
   677→    return findings_data, []
   678→
   679→
   680→def load_import_findings_data(
   681→    import_file: str,
   682→    *,
   683→    colorize_fn=None,
   684→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   685→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   686→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   687→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   688→    attested_external: bool = False,
   689→    manual_override: bool = False,
   690→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   691→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   692→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   693→) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   694→    """Load and normalize review import payload to object format.
   695→
   696→    Raises ``ImportPayloadLoadError`` when validation fails.
   697→    """
   698→    data, errors = _parse_and_validate_import(
   699→        import_file,
   700→        lang_name=lang_name,
   701→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   702→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   703→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   704→        attested_external=attested_external,
   705→        manual_override=manual_override,
   706→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   707→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   708→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   709→    )
   710→    if errors:
   711→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(errors)
   712→    assert data is not None  # guaranteed when errors is empty
   713→    return data
   714→
   715→
   716→def print_import_load_errors(
   717→    errors: list[str],
   718→    *,
   719→    import_file: str,
   720→    colorize_fn,
   721→) -> None:
   722→    """Print import payload validation errors and actionable hints."""
   723→    for err in errors:
   724→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {err}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   725→    _print_import_error_hints(errors, import_file=import_file, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   726→
   727→
   728→def assessment_policy_from_payload(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> AssessmentImportPolicy:
   729→    """Return parsed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   730→    policy = payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY]
   731→    if isinstance(policy, dict):
   732→        return policy
   733→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   734→
   735→
   736→def assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   737→    payload: ReviewImportPayload,
   738→) -> AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
   739→    """Return typed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   740→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(assessment_policy_from_payload(payload))
   741→
   742→
   743→def assessment_mode_label(policy: AssessmentImportPolicy) -> str:
   744→    """Return a user-facing label for the selected assessment import mode."""
   745→    mode = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy).mode.strip().lower()
   746→    return _ASSESSMENT_MODE_LABELS.get(mode, f"unknown ({mode or 'none'})")
   747→
   748→
   749→def print_assessment_mode_banner(
   750→    policy: AssessmentImportPolicy,
   751→    *,
   752→    colorize_fn,
   753→) -> None:
   754→    """Print the selected assessment import mode to make policy explicit."""
   755→    policy_model = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy)
   756→    mode = policy_model.mode.strip().lower()
   757→    assessments_present = bool(policy_model.assessments_present)
   758→    if not assessments_present and mode == "none":
   759→        return
   760→    style = "yellow" if mode in {"manual_override", "findings_only"} else "dim"
   761→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Assessment import mode: {assessment_mode_label(policy)}", style))
   762→
   763→
   764→def print_assessment_policy_notice(
   765→    policy: AssessmentImportPolicy,
   766→    *,
   767→    import_file: str,
   768→    colorize_fn,
   769→) -> None:
   770→    """Render trust/override status for assessment-bearing imports."""
   771→    policy_model = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy)
   772→    if not policy_model.assessments_present:
   773→        return
   774→    mode = policy_model.mode.strip().lower()
   775→    reason = policy_model.reason.strip()
   776→
   777→    if mode == "trusted":
   778→        packet_path = policy_model.provenance.packet_path.strip() or None
   779→        detail = f" · blind packet {packet_path}" if packet_path else ""
   780→        print(
   781→            colorize_fn(
   782→                f"  Assessment provenance: trusted blind batch artifact{detail}.",
   783→                "dim",
   784→            )
   785→        )
   786→        return
   787→
   788→    if mode == "trusted_internal":
   789→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   790→        reason_text = policy_model.reason.strip()
   791→        suffix = f" ({reason_text})" if reason_text else ""
   792→        print(
   793→            colorize_fn(
   794→                f"  Assessment updates applied: {count} dimension(s){suffix}.",
   795→                "dim",
   796→            )
   797→        )
   798→        return
   799→
   800→    if mode == "manual_override":
   801→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   802→        print(
   803→            colorize_fn(
   804→                f"  WARNING: applying {count} assessment update(s) via manual override from untrusted provenance.",
   805→                "yellow",
   806→            )
   807→        )
   808→        if reason:
   809→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   810→        return
   811→
   812→    if mode == "attested_external":
   813→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   814→        print(
   815→            colorize_fn(
   816→                f"  Assessment updates applied via attested external blind review: {count} dimension(s).",
   817→                "dim",
   818→            )
   819→        )
   820→        if reason:
   821→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   822→        return
   823→
   824→    if mode == "findings_only":
   825→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   826→        print(
   827→            colorize_fn(
   828→                "  WARNING: untrusted assessment source detected. "
   829→                f"Imported findings only; skipped {count} assessment score update(s).",
   830→                "yellow",
   831→            )
   832→        )
   833→        if reason:
   834→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   835→        print(
   836→            colorize_fn(
   837→                "  Assessment scores in state were left unchanged.",
   838→                "dim",
   839→            )
   840→        )
   841→        print(
   842→            colorize_fn(
   843→                "  Happy path: use `desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   844→                "dim",
   845→            )
   846→        )
   847→        print(
   848→            colorize_fn(
   849→                "  If you intentionally want manual assessment import, rerun with "
   850→                f"`desloppify review --import {import_file} --manual-override --attest \"<why this is justified>\"`.",
   851→                "dim",
   852→            )
   853→        )
   854→        print(
   855→            colorize_fn(
   856→                "  Claude cloud path for durable scores: "
   857→                f"`desloppify review --import {import_file} --attested-external "
   858→                f"--attest \"{_ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_ATTEST_EXAMPLE}\"`",
   859→                "dim",
   860→            )
   861→        )

   864→def print_skipped_validation_details(diff: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   865→    """Print validation warnings for skipped imported findings."""
   866→    n_skipped = diff.get("skipped", 0)
   867→    if n_skipped <= 0:
   868→        return
   869→    print(
   870→        colorize_fn(
   871→            f"\n  \u26a0 {n_skipped} finding(s) skipped (validation errors):",
   872→            "yellow",
   873→        )
   874→    )
   875→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   876→        reasons = detail["missing"]
   877→        missing_fields = [r for r in reasons if not r.startswith("invalid ")]
   878→        validation_errors = [r for r in reasons if r.startswith("invalid ")]
   879→        parts = []
   880→        if missing_fields:
   881→            parts.append(f"missing {', '.join(missing_fields)}")
   882→        parts.extend(validation_errors)
   883→        print(
   884→            colorize_fn(
   885→                f"    #{detail['index']} ({detail['identifier']}): {'; '.join(parts)}",
   886→                "yellow",
   887→            )
   888→        )
   889→
   890→
   891→def print_assessments_summary(state: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   892→    """Print holistic subjective assessment summary when present."""
   893→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   894→    if not assessments:
   895→        return
   896→    parts = [
   897→        f"{key.replace('_', ' ')} {value['score']}"
   898→        for key, value in sorted(assessments.items())
   899→    ]
   900→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Assessments: {', '.join(parts)}", "bold"))
   901→
   902→
   903→def print_open_review_summary(state: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> str:
   904→    """Print current open review finding count and return next command."""
   905→    open_review = [
   906→        finding
   907→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   908→        if finding["status"] == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   909→    ]
   910→    if not open_review:
   911→        return "desloppify scan"
   912→    print(
   913→        colorize_fn(
   914→            f"\n  {len(open_review)} review issue{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open total "
   915→            f"({len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open total)",
   916→            "bold",
   917→        )
   918→    )
   919→    print(colorize_fn("  Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue", "dim"))
   920→    return "desloppify show review --status open"
   921→
   922→
   923→def print_review_import_scores_and_integrity(
   924→    state: dict[str, Any],
   925→    config: dict[str, Any],
   926→    *,
   927→    state_mod,
   928→    target_strict_score_from_config_fn,
   929→    subjective_at_target_fn,
   930→    subjective_rerun_command_fn,
   931→    colorize_fn,
   932→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   933→    """Print score snapshot plus subjective integrity warnings."""
   934→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   935→    if scores.overall is not None and scores.objective is not None and scores.strict is not None:
   936→        print(
   937→            colorize_fn(
   938→                f"\n  Current scores: overall {scores.overall:.1f}/100 · "
   939→                f"objective {scores.objective:.1f}/100 · strict {scores.strict:.1f}/100",
   940→                "dim",
   941→            )
   942→        )
   943→
   944→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config_fn(config, fallback=95.0)
   945→    at_target = subjective_at_target_fn(
   946→        state,
   947→        state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   948→        target=target_strict,
   949→    )
   950→    if not at_target:
   951→        return []
   952→
   953→    command = subjective_rerun_command_fn(at_target, max_items=5)
   954→    count = len(at_target)
   955→    if count >= 2:
   956→        print(
   957→            colorize_fn(
   958→                "  WARNING: "
   959→                f"{count} subjective scores match the target score. "
   960→                "On the next scan, those dimensions will be reset to 0.0 by the anti-gaming safeguard "
   961→                f"unless you rerun and re-import objective reviews first: {command}",
   962→                "red",
   963→            )
   964→        )
   965→    else:
   966→        print(
   967→            colorize_fn(
   968→                "  WARNING: "
   969→                f"{count} subjective score matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   970→                f"Can you rerun it by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   971→                "yellow",
   972→            )
   973→        )
   974→    return at_target
   975→
   976→

   978→    "ImportPayloadLoadError",

1→"""Helpers used by holistic review preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→
     9→HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW = [
    10→    "Read .desloppify/query.json for context, excerpts, and investigation batches",
    11→    "For each batch: start from listed seed files, then explore likely hotspots/unreviewed neighbors; evaluate the batch's dimensions (batches are independent — parallelize)",
    12→    "Cross-reference findings with the sibling_behavior and convention data",
    13→    "IMPORTANT: findings must be defects only — never positive observations. High scores capture quality; findings capture problems.",
    14→    "Write ALL findings to findings.json — do NOT fix code before importing. Import creates tracked state entries that let desloppify correlate fixes to findings.",
    15→    "Preferred local Codex path: desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import",
    16→    "Claude cloud durable path: run `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude`, follow the session template/instructions, then run the printed `--external-submit` command",
    17→    "Fallback path: `desloppify review --import findings.json` (findings only). Use manual override only for emergency/provisional imports.",
    18→    "AFTER importing: run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue, then fix each finding in code and `desloppify resolve fixed <id>`",
    19→]
    20→
    21→
    22→def append_full_sweep_batch(
    23→    *,
    24→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
    25→    dims: list[str],
    26→    all_files: list[str],
    27→    lang: Any,
    28→    max_files: int | None = None,
    29→) -> None:
    30→    """Append an optional cross-cutting full-codebase batch."""
    31→    if not dims:
    32→        return
    33→    all_rel_files: list[str] = []
    34→    for filepath in all_files:
    35→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
    36→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath)
    37→            if zone.value in ("test", "generated", "vendor"):
    38→                continue
    39→        all_rel_files.append(rel(filepath))
    40→        if isinstance(max_files, int) and max_files > 0 and len(all_rel_files) >= max_files:
    41→            break
    42→    if not all_rel_files:
    43→        return
    44→    batches.append(
    45→        {
    46→            "name": "Full Codebase Sweep",
    47→            "dimensions": list(dims),
    48→            "files_to_read": all_rel_files,
    49→            "why": "thorough default: evaluate cross-cutting quality across all production files",
    50→        }
    51→    )
    52→

1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from datetime import date
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import TIER_LABELS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    15→
    16→
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (
    24→        overall_score is not None
    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState) -> str:
   292→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
   293→    findings = state["findings"]
   294→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   295→
   296→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   297→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   298→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   299→    lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   300→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   301→
   302→    return "\n".join(lines)
   303→

1→"""Text templates for scan reporting output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from textwrap import dedent
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_workflow_guide(attest_example: str) -> str:
     9→    """Render the scan workflow guide with current attestation text."""
    10→    return dedent(
    11→        f"""
    12→        ## Workflow Guide
    13→
    14→        1. **Review findings first** (if any): `desloppify show review --status open` — high-value subjective findings
    15→        2. **Run auto-fixers** (if available): `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply
    16→        3. **Manual fixes**: `desloppify next` — highest-priority item. Fix it, then:
    17→           `desloppify resolve fixed "<id>" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    18→           Required attestation keywords: 'I have actually' and 'not gaming'.
    19→        4. **Rescan**: `desloppify scan --path <path>` — verify improvements, catch cascading effects
    20→        5. **Reset subjective baseline when needed**:
    21→           `desloppify scan --path <path> --reset-subjective` (then run a fresh review/import cycle)
    22→        6. **Check progress**: `desloppify status` — dimension scores dashboard
    23→
    24→        ### Decision Guide
    25→        - **Tackle**: T1/T2 (high impact), auto-fixable, security findings
    26→        - **Consider skipping**: T4 low-confidence, test/config zone findings (lower impact)
    27→        - **Wontfix**: Intentional patterns, false positives →
    28→          `desloppify resolve wontfix "<id>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    29→        - **Batch wontfix**: Multiple intentional patterns →
    30→          `desloppify resolve wontfix "<detector>::*::<category>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    31→
    32→        ### Understanding Dimensions
    33→        - **Mechanical** (File health, Code quality, etc.): Fix code → rescan
    34→        - **Subjective** (Naming quality, Logic clarity, etc.): Address review findings → re-review
    35→        - **Health vs Strict**: Health ignores wontfix; Strict penalizes it. Focus on Strict.
    36→        """
    37→    ).strip()
    38→
    39→
    40→__all__ = ["build_workflow_guide"]
    41→

1→"""Rendering and scoring helpers for finding work orders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.scoring import CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS, HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→
    14→def finding_weight(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, float, str]:
    15→    """Compute (weight, impact_pts, finding_id) for a finding."""
    16→    confidence = finding.get("confidence", "low")
    17→    is_holistic = finding.get("detail", {}).get("holistic", False)
    18→
    19→    weight = CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(confidence, 0.3)
    20→    if is_holistic:
    21→        weight *= HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
    22→
    23→    return weight, weight, finding.get("id", "")
    24→
    25→
    26→def _append_assessment_context(
    27→    lines: list[str], finding: dict, subjective_assessments: dict | None
    28→) -> None:
    29→    if not subjective_assessments:
    30→        return
    31→
    32→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    33→    dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    34→    assessment = subjective_assessments.get(dimension)
    35→    if not assessment:
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    display_dimension = dimension.replace("_", " ")
    39→    source = assessment.get("source", "review")
    40→    assessed_at = assessment.get("assessed_at", "")[:10]
    41→    lines.append(
    42→        f"**Dimension assessment**: {display_dimension} — {assessment['score']}/100 "
    43→        f"({source} review, {assessed_at})"
    44→    )
    45→    lines.append(
    46→        "Fixing this issue and re-reviewing should improve the "
    47→        f"{display_dimension} score.\n"
    48→    )
    49→
    50→
    51→def _append_evidence(lines: list[str], detail: dict) -> None:
    52→    evidence = detail.get("evidence", [])
    53→    if evidence:
    54→        lines.append("## Evidence\n")
    55→        for item in evidence:
    56→            lines.append(f"- {item}")
    57→        lines.append("")
    58→
    59→    evidence_lines = detail.get("evidence_lines", [])
    60→    if evidence_lines:
    61→        lines.append("## Code References\n")
    62→        for item in evidence_lines:
    63→            lines.append(f"- {item}")
    64→        lines.append("")
    65→
    66→
    67→def _append_files(
    68→    lines: list[str], finding: dict, detail: dict, is_holistic: bool
    69→) -> None:
    70→    if is_holistic:
    71→        related_files = detail.get("related_files", [])
    72→        if not related_files:
    73→            return
    74→        lines.append("## Files\n")
    75→        for related in related_files:
    76→            lines.append(f"- `{related}`")
    77→        lines.append("")
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    file_path = finding.get("file", "")
    81→    if file_path and file_path != ".":
    82→        lines.append("## Files\n")
    83→        lines.append(f"- `{file_path}`")
    84→        lines.append("")
    85→
    86→
    87→def _append_investigation(lines: list[str], finding_id: str, detail: dict) -> bool:
    88→    investigation = detail.get("investigation")
    89→    if not investigation:
    90→        return False
    91→
    92→    investigated_at = detail.get("investigated_at", "")
    93→    date_suffix = ""
    94→    if investigated_at:
    95→        try:
    96→            date_suffix = (
    97→                f" ({datetime.fromisoformat(investigated_at).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')})"
    98→            )
    99→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   100→            logger.debug(
   101→                "Invalid investigated_at value %r on %s: %s",
   102→                investigated_at,
   103→                finding_id,
   104→                exc,
   105→            )
   106→            date_suffix = " (invalid date)"
   107→
   108→    lines.append(f"## Investigation{date_suffix}\n")
   109→    lines.append(f"{investigation}\n")
   110→    return True
   111→
   112→
   113→def _append_footer(
   114→    lines: list[str],
   115→    *,
   116→    has_investigation: bool,
   117→    lang_name: str,
   118→    finding_id: str,
   119→    number: int | None,
   120→) -> None:
   121→    if has_investigation:
   122→        lines.append("## Ready to Fix\n")
   123→        lines.append("When done:\n")
   124→        lines.append("```bash")
   125→        lines.append(f'desloppify --lang {lang_name} resolve fixed "{finding_id}"')
   126→        lines.append("```\n")
   127→        return
   128→
   129→    lines.append("## Status: Needs Investigation\n")
   130→    lines.append("Investigate the files above, then resolve with a note:\n")
   131→    lines.append("```bash")
   132→    lines.append(
   133→        f'desloppify --lang {lang_name} resolve fixed "{finding_id}" --note "description of fix"'
   134→    )
   135→    lines.append("```\n")
   136→    lines.append("Or save detailed analysis first:\n")
   137→    lines.append("```bash")
   138→    lines.append(
   139→        f'desloppify --lang {lang_name} show "{finding_id}" --notes analysis.md'
   140→    )
   141→    lines.append("```\n")
   142→
   143→
   144→def render_issue_detail(
   145→    finding: dict,
   146→    lang_name: str,
   147→    number: int | None = None,
   148→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   149→) -> str:
   150→    """Render one finding as a markdown work order from state."""
   151→    finding_id = finding["id"]
   152→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   153→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   154→    is_holistic = detail.get("holistic", False)
   155→    is_review = detector in ("review", "concerns")
   156→
   157→    # Derive dimension: from detail for review, from registry for mechanical.
   158→    raw_dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   159→    if raw_dimension:
   160→        dimension = raw_dimension.replace("_", " ")
   161→    else:
   162→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   163→        dimension = meta.dimension.replace("_", " ") if meta else "unknown"
   164→
   165→    confidence = finding.get("confidence", "low")
   166→
   167→    parts = finding_id.split("::")
   168→    identifier = parts[-2] if len(parts) >= 3 else finding.get("file", "unknown")
   169→    weight, impact_pts, _ = finding_weight(finding)
   170→    label = "+++" if weight >= 8 else "++" if weight >= 5 else "+"
   171→
   172→    lines: list[str] = []
   173→    lines.append(f"# {dimension}: {identifier}\n")
   174→    lines.append(f"**Finding**: `{finding_id}`  ")
   175→    if not is_review:
   176→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   177→        detector_display = meta.display if meta else detector
   178→        lines.append(f"**Detector**: {detector_display} | **Confidence**: {confidence}  ")
   179→    else:
   180→        lines.append(f"**Dimension**: {dimension} | **Confidence**: {confidence}  ")
   181→    lines.append(f"**Score impact**: {label} (~{impact_pts:.1f} pts)\n")
   182→
   183→    _append_assessment_context(lines, finding, subjective_assessments)
   184→
   185→    lines.append("## Problem\n")
   186→    lines.append(f"{finding.get('summary', '')}\n")
   187→
   188→    _append_evidence(lines, detail)
   189→
   190→    suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   191→    if suggestion:
   192→        lines.append("## Suggested Fix\n")
   193→        lines.append(f"{suggestion}\n")
   194→
   195→    _append_files(lines, finding, detail, is_holistic)
   196→
   197→    reasoning = detail.get("reasoning", "")
   198→    if reasoning:
   199→        lines.append("## Why This Matters\n")
   200→        lines.append(f"{reasoning}\n")
   201→
   202→    has_investigation = _append_investigation(lines, finding_id, detail)
   203→    _append_footer(
   204→        lines,
   205→        has_investigation=has_investigation,
   206→        lang_name=lang_name,
   207→        finding_id=finding_id,
   208→        number=number,
   209→    )
   210→    return "\n".join(lines)
   211→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        else:
   182→            key = "items"
   183→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   184→    return grouped
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = [
   188→    "build_finding_items",
   189→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   190→    "item_explain",
   191→    "item_sort_key",
   192→    "tier_counts",
   193→    "subjective_score_value",
   194→    "group_queue_items",
   195→]
   196→
