```
1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
   151→                    f"Use --top {top + 20} to see more.\n"
   152→                ),
   153→                "dim",
   154→            )
   155→        )
   156→
   157→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   158→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   159→    for finding in matches:
   160→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   161→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   164→    print(
   165→        colorize(
   166→            (
   167→                "    By tier:     "
   168→                + ", ".join(
   169→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   170→                )
   171→            ),
   172→            "dim",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    print(
   176→        colorize(
   177→            (
   178→                "    By detector: "
   179→                + ", ".join(
   180→                    f"{detector}:{count}"
   181→                    for detector, count in sorted(
   182→                        by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]
   183→                    )
   184→                )
   185→            ),
   186→            "dim",
   187→        )
   188→    )
   189→    if hidden_total:
   190→        print(
   191→            colorize(
   192→                (
   193→                    "    Hidden:      "
   194→                    + ", ".join(
   195→                        f"{detector}:+{count}"
   196→                        for detector, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   197→                    )
   198→                ),
   199→                "dim",
   200→            )
   201→        )
   202→    print()
   203→
   204→
   205→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict, matches: list[dict]) -> None:
   206→    """Render a compact plan from current findings and narrative actions."""
   207→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   208→    if not actions and not matches:
   209→        return
   210→
   211→    print(
   212→        colorize(
   213→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   214→            "yellow",
   215→        )
   216→    )
   217→    if actions:
   218→        top = actions[0]
   219→        print(
   220→            colorize(
   221→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   222→                "cyan",
   223→            )
   224→        )
   225→    elif matches:
   226→        first = matches[0]
   227→        print(
   228→            colorize(

   230→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   231→                "cyan",
   232→            )
   233→        )
   234→
   235→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   236→        print()
   237→
   238→
   239→def show_subjective_followup(state: dict, target_strict_score: float) -> None:
   240→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   241→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   242→    if not dim_scores:
   243→        return
   244→
   245→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   246→        state,
   247→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   248→    )
   249→    if not subjective:
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   253→        state,
   254→        subjective,
   255→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   256→        max_quality_items=3,
   257→        max_integrity_items=5,
   258→    )
   259→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   260→        print()
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "group_matches_by_file",
   265→    "render_findings",
   266→    "show_agent_plan",
   267→    "show_subjective_followup",
   268→    "write_show_output_file",
   269→]
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(followup: SubjectiveFollowup, *, leading_newline: bool = False) -> bool:
    21→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    22→
    23→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    24→    """
    25→    printed = False
    26→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    27→    if followup.low_assessed:
    28→        print(
    29→            colorize(
    30→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    31→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    32→                "cyan",
    33→            )
    34→        )
    35→        print(
    36→            colorize(
    37→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    38→                "dim",
    39→            )
    40→        )
    41→        printed = True
    42→
    43→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    44→        if printed:
    45→            print()
    46→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    47→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    48→        printed = True
    49→    return printed
    50→
    51→
    52→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    53→
```

```
1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps."""
    18→    if not steps:
    19→        return
    20→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    21→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    22→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    23→    if next_command:
    24→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    25→
    26→
    27→def print_replacement_groups(
    28→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    29→    *,
    30→    title: str,
    31→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    34→    if not groups:
    35→        return
    36→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    37→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    38→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    39→        for old, new in replacements:
    40→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    41→    print()
    42→
    43→
    44→def print_ranked_actions(actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3) -> bool:
    45→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    46→    ranked = sorted(
    47→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    48→        key=lambda action: (
    49→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    50→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    51→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    52→        ),
    53→    )
    54→    if not ranked:
    55→        return False
    56→    print(colorize("  Biggest things impacting score:", "cyan"))
    57→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    58→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    59→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    60→        command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    61→        print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    62→    return True
    63→
    64→
    65→__all__ = [
    66→    "print_agent_plan",
    67→    "print_ranked_actions",
    68→    "print_replacement_groups",
    69→]
    70→
```

```
1→"""Subjective scan reporting: common helpers, integrity checks, and output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import coerce_target_score
    10→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class SubjectiveFollowup:
    19→    threshold: float
    20→    threshold_label: str
    21→    low_assessed: list[dict]
    22→    rendered: str
    23→    command: str
    24→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None
    25→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    26→
    27→
    28→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    29→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    30→    seen: set[str] = set()
    31→    ordered: list[str] = []
    32→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    33→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    34→            if key in seen:
    35→                continue
    36→            ordered.append(key)
    37→            seen.add(key)
    38→    return ",".join(ordered)
    39→
    40→
    41→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    42→    return ", ".join(
    43→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    44→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    49→    count = len(entries)
    50→    names = ", ".join(
    51→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    52→    )
    53→    if count > max_names:
    54→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    55→    return names
    56→
    57→
    58→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    59→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    60→        return 0
    61→    if isinstance(value, int):
    62→        return value
    63→    if isinstance(value, float):
    64→        return int(value)
    65→    if isinstance(value, str):
    66→        try:
    67→            return int(value.strip())
    68→        except ValueError:
    69→            return 0
    70→    return 0
    71→
    72→
    73→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    74→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    75→        return []
    76→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    77→
    78→
    79→def subtract_reason_counts(
    80→    total_counts: dict[str, int],
    81→    scoped_counts: dict[str, int],
    82→) -> dict[str, int]:
    83→    """Subtract scoped reason counters from global counters."""
    84→    remainder: dict[str, int] = {}
    85→    for reason, total in total_counts.items():
    86→        delta = int(total) - int(scoped_counts.get(reason, 0))
    87→        if delta > 0:
    88→            remainder[reason] = delta
    89→    return remainder
    90→
    91→
    92→def subjective_rerun_command(
    93→    items: list[dict],
    94→    *,
    95→    max_items: int = 5,
    96→    refresh: bool = True,
    97→) -> str:
    98→    _ = refresh
    99→    dim_keys = flatten_cli_keys(items, max_items=max_items)
   100→    command_parts = [
   101→        "desloppify",
   102→        "review",
   103→        "--run-batches",
   104→        "--runner",
   105→        "codex",
   106→        "--parallel",
   107→        "--scan-after-import",
   108→    ]
   109→    if dim_keys:
   110→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   111→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   120→    return scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   121→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   126→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   127→) -> list[dict]:
   128→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   129→    for entry in entries:
   130→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   131→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   132→
   133→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   134→    for key in dimension_keys:
   135→        if key in by_key:
   136→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   137→            continue
   138→        mapped.append(
   139→            {
   140→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   141→                "score": 0.0,
   142→                "strict": 0.0,
   143→                "issues": 0,
   144→                "placeholder": False,
   145→                "cli_keys": [key],
   146→            }
   147→        )
   148→    return mapped
   149→
   150→
   151→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   154→    *,
   155→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   156→    max_items: int = 5,
   157→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   158→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   159→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   160→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   161→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   162→    )
   163→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   164→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   165→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   166→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   167→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   168→
   169→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   170→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   171→            reset_keys, subjective_entries
   172→        )
   173→        return {
   174→            "status": "penalized",
   175→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   176→            "target": target_display,
   177→            "entries": reset_entries,
   178→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   179→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   180→        }
   181→
   182→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   183→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   184→            matched_keys, subjective_entries
   185→        )
   186→        return {
   187→            "status": "warn",
   188→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   189→            "target": target_display,
   190→            "entries": matched_entries,
   191→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   192→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   193→        }
   194→
   195→    at_target = sorted(
   196→        [
   197→            entry
   198→            for entry in subjective_entries
   199→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   200→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   201→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   202→                threshold_value,
   203→            )
   204→        ],
   205→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   206→    )
   207→    if not at_target:
   208→        return None
   209→
   210→    return {
   211→        "status": "at_target",
   212→        "count": len(at_target),
   213→        "target": threshold_value,
   214→        "entries": at_target,
   215→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   216→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   217→    }
   218→
   219→
   220→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   221→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   222→    *,
   223→    fallback_target: float = 95.0,
   224→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   225→    if not integrity_notice:
   226→        return []
   227→
   228→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   229→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   230→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   231→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   232→        fallback=fallback_target,
   233→    )
   234→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   235→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   236→
   237→    if status == "penalized":
   238→        return [
   239→            (
   240→                "red",
   241→                "WARNING: "
   242→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   243→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   244→            ),
   245→            (
   246→                "yellow",
   247→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   248→            ),
   249→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   250→        ]
   251→
   252→    if status == "warn":
   253→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   254→        return [
   255→            (
   256→                "yellow",
   257→                "WARNING: "
   258→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   259→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   260→            ),
   261→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   262→        ]
   263→
   264→    if status == "at_target":
   265→        return [
   266→            (
   267→                "yellow",
   268→                "WARNING: "
   269→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   270→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   271→            ),
   272→        ]
   273→
   274→    return []
   275→
   276→
   277→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   278→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   279→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   280→
   281→
   282→def _subjective_reset_command(state: dict) -> str:
   283→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path")
   284→    if not isinstance(scan_path, str) or not scan_path.strip():
   285→        scan_path = "."
   286→    return f"`desloppify scan --path {scan_path} --reset-subjective`"
   287→
   288→
   289→def build_subjective_followup(
   290→    state: dict,
   291→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   292→    *,
   293→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   294→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   295→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   296→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   297→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   298→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   299→    low_assessed = sorted(
   300→        [
   301→            entry
   302→            for entry in subjective_entries
   303→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   304→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   305→        ],
   306→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   307→    )
   308→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   309→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   310→        low_assessed,
   311→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   312→    )
   313→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   314→        state,
   315→        subjective_entries,
   316→        threshold=threshold_value,
   317→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   318→    )
   319→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   320→        integrity_notice,
   321→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   322→    )
   323→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   324→        threshold=threshold_value,
   325→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   326→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   327→        rendered=rendered,
   328→        command=command,
   329→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   330→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→
   334→def show_subjective_paths(
   335→    state: dict,
   336→    dim_scores: dict,
   337→    *,
   338→    colorize_fn,
   339→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   340→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   341→    target_strict_score: float | None = None,
   342→) -> None:
   343→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   344→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   345→    if not subjective_entries:
   346→        return
   347→
   348→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   349→        state,
   350→        subjective_entries,
   351→        threshold=threshold_value,
   352→        max_quality_items=3,
   353→        max_integrity_items=5,
   354→    )
   355→    unassessed = sorted(
   356→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   357→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   358→    )
   359→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   360→
   361→    all_findings = state.get("findings", {})
   362→    if not isinstance(all_findings, dict):
   363→        all_findings = {}
   364→    scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(all_findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   365→    coverage_in_scope, reason_counts_in_scope, holistic_reason_counts_in_scope = (
   366→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped)
   367→    )
   368→    coverage_global, reason_counts_global, holistic_reason_counts_global = (
   369→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_findings)
   370→    )
   371→    if not all_findings and coverage_global == 0 and coverage_in_scope > 0:
   372→        # Test/mocked states may supply only scoped findings.
   373→        coverage_global = coverage_in_scope
   374→        reason_counts_global = dict(reason_counts_in_scope)
   375→        holistic_reason_counts_global = dict(holistic_reason_counts_in_scope)
   376→    coverage_out_of_scope = max(coverage_global - coverage_in_scope, 0)
   377→    reason_counts_out_of_scope = subtract_reason_counts(
   378→        reason_counts_global,
   379→        reason_counts_in_scope,
   380→    )
   381→    holistic_in_scope = sum(holistic_reason_counts_in_scope.values())
   382→    holistic_global = sum(holistic_reason_counts_global.values())
   383→    holistic_out_of_scope = max(holistic_global - holistic_in_scope, 0)
   384→    if (
   385→        not unassessed
   386→        and not low_assessed
   387→        and coverage_global <= 0
   388→        and not followup.integrity_notice
   389→    ):
   390→        return
   391→
   392→    print(colorize_fn("  Subjective path:", "cyan"))
   393→    print(
   394→        colorize_fn(
   395→            f"    Reset baseline from zero: {_subjective_reset_command(state)}",
   396→            "dim",
   397→        )
   398→    )
   399→    if target_strict_score is not None:
   400→        strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   401→        if strict_score is not None:
   402→            gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   403→            if gap > 0:
   404→                print(
   405→                    colorize_fn(
   406→                        f"    North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   407→                        "yellow",
   408→                    )
   409→                )
   410→            else:
   411→                print(
   412→                    colorize_fn(
   413→                        f"    North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   414→                        "green",
   415→                    )
   416→                )
   417→
   418→    if unassessed or holistic_global > 0:
   419→        integrity_bits: list[str] = []
   420→        if unassessed:
   421→            integrity_bits.append("unassessed subjective dimensions")
   422→        if holistic_global > 0:
   423→            integrity_bits.append("holistic review stale/missing")
   424→        integrity_label = " + ".join(integrity_bits)
   425→        print(colorize_fn(f"    High-priority integrity gap: {integrity_label}", "yellow"))
   426→        print(
   427→            colorize_fn(
   428→                "    Refresh baseline (Codex local): `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`",
   429→                "dim",
   430→            )
   431→        )
   432→        print(
   433→            colorize_fn(
   434→                "    Claude cloud durable path: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude`, then run the printed `--external-submit ... --scan-after-import` command",
   435→                "dim",
   436→            )
   437→        )
   438→        print(
   439→            colorize_fn(
   440→                "    Findings-only fallback: `desloppify review --prepare`, then `desloppify review --import findings.json && desloppify scan`",
   441→                "dim",
   442→            )
   443→        )
   444→
   445→    if low_assessed:
   446→        print(
   447→            colorize_fn(
   448→                f"    Quality below target (<{followup.threshold_label}%): {followup.rendered}",
   449→                "yellow",
   450→            )
   451→        )
   452→        print(
   453→            colorize_fn(
   454→                f"    Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
   455→                "dim",
   456→            )
   457→        )
   458→
   459→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   460→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {message}", style))
   461→
   462→    if unassessed:
   463→        rendered = ", ".join(entry["name"] for entry in unassessed[:3])
   464→        if len(unassessed) > 3:
   465→            rendered = f"{rendered}, +{len(unassessed) - 3} more"
   466→        print(colorize_fn(f"    Unassessed (0% placeholder): {rendered}", "yellow"))
   467→        print(
   468→            colorize_fn(
   469→                "    Start with holistic refresh, then tune specific dimensions.", "dim"
   470→            )
   471→        )
   472→
   473→    if coverage_global > 0:
   474→        detail = []
   475→        if reason_counts_in_scope.get("changed", 0) > 0:
   476→            detail.append(f"{reason_counts_in_scope['changed']} changed")
   477→        if reason_counts_in_scope.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:
   478→            detail.append(f"{reason_counts_in_scope['unreviewed']} unreviewed")
   479→        reason_text = ", ".join(detail) if detail else "stale/unreviewed"
   480→        suffix = "file" if coverage_in_scope == 1 else "files"
   481→        print(
   482→            colorize_fn(
   483→                f"    Coverage debt: {coverage_in_scope} {suffix} need review ({reason_text})",
   484→                "yellow",
   485→            )
   486→        )
   487→        print(
   488→            colorize_fn(
   489→                "    Scope split: "
   490→                f"open (in-scope) {coverage_in_scope} · "
   491→                f"open (out-of-scope carried) {coverage_out_of_scope} · "
   492→                f"open (global) {coverage_global}",
   493→                "yellow",
   494→            )
   495→        )
   496→        if reason_counts_out_of_scope:
   497→            out_detail: list[str] = []
   498→            if reason_counts_out_of_scope.get("changed", 0) > 0:
   499→                out_detail.append(f"{reason_counts_out_of_scope['changed']} changed")
   500→            if reason_counts_out_of_scope.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:
   501→                out_detail.append(
   502→                    f"{reason_counts_out_of_scope['unreviewed']} unreviewed"
   503→                )
   504→            if not out_detail:
   505→                out_detail = [
   506→                    f"{count} {reason}"
   507→                    for reason, count in sorted(reason_counts_out_of_scope.items())
   508→                ]
   509→            print(
   510→                colorize_fn(
   511→                    "    Out-of-scope carried reasons: "
   512→                    + ", ".join(out_detail),
   513→                    "yellow",
   514→                )
   515→            )
   516→        if holistic_global > 0:
   517→            print(
   518→                colorize_fn(
   519→                    f"    Includes {holistic_global} holistic stale/missing signal(s)"
   520→                    f" ({holistic_in_scope} in-scope, {holistic_out_of_scope} out-of-scope carried).",
   521→                    "yellow",
   522→                )
   523→            )
   524→        print(
   525→            colorize_fn(
   526→                "    Triage: `desloppify show subjective_review --status open`", "dim"
   527→            )
   528→        )
   529→
   530→    print()
   531→
   532→
   533→__all__ = [
   534→    "SubjectiveFollowup",
   535→    "build_subjective_followup",
   536→    "coerce_notice_count",
   537→    "coerce_str_keys",
   538→    "flatten_cli_keys",
   539→    "render_subjective_names",
   540→    "render_subjective_scores",
```

---

### 3. HOW REVIEW/SUBJECTIVE FINDINGS ARE DESCRIBED VS MECHANICAL FINDINGS

#### Terminology split

The code maintains a clear distinction:

1. **Mechanical findings** -- produced by detectors (unused, smells, complexity, etc.). These are objective, reproducible, and have auto-fixers. Shown in T1-T3 tiers.

2. **Review findings** (`detector == "review"`) -- produced by human/LLM review import. These are per-finding issues attached to a specific dimension and file. They have an "investigation" workflow where users can attach notes. They are weighted by confidence and a holistic multiplier.

3. **Subjective findings** (`detector == "subjective_review"` or `"subjective_assessment"`) -- synthetic coverage-debt signals. These are always tier 4. They represent files that need review or holistic signals that are stale.

4. **Subjective dimensions** (`kind == "subjective_dimension"`) -- synthetic work queue items created by `build_subjective_items()` for dimensions scoring below target. Always tier 4.

#### How ranking treats them (ranking.py)

The `item_sort_key` function (line 83-101) makes the ranking policy explicit:
- Subjective items and subjective dimensions always sort as T4, with a secondary sort key of `1` (vs `0` for mechanical), meaning **within T4, subjective items sort after mechanical items**.
- Within subjective items, they sort by ascending strict score (worst scores first).
- Mechanical items sort by: tier ascending, confidence ascending (high before low), review_weight descending (for review findings), count descending, then id.

The `item_explain` function confirms this policy in user-facing text:
- `"Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."` (line 112-114)
- `"Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."` (line 148-150)

#### Status rendering distinction (status_parts/render.py)

The status dimension table has a clear visual separator (line 243-247):
```
── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────
```
Subjective dimensions show `[stale]`, `[unassessed]`, and `[open issues: N]` tags. They always display tier T4 and action type "review".

The `show_review_summary()` function (lines 544-569) separately calls out review findings:
```
Review: N findings open, M uninvestigated -- `desloppify show review --status open`
```

---

### 4. IS THE GUIDANCE COHERENT?

#### Strengths

1. **Consistent command vocabulary.** The `issues` command is fully removed from live source code. All references now use `desloppify show review --status open` for review findings.

2. **Clear tiering policy.** Multiple places explain that subjective items are always T4 and never outrank mechanical T1-T3. The ranking code enforces this.

3. **Three-path review workflow is consistently described.** Scan reporting, status, next, and review prep all reference the same three paths: Codex local, Claude cloud, and findings-only fallback.

4. **`primary_command_for_finding()` is the single source** for what command to suggest for each finding type. All work queue items flow through this.

5. **The `HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW` constant** in `_prepare/helpers.py` has been updated to say `desloppify show review --status open` instead of `desloppify issues`.

#### Issues found

1. **Three stale `desloppify issues` references** in test snapshots and one test assertion (listed in section 1 above). These are tests/snapshots only, but they will produce confusing guidance if a user reads the snapshot JSON or if the test breaks in the wrong way.

2. **`issue_queue` action type vestige.** The action_models.py and action_engine.py still reference an `issue_queue` action type (priority 0, highest priority). This type is never actually produced by the current action computation code, but it exists in the priority ordering. This is dead code but not harmful.

3. **The `action_engine.py` subjective_review entry** suggests `desloppify fix review` (line 184), which is a different command from `desloppify review --prepare`. The `fix review` subcommand appears to be a separate entrypoint. This could be confusing since most other subjective guidance points to `desloppify review --prepare`.

4. **Inconsistent subjective command surface area.** Users are told about subjective findings through at least four different command paths depending on context:
   - `desloppify review --prepare` (for initial/holistic review)
   - `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` (for automated review)
   - `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` (for Claude cloud)
   - `desloppify show subjective_review --status open` (for triaging coverage debt)
   - `desloppify show review --status open` (for investigating review findings)
   
   These are all legitimate distinct operations, but a user encountering them for the first time gets a lot of commands without a clear decision tree. The workflow guide in `scan_reporting_text.py` is the closest thing to a decision tree, but it only mentions step 1 as "Review findings first" without distinguishing the paths.

5. **The `next` command followup nudges are very verbose.** The `render_followup_nudges` function (lines 258-384 in next_parts/render.py) can print a large block of text covering: north star gap, subjective quality, subjective integrity gap, unassessed dimensions, coverage debt, holistic signals, open review findings, and more. A user running `desloppify next` to get their single next task could see 15+ lines of followup guidance.

6. **Missing explicit guidance on how to discover subjective findings.** When a user has only mechanical findings and no review has been run, the guidance says "Run a subjective design review to catch issues linters miss: desloppify review --prepare" (reminders.py line 291-293). But the threshold is `strict_score >= 80`, meaning users with lower scores never see this nudge. There is no early prompt to discover the subjective dimension system.

7. **The `issues_render.py` work orders use `--lang` flag explicitly** (lines 126, 133, 139), e.g. `desloppify --lang python resolve fixed "{id}"`. Most other guidance omits `--lang`. This inconsistency means a user following a work order might use `--lang`, while following narrative guidance they would not.

8. **The plan render (planning/render.py)** shows subjective items with `[subjective]` prefix (line 232) and their `primary_command`, which is clear. However, the dimension table shows subjective measures with action type "review" (line 165) but does not explain what "review" means as an action type. The status render legend (line 289) says `Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix` -- but "review" as an action type is separate from these and is not listed in the legend.

---

### 5. SUMMARY OF FINDINGS

**Critical (stale/broken references):**
- 3 files reference `desloppify issues` (all in tests/snapshots -- not live code)

**Moderate (potential user confusion):**
- `desloppify fix review` vs `desloppify review --prepare` inconsistency in action_engine.py
- "review" action type not included in the dimension table legend
- `--lang` flag included in issues_render.py work orders but absent from all other guidance
- `issue_queue` dead action type still in priority ordering

**Minor (verbosity/discoverability):**
- The `next` command followup nudges can be extremely verbose
- Subjective review is only nudged at strict_score >= 80
- No consolidated decision tree for the 5+ subjective-related command paths

```
1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
    14→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
    15→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    16→    pattern = args.pattern
    17→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
    18→    if chronic:
    19→        scope = pattern
    20→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
    21→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
    22→    if not pattern:
    23→        print(
    24→            colorize(
    25→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
    26→                "yellow",
    27→            )
    28→        )
    29→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
    30→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
    31→
    32→
    33→def load_matches(
    34→    state: dict,
    35→    *,
    36→    scope: str | None,
    37→    status_filter: str,
    38→    chronic: bool,
    39→) -> list[dict]:
    40→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
    41→    queue = build_work_queue(
    42→        state,
    43→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    44→            count=None,
    45→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    46→            scope=scope,
    47→            status=status_filter,
    48→            include_subjective=False,
    49→            chronic=chronic,
    50→            no_tier_fallback=True,
    51→        ),
    52→    )
    53→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    54→
    55→
    56→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
    57→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
    58→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
    59→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
    60→    )
    61→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
    62→        matches,
    63→        budget=noise_budget,
    64→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
    65→    )
    66→    return (
    67→        surfaced_matches,
    68→        hidden_by_detector,
    69→        noise_budget,
    70→        global_noise_budget,
    71→        budget_warning,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = ["load_matches", "resolve_noise", "resolve_show_scope"]
    76→
```

```
1→"""show command: dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import json
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan

    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path

    31→from .scope import load_matches, resolve_noise, resolve_show_scope
    32→
    33→
    34→def _lookup_from_last_query(number: int) -> str | None:
    35→    """Look up finding ID by 1-based index from the last query output."""
    36→    try:
    37→        qf = query_file_path()
    38→        if not qf.exists():
    39→            return None
    40→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
    41→        items = data.get("items") or data.get("findings") or []
    42→        if not items:
    43→            return None
    44→        if number < 1 or number > len(items):
    45→            return None
    46→        item = items[number - 1]
    47→        return item.get("id") or item.get("finding_id") or None
    48→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, KeyError, TypeError):
    49→        return None
    50→
    51→
    52→def _show_single_finding(
    53→    state: dict,
    54→    runtime,
    55→    finding_id: str,
    56→    lang_name: str | None,
    57→    notes_file: str | None,
    58→) -> None:
    59→    """Render detailed work order for a single finding, optionally attaching notes."""
    60→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    61→    if not finding:
    62→        print(
    63→            colorize(f"\n  Finding not found: {finding_id}\n", "red"),
    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    effective_lang = lang_name or finding.get("lang", "unknown")
    69→
    70→    if notes_file:
    71→        notes_path = Path(notes_file)
    72→        if not notes_path.exists():
    73→            print(
    74→                colorize(f"\n  File not found: {notes_file}\n", "red"),
    75→                file=sys.stderr,
    76→            )
    77→            return
    78→        try:
    79→            text = notes_path.read_text()
    80→        except OSError as e:
    81→            print(
    82→                colorize(f"\n  Could not read file: {e}\n", "red"),
    83→                file=sys.stderr,
    84→            )
    85→            return
    86→        ok = update_investigation(state, finding_id, text)
    87→        if not ok:
    88→            print(
    89→                colorize(f"\n  Could not update finding: {finding_id}\n", "red"),
    90→                file=sys.stderr,
    91→            )
    92→            return
    93→        save_state(state, runtime.state_path)
    94→        print(colorize(f"\n  Investigation saved for {finding_id}.", "green"))
    95→
    96→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    97→    doc = render_issue_detail(
    98→        finding, effective_lang, subjective_assessments=assessments
    99→    )
   100→    print()
   101→    print(doc)
   102→
   103→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   104→        state, context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show")
   105→    )
   106→    write_query(
   107→        {
   108→            "command": "show",
   109→            "action": "detail",
   110→            "finding": {
   111→                "id": finding_id,
   112→                "summary": finding.get("summary", ""),
   113→                "detail": finding.get("detail", {}),
   114→            },
   115→            "next_command": f'desloppify resolve fixed "{finding_id}" --note "<what you fixed>"',
   116→            "narrative": narrative,
   117→        }
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
   122→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
   123→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   124→
   125→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   126→    if not concerns:
   127→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
   131→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
   132→
   133→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
   134→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
   135→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
   136→        for ev in c.evidence:
   137→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
   138→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
   139→
   140→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
   141→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
   142→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
   143→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
   144→            if reasoning:
   145→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
   146→        print()
   147→
   148→
   149→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   150→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   151→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   152→    state = runtime.state
   153→    config = runtime.config
   154→
   155→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   159→    if stale_warning:
   160→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   161→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   162→    if skill_warning:
   163→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   164→
   165→    # Handle "show concerns" as a special view.
   166→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   167→    notes_file = getattr(args, "notes", None)
   168→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   169→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
   170→        _show_concerns(state, lang.name if lang else None)
   171→        return
   172→
   173→    # Numeric shorthand: look up from last query output.
   174→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().isdigit():
   175→        looked_up = _lookup_from_last_query(int(pattern_raw.strip()))
   176→        if looked_up:
   177→            pattern_raw = looked_up
   178→            # Rewrite args.pattern so downstream sees the resolved ID.
   179→            args.pattern = pattern_raw
   180→
   181→    # Single-finding detail mode.
   182→    if pattern_raw and "::" in pattern_raw:
   183→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
   184→        _show_single_finding(
   185→            state,
   186→            runtime,
   187→            pattern_raw,
   188→            lang.name if lang else None,
   189→            notes_file,
   190→        )
   191→        return
   192→
   193→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   194→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   195→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   196→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   197→        return
   198→
   199→    matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
   200→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   201→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   202→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   203→        state,
   204→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    if not matches:
   208→        print(colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow"))
   209→        write_query(
   210→            {
   211→                "command": "show",
   212→                "query": pattern,
   213→                "status_filter": status_filter,
   214→                "total": 0,
   215→                "findings": [],
   216→                "narrative": narrative,
   217→            }
   218→        )
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    (
   222→        surfaced_matches,
   223→        hidden_by_detector,
   224→        noise_budget,
   225→        global_noise_budget,
   226→        budget_warning,
   227→    ) = resolve_noise(
   228→        config,
   229→        matches,
   230→    )
   231→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   232→
   233→    payload = build_show_payload(
   234→        surfaced_matches,
   235→        pattern,
   236→        status_filter,
   237→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   238→            total_matches=len(matches),
   239→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   240→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   241→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   245→
   246→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   247→    if output_file:
   248→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   249→            return
   250→        raise SystemExit(1)
   251→
   252→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   253→    render_findings(
   254→        surfaced_matches,
   255→        pattern=pattern,
   256→        status_filter=status_filter,
   257→        show_code=show_code,
   258→        top=top,
   259→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   260→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   261→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   262→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   263→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   264→    )
   265→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches)
   266→    show_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→
   272→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   273→
```

```
1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
   151→                    f"Use --top {top + 20} to see more.\n"
   152→                ),
   153→                "dim",
   154→            )
   155→        )
   156→
   157→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   158→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   159→    for finding in matches:
   160→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   161→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   164→    print(
   165→        colorize(
   166→            (
   167→                "    By tier:     "
   168→                + ", ".join(
   169→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   170→                )
   171→            ),
   172→            "dim",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    print(
   176→        colorize(
   177→            (
   178→                "    By detector: "
   179→                + ", ".join(
   180→                    f"{detector}:{count}"
   181→                    for detector, count in sorted(
   182→                        by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]
   183→                    )
   184→                )
   185→            ),
   186→            "dim",
   187→        )
   188→    )
   189→    if hidden_total:
   190→        print(
   191→            colorize(
   192→                (
   193→                    "    Hidden:      "
   194→                    + ", ".join(
   195→                        f"{detector}:+{count}"
   196→                        for detector, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   197→                    )
   198→                ),
   199→                "dim",
   200→            )
   201→        )
   202→    print()
   203→
   204→
   205→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict, matches: list[dict]) -> None:
   206→    """Render a compact plan from current findings and narrative actions."""
   207→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   208→    if not actions and not matches:
   209→        return
   210→
   211→    print(
   212→        colorize(
   213→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   214→            "yellow",
   215→        )
   216→    )
   217→    if actions:
   218→        top = actions[0]
   219→        print(
   220→            colorize(
   221→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   222→                "cyan",
   223→            )
   224→        )
   225→    elif matches:
   226→        first = matches[0]
   227→        print(
   228→            colorize(
   229→                "  Agent focus: `desloppify next --count 20` — "
   230→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   231→                "cyan",
   232→            )
   233→        )
   234→
   235→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   236→        print()
   237→
   238→
   239→def show_subjective_followup(state: dict, target_strict_score: float) -> None:
   240→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   241→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   242→    if not dim_scores:
   243→        return
   244→
   245→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   246→        state,
   247→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   248→    )
   249→    if not subjective:
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   253→        state,
   254→        subjective,
   255→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   256→        max_quality_items=3,
   257→        max_integrity_items=5,
   258→    )
   259→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   260→        print()
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "group_matches_by_file",
   265→    "render_findings",
   266→    "show_agent_plan",
   267→    "show_subjective_followup",
   268→    "write_show_output_file",
   269→]
   270→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    return (
    93→        detector == scope
    94→        or filepath == scope
    95→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   100→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   101→
   102→
   103→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   104→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   105→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   106→    target = cleaned.lower()
   107→
   108→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   109→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   110→            return str(dim_key)
   111→    return slugify(cleaned)
   112→
   113→
   114→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   115→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   116→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   117→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   118→    return {
   119→        cleaned.lower(),
   120→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   121→        slugify(cleaned),
   122→        canonical.lower(),
   123→        slugify(canonical),
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   128→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   129→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   130→    if not lang:
   131→        return None
   132→
   133→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   134→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   138→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   142→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   143→        return None
   144→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   145→
   146→
   147→def primary_command_for_finding(
   148→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   149→) -> str:
   150→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   151→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   152→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   153→        available_fixers = [
   154→            fixer
   155→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   156→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   157→        ]
   158→        if available_fixers:
   159→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   160→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   161→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   162→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   163→        return "desloppify show subjective_review --status open"
   164→    return f'desloppify resolve fixed "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   165→
   166→
   167→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   168→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   169→    if not dim_scores:
   170→        return {}
   171→
   172→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   173→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   174→    for entry in entries:
   175→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   176→        if not name:
   177→            continue
   178→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   179→        [REDACTED](name)
   180→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   181→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   182→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   183→            if not key:
   184→                continue
   185→            aliases.add(key)
   186→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   187→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   188→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   189→        for alias in aliases:
   190→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   191→    return scores
   192→
   193→
   194→def build_subjective_items(
   195→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   198→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   199→    if not dim_scores:
   200→        return []
   201→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   202→
   203→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   204→    if not subjective_entries:
   205→        return []
   206→    unassessed_dims = {
   207→        str(name).strip()
   208→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   209→            dim_scores
   210→        )
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   214→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   215→    for finding in findings.values():
   216→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   217→            continue
   218→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   219→        if not dim_key:
   220→            continue
   221→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   222→
   223→    items: list[dict] = []
   224→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   225→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   226→        if not name:
   227→            continue
   228→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   229→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   230→            continue
   231→
   232→        [REDACTED](name)
   233→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   234→        cli_keys = [
   235→            str(key).strip().lower()
   236→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   237→            if str(key).strip()
   238→        ]
   239→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   240→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   241→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   242→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   243→            name in unassessed_dims
   244→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   245→        )
   246→        if is_unassessed:
   247→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   248→        else:
   249→            if open_review > 0:
   250→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   251→            elif cli_keys:
   252→                primary_command = (
   253→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   254→                )
   255→            else:
   256→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   257→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%)"
   258→        items.append(
   259→            {
   260→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   261→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   262→                "file": ".",
   263→                "tier": 4,
   264→                "effective_tier": 4,
   265→                "confidence": "medium",
   266→                "summary": summary,
   267→                "detail": {
   268→                    "dimension_name": name,
   269→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   270→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   271→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   272→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   273→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   274→                },
   275→                "status": "open",
   276→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   277→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   278→            }
   279→        )
   280→    return items
```
