1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→
    40→
    41→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    42→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    43→
    44→    items: list[dict]
    45→    total: int
    46→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    47→    requested_tier: int | None
    48→    selected_tier: int | None
    49→    fallback_reason: str | None
    50→    available_tiers: list[int]
    51→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    52→
    53→
    54→def build_work_queue(
    55→    state: StateModel,
    56→    *,
    57→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    58→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    59→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    60→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    61→
    62→    status = resolved_options.status
    63→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    64→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    65→    try:
    66→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    67→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    68→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    69→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    70→
    71→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    72→        state,
    73→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    74→        status_filter=status,
    75→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    76→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    77→    )
    78→
    79→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    80→    if (
    81→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    82→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    83→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    84→    ):
    85→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    86→            state,
    87→            state.get("findings", {}),
    88→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
    89→        )
    90→        for item in subjective_items:
    91→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
    92→                all_items.append(item)
    93→
    94→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    95→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
    96→
    97→    requested_tier = (
    98→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
    99→    )
   100→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   101→    fallback_reason = None
   102→    filtered = all_items
   103→
   104→    if requested_tier is not None:
   105→        filtered = [
   106→            item
   107→            for item in all_items
   108→            if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == requested_tier
   109→        ]
   110→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   111→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   112→            if chosen is not None:
   113→                selected_tier = chosen
   114→                filtered = [
   115→                    item
   116→                    for item in all_items
   117→                    if int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == chosen
   118→                ]
   119→                fallback_reason = (
   120→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   121→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   122→                )
   123→        elif not filtered:
   124→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   125→
   126→    total = len(filtered)
   127→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   128→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   129→
   130→    if resolved_options.explain:
   131→        for item in filtered:
   132→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   133→
   134→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   135→    return {
   136→        "items": filtered,
   137→        "total": total,
   138→        "tier_counts": counts,
   139→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   140→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   141→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   142→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   143→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   144→    }
   145→
   146→
   147→__all__ = [
   148→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   149→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   150→    "WorkQueueResult",
   151→    "build_work_queue",
   152→    "group_queue_items",
   153→]
   154→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    84→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    85→        return (
    86→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
    87→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
    88→            subjective_score_value(item),
    89→            item.get("id", ""),
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    detail = detail_dict(item)
    93→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    94→    return (
    95→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
    96→        0,
    97→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
    98→        -review_weight,
    99→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   100→        item.get("id", ""),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   105→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   106→
   107→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   108→        return {
   109→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   110→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   111→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   112→            "policy": (
   113→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   114→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   115→            ),
   116→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   117→        }
   118→
   119→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   120→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   121→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   122→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   123→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   124→    if is_subjective:
   125→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   126→    elif is_review:
   127→        ranking_factors = [
   128→            "tier asc",
   129→            "confidence asc",
   130→            "review_weight desc",
   131→            "count desc",
   132→            "id asc",
   133→        ]
   134→    else:
   135→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   136→    explain = {
   137→        "kind": "finding",
   138→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   139→        "confidence": confidence,
   140→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   141→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   142→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   143→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   144→    }
   145→    if is_review:
   146→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   147→    if is_subjective:
   148→        explain["policy"] = (
   149→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   150→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   151→        )
   152→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   153→    return explain
   154→
   155→
   156→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   157→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   158→    for item in items:
   159→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   160→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   161→    return counts
   162→
   163→
   164→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   165→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   166→    if not available:
   167→        return None
   168→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   169→
   170→
   171→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   172→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   173→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   174→    for item in items:
   175→        if group == "file":
   176→            key = item.get("file", "")
   177→        elif group == "detector":
   178→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   179→        elif group == "tier":
   180→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   181→        else:
   182→            key = "items"
   183→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   184→    return grouped
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = [
   188→    "build_finding_items",
   189→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   190→    "item_explain",
   191→    "item_sort_key",
   192→    "tier_counts",
   193→    "subjective_score_value",
   194→    "group_queue_items",
   195→]
   196→

1→"""Payload builders for show command query/output serialization."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→
     9→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    10→class ShowPayloadMeta:
    11→    total_matches: int | None = None
    12→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int] | None = None
    13→    noise_budget: int | None = None
    14→    global_noise_budget: int | None = None
    15→
    16→
    17→def build_show_payload(
    18→    matches: list[dict],
    19→    pattern: str,
    20→    status_filter: str,
    21→    meta: ShowPayloadMeta | None = None,
    22→) -> dict:
    23→    """Build the structured JSON payload shared by query file and --output."""
    24→    resolved_meta = meta or ShowPayloadMeta()
    25→
    26→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    27→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    28→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
    29→    for finding in matches:
    30→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    31→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
    32→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
    33→
    34→    payload = {
    35→        "query": pattern,
    36→        "status_filter": status_filter,
    37→        "total": len(matches),
    38→        "summary": {
    39→            "by_tier": {f"T{tier}": count for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())},
    40→            "by_detector": dict(sorted(by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1])),
    41→            "files": len(by_file),
    42→        },
    43→        "by_file": {
    44→            fp: [
    45→                {
    46→                    "id": f["id"],
    47→                    "tier": f["tier"],
    48→                    "confidence": f["confidence"],
    49→                    "summary": f["summary"],
    50→                    "detail": f.get("detail", {}),
    51→                }
    52→                for f in fs
    53→            ]
    54→            for fp, fs in sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1]))
    55→        },
    56→    }
    57→    if resolved_meta.total_matches is not None:
    58→        payload["total_matching"] = resolved_meta.total_matches
    59→    if resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector:
    60→        payload["hidden"] = {
    61→            "by_detector": resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector,
    62→            "total": sum(resolved_meta.hidden_by_detector.values()),
    63→        }
    64→    if resolved_meta.noise_budget is not None:
    65→        payload["noise_budget"] = resolved_meta.noise_budget
    66→    if resolved_meta.global_noise_budget is not None:
    67→        payload["noise_global_budget"] = resolved_meta.global_noise_budget
    68→    return payload
    69→
    70→
    71→__all__ = ["ShowPayloadMeta", "build_show_payload"]
    72→

## How the `show` Command Works End-to-End

### 1. Pattern Matching: What `show` Accepts

The `show` parser (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py`, line 117-120) defines `pattern` as a positional argument:

```python
p_show.add_argument(
    "pattern",
    nargs="?",
    default=None,
    help="File path, directory, detector name, finding ID, or glob",
)
```

So `show` accepts a single optional string that can be a file path, directory, detector name, finding ID, or glob pattern.

### 2. Command Flow in `cmd_show` (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`)

The flow has several early-exit branches before reaching the main pattern-matching path:

1. **"concerns" keyword** (line 168): If `pattern == "concerns"`, it short-circuits to `_show_concerns()`. This is a fully special-cased view.

2. **Numeric shorthand** (line 174): If the pattern is a digit like `"3"`, it looks up a finding ID by 1-based index from the last query output (`.desloppify/query.json`).

3. **Single-finding detail mode** (line 182): If the pattern contains `"::"`, it is treated as a finding ID and renders that single finding via `_show_single_finding()`. This bypasses all scope/queue logic.

4. **General scope/pattern mode** (line 195 onward): For everything else, it calls `resolve_show_scope()`, then `load_matches()`, then renders.

### 3. `resolve_show_scope` (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py`, line 13-30)

This is simple -- it just extracts the pattern and status filter:

- If `--chronic` is set, pattern can be `None` (defaults to `"<chronic>"`), status is forced to `"open"`, and `scope` is the raw pattern.
- If no pattern and no `--chronic`, it prints a help message and returns `ok=False`.
- Otherwise, `scope = pattern` (the raw string the user typed).

The `scope` string is passed directly to `build_work_queue`.

### 4. `load_matches` -- The Critical Line (`scope.py`, line 33-53)

```python
def load_matches(state, *, scope, status_filter, chronic):
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            count=None,
            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
            scope=scope,
            status=status_filter,
            include_subjective=False,   # <-- KEY
            chronic=chronic,
            no_tier_fallback=True,
        ),
    )
    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
```

**Two critical filters here:**

- **`include_subjective=False`**: This means `build_subjective_items()` is NEVER called for the `show` command. The synthetic subjective dimension items are excluded.
- **`item.get("kind") == "finding"`**: Even if subjective items somehow snuck in, this line filters to only items with `kind == "finding"`. The synthetic subjective items have `kind == "subjective_dimension"`, so they'd be excluded anyway. This is a double gate.

### 5. How Pattern Matching Actually Works: `scope_matches()` (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`, line 63-96)

This is the core filtering function, applied per-item in `build_finding_items()` (ranking.py line 76):

```python
def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    if not scope:
        return True

    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    kind = item.get("kind", "")

    # Branch 1: Glob patterns
    if "*" in scope:
        return any(
            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
        )

    # Branch 2: Finding ID prefix
    if "::" in scope:
        return item_id.startswith(scope)

    # Branch 3: Subjective dimension items (special case)
    lowered = scope.lower()
    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
        return (
            lowered in item_id.lower()
            or lowered in dimension.lower()
            or lowered in summary.lower()
        )

    # Branch 4: Default -- exact detector name OR file path match
    return (
        detector == scope
        or filepath == scope
        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
    )
```

**The four matching modes:**

| Scope Input | Matching Logic |
|---|---|
| Contains `*` (glob) | `fnmatch` against: item ID, filepath, detector name, dimension name, summary |
| Contains `::` | Prefix match against finding ID |
| No `*`, no `::`, item is `subjective_dimension` kind | Substring match (case-insensitive) against: item ID, dimension name, summary |
| No `*`, no `::`, item is regular finding | **Exact match** against detector name, OR exact/prefix match against filepath |

### 6. Answering Your Specific Questions

**Q: What patterns does `show` accept? Can you do `show review`, `show subjective_review`, `show smells`?**

Yes. For regular findings (which is all `show` returns, due to `include_subjective=False`), pattern matching uses Branch 4 (exact detector match or filepath match). So:

- `show review` -- matches all findings where `detector == "review"`. Works.
- `show subjective_review` -- matches all findings where `detector == "subjective_review"`. Works.
- `show smells` -- would only work if there's a detector literally named `"smells"`. The actual detector names are things like `py_smells`, `ts_smells`, etc. So `show smells` would match nothing. You'd need `show py_smells` or `show *smells*` (glob form).
- `show src/utils.py` -- matches all findings against that filepath.
- `show src/` -- matches all findings in files under `src/`.

**Q: How does pattern matching work -- is it matching against detector name, file path, finding ID?**

For non-glob, non-`::` patterns, it matches against **detector name** (exact equality) or **file path** (exact equality or directory prefix). Finding IDs are only matched when the pattern contains `::`. To match across multiple fields, you must use a glob pattern with `*`.

**Q: What's the difference between `show review` and `show review --status open`?**

**Functionally nothing** -- `--status` defaults to `"open"` (parser_groups.py line 128). Both filter to `detector == "review"` AND `status == "open"`. To see resolved review findings, you'd use `show review --status fixed` or `show review --status all`.

**Q: Could `show subjective` work as a pattern that matches both `review` and `subjective_review` findings?**

**No, not as currently implemented.** Here's why:

1. The `show` command calls `load_matches`, which builds items with `include_subjective=False` and then filters to `kind == "finding"` only.

2. For regular findings, `scope_matches` uses Branch 4 (the default branch), which does an **exact** detector name comparison: `detector == scope`. The string `"subjective"` does not exactly equal `"review"` or `"subjective_review"`, so neither would match.

3. Branch 3 (substring matching against dimension/summary) only applies to items with `kind == "subjective_dimension"`, which are synthetic items from `build_subjective_items()`. Those items never reach `show` because of the double gate (`include_subjective=False` + `kind == "finding"` filter).

4. You **could** use a glob: `show subjective*` would fnmatch against detector names and match `subjective_review`, but it would NOT match `review` (since "review" doesn't start with "subjective").

5. To match both `review` AND `subjective_review` findings, you'd need `show *review*` (glob). That would fnmatch the detector name against `*review*`, matching both `"review"` and `"subjective_review"`.

**Q: What does `build_subjective_items()` create, and could those items show up in a `show` view?**

`build_subjective_items()` (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`, line 194-280) creates **synthetic work items** representing subjective dimensions that are below a score threshold. Each item looks like:

```python
{
    "id": "subjective::mid_elegance",       # slugified dimension key
    "detector": "subjective_assessment",     # always this value
    "file": ".",                              # always codebase-wide
    "tier": 4,                               # always tier 4
    "effective_tier": 4,
    "confidence": "medium",
    "summary": "Subjective dimension below target: Mid elegance (72.5%)",
    "detail": {
        "dimension_name": "Mid elegance",
        "dimension": "mid_elegance",
        "issues": 3,
        "strict_score": 72.5,
        "open_review_findings": 2,
        "cli_keys": ["mid_elegance"],
    },
    "status": "open",
    "kind": "subjective_dimension",          # NOT "finding"
    "primary_command": "desloppify show review --status open",
}
```

These items **cannot** show up in a `show` view for two reasons:

1. `load_matches` passes `include_subjective=False` to `build_work_queue`, so `build_subjective_items()` is never called.
2. Even if they were included, the final filter `item.get("kind") == "finding"` would exclude them since their kind is `"subjective_dimension"`.

The `primary_command` on these synthetic items is interesting -- it often points to `desloppify show review --status open` (when there are open review findings) or `desloppify review --prepare` (when unassessed or no open review findings). So the intended flow is: `next` command surfaces subjective items, which then direct the user to `show review`.

### 7. Summary: Could `show subjective` Work?

To make `show subjective` work as a view that shows both `review` and `subjective_review` findings, you would need to change the system in one of these ways:

**Option A: Add a special-case keyword** (like `"concerns"` is special-cased). In `cmd_show`, detect `pattern == "subjective"` and route to a custom handler that queries both detector types.

**Option B: Change `scope_matches` to support substring/prefix matching for detector names.** Currently Branch 4 does `detector == scope` (exact). If it did `scope in detector` or `detector.startswith(scope)`, then `"subjective"` would match `"subjective_review"` but still not `"review"`.

**Option C: Make `show` include subjective items.** Remove the `include_subjective=False` and `kind == "finding"` gates in `load_matches`. Then the synthetic dimension items would appear alongside real findings. But those items have a different structure (kind `"subjective_dimension"`, file `.`, etc.) so the rendering code in `render.py` would need updates.

**Option D: Use a glob.** Users can already do `show *review*` to get both `review` and `subjective_review` findings. But this only covers real findings, not the synthetic dimension items.

The cleanest approach for a `show subjective` command would likely be **Option A** -- a special-cased keyword (like `concerns`) that builds a composite view from both `subjective_review` findings and the synthetic `build_subjective_items()` output, with custom rendering.

   119→    if detail.get("category"):
   120→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   121→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   122→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   123→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   124→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   125→
   126→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   127→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   128→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   129→        if snippet:
   130→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   131→            print(snippet)
   132→
   133→    if dim_scores:
   134→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   135→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   136→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   137→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   138→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   139→            print(
   140→                colorize(
   141→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   142→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   143→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   144→                    "dim",
   145→                )
   146→            )
   147→
   148→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   149→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   150→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   151→        similar_count = sum(
   152→            1
   153→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   154→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   155→        )
   156→        if similar_count > 1:
   157→            print(
   158→                colorize(
   159→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   160→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   161→                    "cyan",
   162→                )
   163→            )
   164→    if explain:
   165→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   166→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   167→        base = (
   168→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   169→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   170→        )
   171→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   172→        if policy:
   173→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   174→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   175→
   176→
   177→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   178→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   179→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   180→        return
   181→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   182→    if not explain:
   183→        return
   184→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   185→    if available:
   186→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   187→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   188→
   189→
   190→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   191→    if queue.get("items"):
   192→        return False
   193→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   194→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   195→    if tier is not None:
   196→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   197→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   198→        if available:
   199→            commands = " | ".join(
   200→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   201→            )
   202→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   203→    return True
   204→
   205→
   206→def render_terminal_items(
   207→    items: list[dict],
   208→    dim_scores: dict,
   209→    findings_scoped: dict,
   210→    *,
   211→    group: str,
   212→    explain: bool,
   213→) -> None:
   214→    if group != "item":
   215→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   216→        return
   217→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   218→        if idx > 0:
   219→            print()
   220→        label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]" if len(items) > 1 else "  Next item"
   221→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   222→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain)
   223→
   224→
   225→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   226→    if len(items) != 1 or items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   227→        return
   228→    item = items[0]
   229→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   230→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   231→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   232→        primary = item.get(
   233→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective_review --status open"
   234→        )
   235→        print(f"    {primary}")
   236→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   237→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   238→        return
   239→
   240→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   241→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   242→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   243→        print(f"    {primary}")
   244→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   245→    else:
   246→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   247→
   248→    print(
   249→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   250→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   251→    )
   252→    print(
   253→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   254→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   255→    )
   256→
   257→
   258→def render_followup_nudges(
   259→    state: dict,
   260→    dim_scores: dict,
   261→    findings_scoped: dict,
   262→    *,
   263→    strict_score: float | None,
   264→    target_strict_score: float,
   265→) -> None:
   266→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   267→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   268→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   269→        state,
   270→        subjective_entries,
   271→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   272→        max_quality_items=3,
   273→        max_integrity_items=5,
   274→    )
   275→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   276→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   277→    if strict_score is not None:
   278→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   279→        if gap > 0:
   280→            print(
   281→                colorize(
   282→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   283→                    "cyan",
   284→                )
   285→            )
   286→        else:
   287→            print(
   288→                colorize(
   289→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   290→                    "green",
   291→                )
   292→            )
   293→    print_subjective_followup(followup, leading_newline=True)
   294→
   295→    coverage_open, coverage_reasons, holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   296→        findings_scoped
   297→    )
   298→    holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())
   299→    if unassessed_subjective or holistic_open > 0:
   300→        bits: list[str] = []
   301→        if unassessed_subjective:
   302→            bits.append("unassessed subjective dimensions")
   303→        if holistic_open > 0:
   304→            bits.append("holistic review stale/missing")
   305→        gap_label = " + ".join(bits)
   306→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective integrity gap: {gap_label}", "yellow"))
   307→        print(
   308→            colorize(
   309→                "  Priority: `desloppify review --prepare`", "dim"
   310→            )
   311→        )
   312→        print(
   313→            colorize(
   314→                "  Then import and rerun `desloppify scan` to refresh strict score.",
   315→                "dim",
   316→            )
   317→        )
   318→        if unassessed_subjective:
   319→            rendered = ", ".join(name for name in unassessed_subjective[:3])
   320→            if len(unassessed_subjective) > 3:
   321→                rendered = f"{rendered}, +{len(unassessed_subjective) - 3} more"
   322→            print(colorize(f"  Unassessed (0% placeholder): {rendered}", "dim"))
   323→
   324→    open_review = [
   325→        finding
   326→        for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   327→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   328→    ]
   329→    if low_assessed and open_review:
   330→        print(
   331→            colorize(
   332→                "  Subjective bottleneck: prioritize investigating open review findings to move elegance scores.",
   333→                "yellow",
   334→            )
   335→        )
   336→
   337→    if coverage_open > 0:
   338→        reason_parts = []
   339→        if coverage_reasons.get("changed", 0) > 0:
   340→            reason_parts.append(f"{coverage_reasons['changed']} changed")
   341→        if coverage_reasons.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:
   342→            reason_parts.append(f"{coverage_reasons['unreviewed']} unreviewed")
   343→        reason_text = ", ".join(reason_parts) if reason_parts else "stale/unreviewed"
   344→        suffix = "file" if coverage_open == 1 else "files"
   345→        print(
   346→            colorize(
   347→                f"  Subjective coverage debt: {coverage_open} {suffix} ({reason_text})",
   348→                "cyan",
   349→            )
   350→        )
   351→        if holistic_open > 0:
   352→            print(
   353→                colorize(
   354→                    f"  Includes {holistic_open} holistic stale/missing signal(s).",
   355→                    "yellow",
   356→                )
   357→            )
   358→        print(
   359→            colorize(
   360→                "  Triage: `desloppify show subjective_review --status open`", "dim"
   361→            )
   362→        )
   363→
   364→    if open_review:
   365→        uninvestigated = sum(
   366→            1
   367→            for finding in open_review
   368→            if not finding.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   369→        )
   370→        suffix = f" ({uninvestigated} uninvestigated)" if uninvestigated else ""
   371→        print(
   372→            colorize(
   373→                f"  Also: {len(open_review)} review findings open{suffix}. "
   374→                "Run `desloppify show review --status open`.",
   375→                "cyan",
   376→            )
   377→        )
   378→    elif low_assessed or unassessed_subjective or coverage_open > 0:
   379→        print(
   380→            colorize(
   381→                "  Then import review output and rerun `desloppify scan` to refresh subjective scores.",
   382→                "dim",
   383→            )
   384→        )
   385→
   386→
   387→__all__ = [
   388→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   389→    "render_followup_nudges",
   390→    "render_queue_header",
   391→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   392→    "render_terminal_items",
   393→    "scorecard_subjective",
   394→    "show_empty_queue",
   395→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   396→]
   397→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    15→        "id": item.get("id"),
    16→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    17→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    18→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    19→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": item.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    24→        "status": item.get("status"),
    25→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→    }
    27→    explain = item.get("explain")
    28→    if explain is not None:
    29→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    30→    return serialized
    31→
    32→
    33→def build_query_payload(
    34→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    35→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    36→    *,
    37→    command: str,
    38→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    39→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    40→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    41→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    42→    return {
    43→        "command": command,
    44→        "items": serialized,
    45→        "queue": {
    46→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    47→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    48→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    49→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    50→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    51→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    52→        },
    53→        "narrative": narrative,
    54→    }
    55→
    56→
    57→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    58→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
    59→    lines = [
    60→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
    61→        "",
    62→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
    63→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
    64→    ]
    65→    for item in items:
    66→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    67→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    68→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    69→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
    70→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
    71→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
    72→    lines.append("")
    73→    return "\n".join(lines)
    74→
    75→
    76→def write_output_file(
    77→    output_file: str,
    78→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    79→    item_count: int,
    80→    *,
    81→    safe_write_text_fn,
    82→    colorize_fn,
    83→) -> bool:
    84→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
    85→    try:
    86→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    87→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
    88→    except OSError as exc:
    89→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    90→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
    91→        return False
    92→    return True
    93→
    94→
    95→def emit_non_terminal_output(
    96→    output_format: str,
    97→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    98→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    99→) -> bool:
   100→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   101→    renderers = {
   102→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   103→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   104→    }
   105→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   106→    if renderer is None:
   107→        return False
   108→    renderer()
   109→    return True
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = [
   113→    "build_query_payload",
   114→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   115→    "render_markdown",
   116→    "serialize_item",
   117→    "write_output_file",
   118→]
   119→

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→
    89→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    90→
    91→    queue = build_work_queue(
    92→        state,
    93→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    94→            tier=tier,
    95→            count=count,
    96→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    97→            scope=scope,
    98→            status=status,
    99→            include_subjective=True,
   100→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   101→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   102→            explain=explain,
   103→        ),
   104→    )
   105→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   106→
   107→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   108→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   109→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   110→        state,
   111→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   115→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative
   116→    )
   117→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   118→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   119→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   120→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   121→        state,
   122→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   123→    )
   124→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   125→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   126→    ]
   127→    write_query(payload)
   128→
   129→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   130→    if output_file:
   131→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   132→            output_file,
   133→            payload,
   134→            len(items),
   135→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   136→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   137→        ):
   138→            return
   139→        raise SystemExit(1)
   140→
   141→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   145→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   150→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   151→        state.get("findings", {}),
   152→        state.get("scan_path"),
   153→    )
   154→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   155→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain
   156→    )
   157→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   158→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   159→        state,
   160→        dim_scores,
   161→        findings_scoped,
   162→        strict_score=strict_score,
   163→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   164→    )
   165→    print()
   166→
   167→
   168→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   169→

1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→

1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
     8→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
     9→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(
    14→    state: dict, *, threshold: float = 90.0
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Return subjective dimensions with score below threshold and evidence issues."""
    17→    low_dims: list[str] = []
    18→    dimension_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    19→    if not isinstance(dimension_scores, dict):
    20→        return low_dims
    21→
    22→    for dim_name, payload in dimension_scores.items():
    23→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    24→            continue
    25→        score = payload.get("score")
    26→        detectors = payload.get("detectors", {})
    27→        subjective_payload = (
    28→            detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    29→            if isinstance(detectors, dict)
    30→            else None
    31→        )
    32→        if not isinstance(subjective_payload, dict):
    33→            continue
    34→        issues = subjective_payload.get("issues", 0)
    35→        if not isinstance(score, int | float) or not isinstance(
    36→            issues, int | float
    37→        ):
    38→            continue
    39→        if score < threshold and issues > 0:
    40→            low_dims.append(str(dim_name))
    41→    return low_dims
    42→
    43→
    44→def _print_narrative_plan_fields(narrative: dict) -> None:
    45→    """Print additional narrative plan fields when present."""
    46→    why_now = narrative.get("why_now")
    47→    primary_action = narrative.get("primary_action")
    48→    verification_step = narrative.get("verification_step")
    49→    risk_flags = narrative.get("risk_flags")
    50→
    51→    has_plan = bool(why_now) or bool(primary_action) or bool(verification_step) or bool(
    52→        risk_flags
    53→    )
    54→    if not has_plan:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    print(colorize("  Narrative Plan:", "cyan"))
    58→    if isinstance(why_now, str) and why_now.strip():
    59→        print(colorize(f"    Why now: {why_now.strip()}", "dim"))
    60→
    61→    if isinstance(primary_action, dict):
    62→        command = primary_action.get("command")
    63→        description = primary_action.get("description")
    64→        if isinstance(command, str) and command.strip():
    65→            line = f"    Next: `{command.strip()}`"
    66→            if isinstance(description, str) and description.strip():
    67→                line += f" — {description.strip()}"
    68→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
    69→
    70→    if isinstance(verification_step, dict):
    71→        command = verification_step.get("command")
    72→        reason = verification_step.get("reason")
    73→        if isinstance(command, str) and command.strip():
    74→            line = f"    Verify: `{command.strip()}`"
    75→            if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.strip():
    76→                line += f" — {reason.strip()}"
    77→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
    78→
    79→    if isinstance(risk_flags, list):
    80→        for risk in risk_flags:
    81→            if not isinstance(risk, dict):
    82→                continue
    83→            message = risk.get("message")
    84→            if not isinstance(message, str) or not message.strip():
    85→                continue
    86→            severity = str(risk.get("severity", "")).lower()
    87→            style = "yellow" if severity in {"high", "critical"} else "dim"
    88→            print(colorize(f"    Risk: {message.strip()}", style))
    89→    print()
    90→
    91→
    92→def _print_filtered_reminders(narrative: dict) -> None:
    93→    """Print narrative reminders except low-value report score reminders."""
    94→    reminders = narrative.get("reminders")
    95→    if not isinstance(reminders, list):
    96→        return
    97→    messages: list[str] = []
    98→    for reminder in reminders:
    99→        if not isinstance(reminder, dict):
   100→            continue
   101→        if reminder.get("type") == "report_scores":
   102→            continue
   103→        message = reminder.get("message")
   104→        if isinstance(message, str) and message.strip():
   105→            messages.append(message.strip())
   106→    if not messages:
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    print(colorize("  Reminders:", "dim"))
   110→    for message in messages:
   111→        print(colorize(f"    - {message}", "dim"))
   112→    print()
   113→
   114→
   115→def _show_subjective_score_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   116→    """Nudge rerun when subjective dimensions are low with evidence issues."""
   117→    low_dims = _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(state, threshold=90.0)
   118→    if not low_dims:
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   122→    reviewed_files = review_cache.get("files", {}) if isinstance(review_cache, dict) else {}
   123→    verb = "rerun" if isinstance(reviewed_files, dict) and reviewed_files else "run"
   124→
   125→    dim_preview = ", ".join(low_dims[:3])
   126→    if len(low_dims) > 3:
   127→        dim_preview += f", +{len(low_dims) - 3} more"
   128→
   129→    print(colorize(f"  Subjective scores below 90: {dim_preview}", "yellow"))
   130→    print(
   131→        colorize(
   132→            f"  You can {verb} the subjective scoring with "
   133→            "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   134→            "cyan",
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→    print(
   138→        colorize(
   139→            "  Then review progress in `desloppify status` and investigate blockers via `desloppify next`.",
   140→            "dim",
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→    print()
   144→
   145→
   146→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
   147→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   148→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
   149→        return {}
   150→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
   151→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   152→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   153→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
   154→    return {}
   155→
   156→
   157→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
   158→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
   159→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   161→        return []
   162→
   163→    warnings: list[str] = []
   164→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
   165→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   166→            continue
   167→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
   168→        confidence = coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   169→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   170→            continue
   171→
   172→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
   173→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
   174→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   175→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
   176→
   177→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
   178→        if impact:
   179→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
   180→        if remediation:
   181→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
   182→        warnings.append(line)
   183→    return warnings
   184→
   185→
   186→def show_post_scan_analysis(
   187→    diff: dict,
   188→    state: dict,
   189→    lang,
   190→    *,
   191→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
   192→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   193→    """Print warnings, narrative headline, and top action. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
   194→    warnings = []
   195→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
   196→        warnings.append(
   197→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted? Check: git log --oneline -5"
   198→        )
   199→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
   200→        warnings.append(
   201→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading from recent fixes. Run fixers again."
   202→        )
   203→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
   204→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
   205→    if chronic_count > 0:
   206→        warnings.append(
   207→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} "
   208→            "(reopened 2+ times). These keep bouncing — fix properly or wontfix. "
   209→            "Run: `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
   210→        )
   211→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
   212→
   213→    if warnings:
   214→        for warning in warnings:
   215→            print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
   216→        print()
   217→
   218→    # Computed narrative: headline + top action as terminal suggestion
   219→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   220→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   221→        state,
   222→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
   223→            diff=diff,
   224→            lang=lang_name,
   225→            command="scan",
   226→        ),
   227→    )
   228→
   229→    # Show one actionable plan and optional strategy context.
   230→    print(
   231→        colorize(
   232→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   233→            "yellow",
   234→        )
   235→    )
   236→    strategy = narrative.get("strategy") or {}
   237→    hint = strategy.get("hint")
   238→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   239→
   240→    if actions:
   241→        top = actions[0]
   242→        print(
   243→            colorize(
   244→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}", "cyan"
   245→            )
   246→        )
   247→        if hint:
   248→            print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {hint}", "dim"))
   249→        print()
   250→    elif hint:
   251→        print(colorize(f"  Agent focus: {hint}", "cyan"))
   252→        print()
   253→
   254→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   255→        print()
   256→
   257→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   258→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   259→        print()
   260→
   261→    _print_narrative_plan_fields(narrative)
   262→    _print_filtered_reminders(narrative)
   263→    _show_subjective_score_nudge(state)
   264→
   265→    # Review findings nudge
   266→    scoped_findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   267→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   268→    )
   269→    open_review = [
   270→        finding
   271→        for finding in scoped_findings.values()
   272→        if finding["status"] == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   273→    ]
   274→    open_subjective, _subjective_reasons, holistic_reasons = (
   275→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped_findings)
   276→    )
   277→    holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())
   278→    if open_review:
   279→        suffix = "s" if len(open_review) != 1 else ""
   280→        print(
   281→            colorize(
   282→                f"  Review: {len(open_review)} finding{suffix} pending — `desloppify show review --status open`",
   283→                "cyan",
   284→            )
   285→        )
   286→        print()
   287→    if open_subjective > 0:
   288→        if holistic_open > 0:
   289→            print(
   290→                colorize(
   291→                    f"  Subjective integrity: {holistic_open} holistic stale/missing signal(s) — "
   292→                    "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`",
   293→                    "yellow",
   294→                )
   295→            )
   296→        else:
   297→            print(
   298→                colorize(
   299→                    f"  Subjective coverage: {open_subjective} file-level review signal(s) open — "
   300→                    "`desloppify show subjective_review --status open`",
   301→                    "cyan",
   302→                )
   303→            )
   304→        print()
   305→
   306→    # Auto-queue: nudge subjective review for high-complexity unreviewed files
   307→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {}).get("files", {})
   308→    scoped = scoped_findings
   309→    complex_unreviewed = set()
   310→    for finding in scoped.values():
   311→        if (
   312→            finding.get("detector") in ("structural", "smells")
   313→            and finding.get("status") == "wontfix"
   314→            and finding.get("file") not in review_cache
   315→        ):
   316→            complex_unreviewed.add(finding.get("file"))
   317→    if len(complex_unreviewed) >= 3:
   318→        print(
   319→            colorize(
   320→                f"  {len(complex_unreviewed)} complex files have never been reviewed — "
   321→                "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   322→                "would provide actionable refactoring guidance",
   323→                "dim",
   324→            )
   325→        )
   326→        print()
   327→
   328→    return warnings, narrative
   329→
   330→
   331→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   332→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   333→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   334→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   335→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   336→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   337→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   338→    confidence_reduced = (
   339→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   340→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   344→        return
   345→
   346→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   347→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   348→    strict_gap = (
   349→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   350→    )
   351→
   352→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   353→    actionable = (
   354→        stats.get("open", 0)
   355→        + wontfix
   356→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   357→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   358→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   359→    )
   360→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   363→
   364→    if wontfix > 5:
   365→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   366→            style = "red"
   367→            msg = (
   368→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   369→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   370→            )
   371→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   372→            style = "yellow"
   373→            msg = (
   374→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   375→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   376→            )
   377→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   378→            style = "yellow"
   379→            msg = (
   380→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   381→                "pts below lenient"
   382→            )
   383→        else:
   384→            style = "dim"
   385→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   386→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   387→
   388→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   389→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   390→        if dim_scores:
   391→            gaps = []
   392→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   393→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   394→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   395→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   396→                if gap > 0:
   397→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   398→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   399→            if gaps:
   400→                top = gaps[:2]
   401→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   402→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   403→
   404→    if ignored > 0:
   405→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   406→        print(
   407→            colorize(
   408→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   409→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   410→                style,
   411→            )
   412→        )
   413→        print(
   414→            colorize(
   415→                "    Suppressed findings still count against strict and verified scores",
   416→                "dim",
   417→            )
   418→        )
   419→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:
   420→        print(
   421→            colorize(
   422→                f"  {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   423→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   424→                "dim",
   425→            )
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    if confidence_reduced:
   429→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   430→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   431→        confidence = coerce_confidence(
   432→            score_confidence.get("confidence"),
   433→            default=1.0,
   434→        )
   435→        dim_text = ""
   436→        if isinstance(impacted_dimensions, list) and impacted_dimensions:
   437→            preview = ", ".join(str(item) for item in impacted_dimensions[:3])
   438→            if len(impacted_dimensions) > 3:
   439→                preview += f", +{len(impacted_dimensions) - 3} more"
   440→            dim_text = f" (dimensions: {preview})"
   441→        print(
   442→            colorize(
   443→                f"  ⚠ Score confidence reduced to {confidence * 100:.0f}%{dim_text}",
   444→                "yellow",
   445→            )
   446→        )
   447→        if isinstance(detectors, list):
   448→            for detector in detectors[:3]:
   449→                if not isinstance(detector, dict):
   450→                    continue
   451→                summary = str(detector.get("summary", "")).strip()
   452→                remediation = str(detector.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   453→                if summary:
   454→                    print(colorize(f"    - {summary}", "dim"))
   455→                if remediation:
   456→                    print(colorize(f"      Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   457→
   458→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 55, "dim"))
   459→    print()
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = ["show_post_scan_analysis", "show_score_integrity"]
   463→

    17→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    18→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    19→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    21→        return 0
    22→
    23→    streak = 0
    24→    for entry in reversed(history):
    25→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    26→            break
    27→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    28→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    29→            break
    30→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    31→            break
    32→        streak += 1
    33→    return streak

    36→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):

    79→
    80→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)

1→"""Prepare flow for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.review import runtime as review_runtime_mod
    10→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_positive_int
    11→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    12→from desloppify.intelligence import review as review_mod
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES = 80
    16→
    17→
    18→def _redacted_review_config(config: dict | None) -> dict:
    19→    """Return review packet config with target score removed for blind assessment."""
    20→    if not isinstance(config, dict):
    21→        return {}
    22→    return {key: value for key, value in config.items() if key != "target_strict_score"}
    23→
    24→
    25→def _coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config: dict | None) -> int | None:
    26→    """Resolve per-batch review file cap from config (0/negative => unlimited)."""
    27→    raw = (config or {}).get("review_batch_max_files", DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES)
    28→    try:
    29→        value = int(raw)
    30→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    31→        return DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES
    32→    return value if value > 0 else None
    33→
    34→
    35→def do_prepare(
    36→    args,
    37→    state,
    38→    lang,
    39→    _state_path,
    40→    *,
    41→    config: dict,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    """Prepare mode: holistic-only review packet in query.json."""
    44→    path = Path(args.path)
    45→    dims_str = getattr(args, "dimensions", None)
    46→    dimensions = dims_str.split(",") if dims_str else None
    47→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
    48→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
    49→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
    50→        default=30,
    51→    )
    52→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
    53→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
    54→        default=20,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    lang_run, found_files = review_runtime_mod.setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
    58→
    59→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    60→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    61→        state,
    62→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    63→    )
    64→    data = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
    65→        path,
    66→        lang_run,
    67→        state,
    68→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    69→            dimensions=dimensions,
    70→            files=found_files or None,
    71→            max_files_per_batch=_coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    72→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
    73→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    74→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    75→        ),
    76→    )
    77→    next_command = (
    78→        "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
    79→    )
    80→    if retrospective:
    81→        next_command += (
    82→            " --retrospective"
    83→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
    84→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
    85→        )
    86→    data["config"] = _redacted_review_config(config)
    87→    data["narrative"] = narrative
    88→    data["next_command"] = next_command
    89→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    90→    if total == 0:
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(

    94→                "Nothing to review.",
    95→                "red",
    96→            ),
    97→            file=sys.stderr,
    98→        )
    99→        scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if isinstance(state, dict) else None
   100→        if scan_path:
   101→            print(
   102→                colorize(

   107→                file=sys.stderr,

   109→        else:

   113→                    "yellow",
   114→                ),
   115→                file=sys.stderr,
   116→            )
   117→        sys.exit(1)
   118→    write_query(data)
   119→    batches = data.get("investigation_batches", [])
   120→    print(colorize(f"\n  Holistic review prepared: {total} files in codebase", "bold"))
   121→    if retrospective:

    54→def _state_subjective_assessments(

    66→def _state_lang_capabilities(

    68→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    69→    """Return normalized language capability map from state."""
    70→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    71→    if capabilities is None:
    72→        normalized: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
    73→        state["lang_capabilities"] = normalized
    74→        return normalized
    75→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict):
