```
   143→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   144→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   145→    config: dict[str, object],
   146→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   147→    lang: LangRun | None,
   148→) -> LangRun | None:
   149→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   150→    if not lang:
   151→        return None
   152→
   153→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   154→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   155→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   156→        lang,
   157→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   158→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   159→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   160→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   161→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   162→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   163→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   164→        ),
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   168→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   169→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   170→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   171→    }
   172→    return runtime_lang
   173→
   174→
   175→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   176→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   177→    *,
   178→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   179→) -> int:
   180→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   181→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   182→
   183→    reset_keys = {
   184→        key.strip()
   185→        for key in assessments
   186→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   187→    }
   188→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   189→
   190→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   191→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   192→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   193→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   194→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   195→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   196→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   197→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   198→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   199→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   200→            payload.pop("components", None)
   201→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   202→            continue
   203→        assessments[key] = {
   204→            "score": 0.0,
   205→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   206→            "assessed_at": now,
   207→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   208→            "placeholder": True,
   209→        }
   210→    return len(reset_keys)
   211→
   212→
   213→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   214→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   215→) -> int:
   216→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   217→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   218→
   219→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   220→    expired = 0
   221→    for payload in assessments.values():
   222→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   223→            continue
   224→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   225→            continue
   226→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   227→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   228→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   229→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   230→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   231→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   233→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   234→        payload.pop("components", None)
   235→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   236→        expired += 1
   237→    return expired

   240→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   241→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   242→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   243→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   244→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   245→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   246→    path = Path(args.path)
   247→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   248→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   249→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   250→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   251→        state
   252→    )
   253→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   254→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   255→            state,
   256→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   257→        )
   258→
   259→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   260→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   261→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   262→
   263→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   264→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   265→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   266→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   267→
   268→    return ScanRuntime(
   269→        args=args,
   270→        state_path=state_file,
   271→        state=state,
   272→        path=path,
   273→        config=config,
   274→        lang=lang,
   275→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   276→        profile=profile,
   277→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   278→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   279→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   280→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   281→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   286→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   287→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   288→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   289→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   290→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   291→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   292→        return findings
   293→
   294→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   295→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   296→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   297→    )
   298→    if not stale:
   299→        return findings
   300→
   301→    augmented = list(findings)
   302→    augmented.extend(stale)
   303→    for stale_finding in stale:
   304→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   305→    return augmented
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   309→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→    *,
   312→    decay_scans: int,
   313→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   314→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   315→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   316→        findings,
   317→        state=runtime.state,
   318→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   319→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   320→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   321→    )
   322→
   323→
   324→def run_scan_generation(
   325→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   326→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   327→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   328→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   329→        disable_parse_cache,
   330→        enable_parse_cache,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→    enable_file_cache()
   334→    enable_parse_cache()
   335→    try:
   336→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   337→            runtime.path,
   338→            lang=runtime.lang,
   339→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   340→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   341→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   342→                profile=runtime.profile,
   343→            ),
   344→        )
   345→    finally:
   346→        disable_parse_cache()
   347→        disable_file_cache()
   348→
   349→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   350→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   351→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   352→    )
   353→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   354→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   355→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   356→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   357→    )
   358→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   359→        findings,
   360→        runtime,
   361→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   362→    )
   363→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   364→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   365→
   366→
   367→def merge_scan_results(
   368→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   369→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   370→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   371→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   372→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   373→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   374→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   375→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   376→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   377→
   378→    if not path_changed:
   379→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   380→    else:
   381→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   382→    prev_dim_scores = (
   383→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   387→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   388→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   389→
   390→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   391→
   392→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   393→        runtime.state,
   394→        findings,
   395→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   396→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   397→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   398→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   399→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   400→            potentials=potentials,
   401→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   402→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   403→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   404→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   405→        ),
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   409→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   410→    )
   411→    state_mod.save_state(
   412→        runtime.state,
   413→        runtime.state_path,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   415→    )
   416→
   417→    return ScanMergeResult(
   418→        diff=diff,
   419→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   420→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   421→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   422→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   423→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   424→    )
   425→
   426→
   427→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   428→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   429→    config: dict[str, object],
   430→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   431→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   432→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   433→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   434→    )
   435→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   436→    open_findings = [
   437→        finding
   438→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   439→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   440→        ).values()
   441→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   442→    ]
   443→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   444→        open_findings,
   445→        budget=noise_budget,
   446→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   447→    )
   448→
   449→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   450→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   451→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   452→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   453→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   454→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   455→    )
   456→
   457→
   458→def persist_reminder_history(
   459→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   460→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   461→) -> None:
   462→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   463→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   464→        return
   465→
   466→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   467→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   468→    state_mod.save_state(
   469→        runtime.state,
   470→        runtime.state_path,
   471→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   472→    )
   473→
   474→
   475→__all__ = [
   476→    "ScanStateContractError",
   477→    "ScanMergeResult",
   478→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   479→    "ScanRuntime",
   480→    "merge_scan_results",
   481→    "persist_reminder_history",
   482→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   483→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   484→    "run_scan_generation",
   485→]
   486→
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(followup: SubjectiveFollowup, *, leading_newline: bool = False) -> bool:
    21→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    22→
    23→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    24→    """
    25→    printed = False
    26→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    27→    if followup.low_assessed:
    28→        print(
    29→            colorize(
    30→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    31→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    32→                "cyan",
    33→            )
    34→        )
    35→        print(
    36→            colorize(
    37→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    38→                "dim",
    39→            )
    40→        )
    41→        printed = True
    42→
    43→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    44→        if printed:
    45→            print()
    46→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    47→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    48→        printed = True
    49→    return printed
    50→
    51→
    52→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    53→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
     8→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod

    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

    12→
    13→def _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(
    14→    state: dict, *, threshold: float = 90.0
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Return subjective dimensions with score below threshold and evidence issues."""
    17→    low_dims: list[str] = []
    18→    dimension_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    19→    if not isinstance(dimension_scores, dict):
    20→        return low_dims
    21→
    22→    for dim_name, payload in dimension_scores.items():
    23→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    24→            continue
    25→        score = payload.get("score")
    26→        detectors = payload.get("detectors", {})
    27→        subjective_payload = (
    28→            detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    29→            if isinstance(detectors, dict)
    30→            else None
    31→        )
    32→        if not isinstance(subjective_payload, dict):
    33→            continue
    34→        issues = subjective_payload.get("issues", 0)
    35→        if not isinstance(score, int | float) or not isinstance(
    36→            issues, int | float
    37→        ):
    38→            continue
    39→        if score < threshold and issues > 0:
    40→            low_dims.append(str(dim_name))
    41→    return low_dims
    42→
    43→
    44→def _print_narrative_plan_fields(narrative: dict) -> None:
    45→    """Print additional narrative plan fields when present."""
    46→    why_now = narrative.get("why_now")
    47→    primary_action = narrative.get("primary_action")
    48→    verification_step = narrative.get("verification_step")
    49→    risk_flags = narrative.get("risk_flags")
    50→
    51→    has_plan = bool(why_now) or bool(primary_action) or bool(verification_step) or bool(
    52→        risk_flags
    53→    )
    54→    if not has_plan:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    print(colorize("  Narrative Plan:", "cyan"))
    58→    if isinstance(why_now, str) and why_now.strip():
    59→        print(colorize(f"    Why now: {why_now.strip()}", "dim"))
    60→
    61→    if isinstance(primary_action, dict):
    62→        command = primary_action.get("command")
    63→        description = primary_action.get("description")
    64→        if isinstance(command, str) and command.strip():
    65→            line = f"    Next: `{command.strip()}`"
    66→            if isinstance(description, str) and description.strip():
    67→                line += f" — {description.strip()}"
    68→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
    69→
    70→    if isinstance(verification_step, dict):
    71→        command = verification_step.get("command")
    72→        reason = verification_step.get("reason")
    73→        if isinstance(command, str) and command.strip():
    74→            line = f"    Verify: `{command.strip()}`"
    75→            if isinstance(reason, str) and reason.strip():
    76→                line += f" — {reason.strip()}"
    77→            print(colorize(line, "dim"))
    78→
    79→    if isinstance(risk_flags, list):
    80→        for risk in risk_flags:
    81→            if not isinstance(risk, dict):
    82→                continue
    83→            message = risk.get("message")
    84→            if not isinstance(message, str) or not message.strip():
    85→                continue
    86→            severity = str(risk.get("severity", "")).lower()
    87→            style = "yellow" if severity in {"high", "critical"} else "dim"
    88→            print(colorize(f"    Risk: {message.strip()}", style))
    89→    print()

    92→def _print_filtered_reminders(narrative: dict) -> None:
    93→    """Print narrative reminders except low-value report score reminders."""
    94→    reminders = narrative.get("reminders")
    95→    if not isinstance(reminders, list):
    96→        return
    97→    messages: list[str] = []
    98→    for reminder in reminders:
    99→        if not isinstance(reminder, dict):
   100→            continue
   101→        if reminder.get("type") == "report_scores":
   102→            continue
   103→        message = reminder.get("message")
   104→        if isinstance(message, str) and message.strip():
   105→            messages.append(message.strip())
   106→    if not messages:
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    print(colorize("  Reminders:", "dim"))
   110→    for message in messages:
   111→        print(colorize(f"    - {message}", "dim"))
   112→    print()

   115→def _show_subjective_score_nudge(state: dict) -> None:
   116→    """Nudge rerun when subjective dimensions are low with evidence issues."""
   117→    low_dims = _subjective_dimensions_below_threshold(state, threshold=90.0)
   118→    if not low_dims:

   146→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
   147→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   148→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
   149→        return {}
   150→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
   151→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:

   157→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
   158→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
   159→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
   160→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   161→        return []
   162→
   163→    warnings: list[str] = []
   164→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
   165→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   166→            continue
   167→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
   168→        confidence = coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   169→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   170→            continue
   171→
   172→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
   173→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
   174→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()

   177→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"

   180→        if remediation:

   182→        warnings.append(line)

   186→def show_post_scan_analysis(

   193→    """Print warnings, narrative headline, and top action. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
   194→    warnings = []

   202→        )

   210→        )

   226→        ),

   229→    # Show one actionable plan and optional strategy context.

   235→    )

   237→    hint = strategy.get("hint")
   238→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   239→
   240→    if actions:
   241→        top = actions[0]
   242→        print(
   243→            colorize(
   244→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}", "cyan"
   245→            )
   246→        )
   247→        if hint:
   248→            print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {hint}", "dim"))
   249→        print()
   250→    elif hint:
   251→        print(colorize(f"  Agent focus: {hint}", "cyan"))
   252→        print()
   253→
   254→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   255→        print()
   256→
   257→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   258→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   259→        print()
   260→
   261→    _print_narrative_plan_fields(narrative)
   262→    _print_filtered_reminders(narrative)
   263→    _show_subjective_score_nudge(state)
   264→
   265→    # Review findings nudge
   266→    scoped_findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   267→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   268→    )
   269→    open_review = [
   270→        finding
   271→        for finding in scoped_findings.values()
   272→        if finding["status"] == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   273→    ]
   274→    open_subjective, _subjective_reasons, holistic_reasons = (
   275→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped_findings)
   276→    )
   277→    holistic_open = sum(holistic_reasons.values())
   278→    if open_review:
   279→        suffix = "s" if len(open_review) != 1 else ""
   280→        print(

   283→                "cyan",
   284→            )
   285→        )
   286→        print()

   288→        if holistic_open > 0:

   296→        else:

   300→                    "`desloppify show subjective_review --status open`",
   301→                    "cyan",
   302→                )
   303→            )
   304→        print()
   305→
   306→    # Auto-queue: nudge subjective review for high-complexity unreviewed files
   307→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {}).get("files", {})
   308→    scoped = scoped_findings
   309→    complex_unreviewed = set()
   310→    for finding in scoped.values():
   311→        if (
   312→            finding.get("detector") in ("structural", "smells")
   313→            and finding.get("status") == "wontfix"
   314→            and finding.get("file") not in review_cache
   315→        ):
   316→            complex_unreviewed.add(finding.get("file"))
   317→    if len(complex_unreviewed) >= 3:
   318→        print(
   319→            colorize(
   320→                f"  {len(complex_unreviewed)} complex files have never been reviewed — "
   321→                "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   322→                "would provide actionable refactoring guidance",
   323→                "dim",
   324→            )
   325→        )
   326→        print()
   327→
   328→    return warnings, narrative
   329→
   330→
   331→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   332→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   333→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   334→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   335→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   336→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   337→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   338→    confidence_reduced = (
   339→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   340→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   344→        return
   345→
   346→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   347→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   348→    strict_gap = (
   349→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   350→    )
   351→
   352→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   353→    actionable = (
   354→        stats.get("open", 0)
   355→        + wontfix
   356→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   357→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   358→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   359→    )
   360→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   361→
   362→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   363→
   364→    if wontfix > 5:
   365→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   366→            style = "red"
   367→            msg = (
   368→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   369→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   370→            )
   371→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   372→            style = "yellow"
   373→            msg = (
   374→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   375→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   376→            )
   377→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   378→            style = "yellow"
   379→            msg = (
   380→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   381→                "pts below lenient"
   382→            )
   383→        else:
   384→            style = "dim"
   385→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   386→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   387→
   388→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   389→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   390→        if dim_scores:
   391→            gaps = []
   392→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   393→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   394→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   395→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   396→                if gap > 0:
   397→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   398→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   399→            if gaps:
   400→                top = gaps[:2]
   401→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   402→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   403→
   404→    if ignored > 0:
   405→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   406→        print(
   407→            colorize(
   408→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   409→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   410→                style,
   411→            )
   412→        )
   413→        print(
   414→            colorize(
   415→                "    Suppressed findings still count against strict and verified scores",
   416→                "dim",
   417→            )
   418→        )
   419→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:
   420→        print(
   421→            colorize(
   422→                f"  {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   423→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   424→                "dim",
   425→            )
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    if confidence_reduced:
   429→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   430→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   431→        confidence = coerce_confidence(
   432→            score_confidence.get("confidence"),
   433→            default=1.0,
   434→        )
   435→        dim_text = ""
   436→        if isinstance(impacted_dimensions, list) and impacted_dimensions:
   437→            preview = ", ".join(str(item) for item in impacted_dimensions[:3])
   438→            if len(impacted_dimensions) > 3:
   439→                preview += f", +{len(impacted_dimensions) - 3} more"
   440→            dim_text = f" (dimensions: {preview})"
   441→        print(
   442→            colorize(
   443→                f"  ⚠ Score confidence reduced to {confidence * 100:.0f}%{dim_text}",
   444→                "yellow",
   445→            )
   446→        )
   447→        if isinstance(detectors, list):
   448→            for detector in detectors[:3]:
   449→                if not isinstance(detector, dict):
   450→                    continue
   451→                summary = str(detector.get("summary", "")).strip()
   452→                remediation = str(detector.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   453→                if summary:
   454→                    print(colorize(f"    - {summary}", "dim"))
   455→                if remediation:
   456→                    print(colorize(f"      Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   457→
   458→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 55, "dim"))
   459→    print()
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = ["show_post_scan_analysis", "show_score_integrity"]
```

```
1→"""scan command: run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→

    58→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    62→    """Print the scan header line."""
    63→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    64→
    65→
    66→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    67→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    68→    lines = [
    69→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    70→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    71→    ]
    72→    print("\n".join(lines))
    73→
    74→
    75→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    76→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    77→    if not effective_include_slow:
    78→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    79→    if noise.budget_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    if noise.hidden_total:
    82→        global_label = (
    83→            f", {noise.global_noise_budget} global"
    84→            if noise.global_noise_budget > 0
    85→            else ""
    86→        )
    87→        print(
    88→            colorize(
    89→                f"  * Noise budget: {noise.noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
    90→                f"({noise.hidden_total} findings hidden in show output: "
    91→                f"{_format_hidden_by_detector(noise.hidden_by_detector)})",
    92→                "dim",
    93→            )
    94→        )
    95→
    96→
    97→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    98→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    99→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
   100→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
   101→        return
   102→
   103→    for entry in warnings:
   104→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   105→            continue
   106→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   107→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
   108→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   109→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   110→
   111→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   112→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   113→        if impact:
   114→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   115→        if remediation:
   116→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   117→
   118→
   119→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   120→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   121→    try:
   122→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   123→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   124→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   125→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   126→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   127→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   128→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   129→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   130→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   131→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   132→        runtime,
   133→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   134→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   135→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   136→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   137→    )
   138→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   139→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   140→        print(
   141→            colorize(
   142→                "  * Subjective reset "
   143→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   144→                "yellow",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   148→        print(
   149→            colorize(
   150→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   151→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   152→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   153→                "yellow",
   154→            )
   155→        )
   156→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   157→
   158→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   159→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   160→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   161→
   162→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   163→
   164→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   165→    show_score_delta(
   166→        runtime.state,
   167→        merge.prev_overall,
   168→        merge.prev_objective,
   169→        merge.prev_strict,
   170→        merge.prev_verified,
   171→    )
   172→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   173→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   174→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   175→
   176→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   177→
   178→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   179→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   180→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   181→    if new_dim_scores:
   182→        show_low_dimension_hints(new_dim_scores)
   183→        show_subjective_paths_section(
   184→            runtime.state,
   185→            new_dim_scores,
   186→            threshold=target_value,
   187→            target_strict_score=target_value,
   188→        )
   189→
   190→    show_score_integrity(runtime.state, merge.diff)
   191→    show_concern_count(runtime.state, lang_name=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None)
   192→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   193→        merge.diff,
   194→        runtime.state,
   195→        runtime.lang,
   196→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   197→    )
   198→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   199→
   200→    write_query(
   201→        build_scan_query_payload(
   202→            runtime.state,
   203→            runtime.config,
   204→            runtime.profile,
   205→            merge.diff,
   206→            warnings,
   207→            narrative,
   208→            merge,
   209→            noise,
   210→        ),
   211→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   215→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   216→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   217→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   218→        badge_path = badge_emit
   219→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   220→    auto_update_skill()
   221→
   222→
   223→__all__ = [
   224→    "cmd_scan",
   225→]
   226→
```

```
1→"""Subjective scan reporting: common helpers, integrity checks, and output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import coerce_target_score
    10→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class SubjectiveFollowup:
    19→    threshold: float
    20→    threshold_label: str
    21→    low_assessed: list[dict]
    22→    rendered: str
    23→    command: str
    24→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None
    25→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    26→
    27→
    28→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    29→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    30→    seen: set[str] = set()
    31→    ordered: list[str] = []
    32→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    33→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    34→            if key in seen:
    35→                continue
    36→            ordered.append(key)
    37→            seen.add(key)
    38→    return ",".join(ordered)
    39→
    40→
    41→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    42→    return ", ".join(
    43→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    44→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    49→    count = len(entries)
    50→    names = ", ".join(
    51→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    52→    )
    53→    if count > max_names:
    54→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    55→    return names
    56→
    57→
    58→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    59→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    60→        return 0
    61→    if isinstance(value, int):
    62→        return value
    63→    if isinstance(value, float):
    64→        return int(value)
    65→    if isinstance(value, str):
    66→        try:
    67→            return int(value.strip())
    68→        except ValueError:
    69→            return 0
    70→    return 0
    71→
    72→
    73→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    74→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    75→        return []
    76→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    77→
    78→
    79→def subtract_reason_counts(
    80→    total_counts: dict[str, int],
    81→    scoped_counts: dict[str, int],
    82→) -> dict[str, int]:
    83→    """Subtract scoped reason counters from global counters."""
    84→    remainder: dict[str, int] = {}
    85→    for reason, total in total_counts.items():
    86→        delta = int(total) - int(scoped_counts.get(reason, 0))
    87→        if delta > 0:
    88→            remainder[reason] = delta
    89→    return remainder
    90→
    91→
    92→def subjective_rerun_command(
    93→    items: list[dict],
    94→    *,
    95→    max_items: int = 5,
    96→    refresh: bool = True,
    97→) -> str:
    98→    _ = refresh
    99→    dim_keys = flatten_cli_keys(items, max_items=max_items)
   100→    command_parts = [
   101→        "desloppify",
   102→        "review",
   103→        "--run-batches",
   104→        "--runner",
   105→        "codex",
   106→        "--parallel",
   107→        "--scan-after-import",
   108→    ]
   109→    if dim_keys:
   110→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   111→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   120→    return scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   121→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   126→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   127→) -> list[dict]:
   128→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   129→    for entry in entries:
   130→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   131→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   132→
   133→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   134→    for key in dimension_keys:
   135→        if key in by_key:
   136→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   137→            continue
   138→        mapped.append(
   139→            {
   140→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   141→                "score": 0.0,
   142→                "strict": 0.0,
   143→                "issues": 0,
   144→                "placeholder": False,
   145→                "cli_keys": [key],
   146→            }
   147→        )
   148→    return mapped
   149→
   150→
   151→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   154→    *,
   155→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   156→    max_items: int = 5,
   157→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   158→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   159→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   160→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   161→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   162→    )
   163→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   164→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   165→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   166→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   167→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   168→
   169→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   170→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   171→            reset_keys, subjective_entries
   172→        )
   173→        return {
   174→            "status": "penalized",
   175→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   176→            "target": target_display,
   177→            "entries": reset_entries,
   178→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   179→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   180→        }
   181→
   182→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   183→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   184→            matched_keys, subjective_entries
   185→        )
   186→        return {
   187→            "status": "warn",
   188→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   189→            "target": target_display,
   190→            "entries": matched_entries,
   191→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   192→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   193→        }
   194→
   195→    at_target = sorted(
   196→        [
   197→            entry
   198→            for entry in subjective_entries
   199→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   200→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   201→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   202→                threshold_value,
   203→            )
   204→        ],
   205→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   206→    )
   207→    if not at_target:
   208→        return None
   209→
   210→    return {
   211→        "status": "at_target",
   212→        "count": len(at_target),
   213→        "target": threshold_value,
   214→        "entries": at_target,
   215→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   216→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   217→    }
   218→
   219→
   220→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   221→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   222→    *,
   223→    fallback_target: float = 95.0,
   224→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   225→    if not integrity_notice:
   226→        return []
   227→
   228→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   229→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   230→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   231→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   232→        fallback=fallback_target,
   233→    )
   234→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   235→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   236→
   237→    if status == "penalized":
   238→        return [
   239→            (
   240→                "red",
   241→                "WARNING: "
   242→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   243→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   244→            ),
   245→            (
   246→                "yellow",
   247→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   248→            ),
   249→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   250→        ]
   251→
   252→    if status == "warn":
   253→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   254→        return [
   255→            (
   256→                "yellow",
   257→                "WARNING: "
   258→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   259→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   260→            ),
   261→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   262→        ]
   263→
   264→    if status == "at_target":
   265→        return [
   266→            (
   267→                "yellow",
   268→                "WARNING: "
   269→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   270→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   271→            ),
   272→        ]
   273→
   274→    return []
   275→
   276→
   277→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   278→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   279→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   280→
   281→
   282→def _subjective_reset_command(state: dict) -> str:
   283→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path")
   284→    if not isinstance(scan_path, str) or not scan_path.strip():
   285→        scan_path = "."
   286→    return f"`desloppify scan --path {scan_path} --reset-subjective`"
   287→
   288→
   289→def build_subjective_followup(
   290→    state: dict,
   291→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   292→    *,
   293→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   294→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   295→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   296→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   297→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   298→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   299→    low_assessed = sorted(
   300→        [
   301→            entry
   302→            for entry in subjective_entries
   303→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   304→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   305→        ],
   306→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   307→    )
   308→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   309→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   310→        low_assessed,
   311→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   312→    )
   313→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   314→        state,
   315→        subjective_entries,
   316→        threshold=threshold_value,
   317→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   318→    )
   319→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   320→        integrity_notice,
   321→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   322→    )
   323→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   324→        threshold=threshold_value,
   325→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   326→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   327→        rendered=rendered,
   328→        command=command,
   329→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   330→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→
   334→def show_subjective_paths(
   335→    state: dict,
   336→    dim_scores: dict,
   337→    *,
   338→    colorize_fn,
   339→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   340→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   341→    target_strict_score: float | None = None,
   342→) -> None:
   343→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   344→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   345→    if not subjective_entries:
   346→        return
   347→
   348→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   349→        state,
   350→        subjective_entries,
   351→        threshold=threshold_value,
   352→        max_quality_items=3,
   353→        max_integrity_items=5,
   354→    )
   355→    unassessed = sorted(
   356→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   357→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   358→    )
   359→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   360→
   361→    all_findings = state.get("findings", {})
   362→    if not isinstance(all_findings, dict):
   363→        all_findings = {}
   364→    scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(all_findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   365→    coverage_in_scope, reason_counts_in_scope, holistic_reason_counts_in_scope = (
   366→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped)
   367→    )
   368→    coverage_global, reason_counts_global, holistic_reason_counts_global = (
   369→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_findings)
   370→    )
   371→    if not all_findings and coverage_global == 0 and coverage_in_scope > 0:
   372→        # Test/mocked states may supply only scoped findings.
   373→        coverage_global = coverage_in_scope
   374→        reason_counts_global = dict(reason_counts_in_scope)
   375→        holistic_reason_counts_global = dict(holistic_reason_counts_in_scope)
   376→    coverage_out_of_scope = max(coverage_global - coverage_in_scope, 0)
   377→    reason_counts_out_of_scope = subtract_reason_counts(
   378→        reason_counts_global,
   379→        reason_counts_in_scope,
   380→    )
   381→    holistic_in_scope = sum(holistic_reason_counts_in_scope.values())
   382→    holistic_global = sum(holistic_reason_counts_global.values())
   383→    holistic_out_of_scope = max(holistic_global - holistic_in_scope, 0)
   384→    if (

   386→        and not low_assessed
   387→        and coverage_global <= 0
   388→        and not followup.integrity_notice

   390→        return

   392→    print(colorize_fn("  Subjective path:", "cyan"))
   393→    print(

   399→    if target_strict_score is not None:

   415→                    )
   416→                )
   417→
   418→    if unassessed or holistic_global > 0:

   422→        if holistic_global > 0:
```

```
   190→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:
   191→    """Attempt to auto-install or auto-update the skill document.
   192→
   193→    Best-effort: swallows all exceptions so a network failure or permission
   194→    error never breaks the scan.
   195→    """
   196→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   197→
   198→    if install and not install.stale:
   199→        return  # Up to date.
   200→
   201→    try:
   202→        from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import (
   203→            resolve_interface,
   204→            update_installed_skill,
   205→        )
   206→
   207→        if install:
   208→            interface = resolve_interface(install=install)
   209→        else:
   210→            interface = _detect_agent_interface()
   211→
   212→        if interface:
   213→            update_installed_skill(interface)
   214→    except (ImportError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
   215→        logger.debug("Skill auto-update skipped: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   216→
   217→
   218→def _print_badge_hint(badge_path: Path | None) -> None:
   219→    if not (badge_path and badge_path.exists()):
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   223→    print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")
   224→    print("Let the user know they can view it, and suggest adding it")
   225→    print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`')
   226→
   227→
   228→def _print_llm_summary(
   229→    state: dict,
   230→    badge_path: Path | None,
   231→    narrative: dict | None = None,
   232→    diff: dict | None = None,
   233→):
   234→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   235→
   236→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   237→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   238→    Only shown when running inside an agent (CLAUDE_CODE or DESLOPPIFY_AGENT env).
   239→    """
   240→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   241→        return
   242→
   243→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   244→    scores = _load_scores(state)
   245→
   246→    if (
   247→        scores.overall is None
   248→        and scores.objective is None
   249→        and scores.strict is None
   250→        and scores.verified is None
   251→        and not dim_scores
   252→    ):

   254→
   255→    print("─" * 60)
   256→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   257→    print("IMPORTANT: ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan.")
   258→    print("Show overall health (lenient + strict), ALL dimension scores,")
   259→    print("AND all subjective dimension scores in a markdown table.")
   260→    print("The goal is to maximize strict scores. Never skip the scores.\n")

   262→    _print_score_lines(
   263→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   264→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   265→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   266→        verified_score=scores.verified,

   274→    )
   275→    _print_workflow_guide()
   276→    _print_narrative_status(narrative)
   277→    _print_badge_hint(badge_path)
   278→    print("─" * 60)
   279→
   280→
   281→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
   282→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if we detect an agent.
   283→
   284→    Called unconditionally from the scan workflow — not gated on scores.
   285→    """

   289→    _try_auto_update_skill()
```

```
1→"""Text templates for scan reporting output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from textwrap import dedent
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_workflow_guide(attest_example: str) -> str:
     9→    """Render the scan workflow guide with current attestation text."""
    10→    return dedent(
    11→        f"""
    12→        ## Workflow Guide
    13→
    14→        1. **Review findings first** (if any): `desloppify show review --status open` — high-value subjective findings
    15→        2. **Run auto-fixers** (if available): `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply
    16→        3. **Manual fixes**: `desloppify next` — highest-priority item. Fix it, then:
    17→           `desloppify resolve fixed "<id>" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    18→           Required attestation keywords: 'I have actually' and 'not gaming'.
    19→        4. **Rescan**: `desloppify scan --path <path>` — verify improvements, catch cascading effects
    20→        5. **Reset subjective baseline when needed**:
    21→           `desloppify scan --path <path> --reset-subjective` (then run a fresh review/import cycle)
    22→        6. **Check progress**: `desloppify status` — dimension scores dashboard
    23→
    24→        ### Decision Guide
    25→        - **Tackle**: T1/T2 (high impact), auto-fixable, security findings
    26→        - **Consider skipping**: T4 low-confidence, test/config zone findings (lower impact)
    27→        - **Wontfix**: Intentional patterns, false positives →
    28→          `desloppify resolve wontfix "<id>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    29→        - **Batch wontfix**: Multiple intentional patterns →
    30→          `desloppify resolve wontfix "<detector>::*::<category>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    31→
    32→        ### Understanding Dimensions
    33→        - **Mechanical** (File health, Code quality, etc.): Fix code → rescan
    34→        - **Subjective** (Naming quality, Logic clarity, etc.): Address review findings → re-review
    35→        - **Health vs Strict**: Health ignores wontfix; Strict penalizes it. Focus on Strict.
    36→        """
    37→    ).strip()
    38→
    39→
    40→__all__ = ["build_workflow_guide"]
    41→
```

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py`

The `cmd_next` function (`_get_items` specifically) is the main entry. For terminal output, it calls these rendering functions in order (lines 144-165):

1. `render_queue_header(queue, explain)` -- tier navigator + optional fallback reason
2. `show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score)` -- if empty queue, prints "Nothing to do!" and returns early
3. `render_terminal_items(items, ...)` -- renders each queue item
4. `render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)` -- resolution commands for a single item
5. **`render_followup_nudges(state, dim_scores, findings_scoped, ...)`** -- the followup nudges
6. A final `print()` for spacing

### Does `output.py` control when nudges are shown?

No. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/output.py` is purely about **serialization and non-terminal output** (JSON payloads, markdown rendering, writing to files). It has no role in controlling when nudges appear. Nudges are only shown for the `"terminal"` output format -- the code returns early (line 141-142 of `next.py`) for JSON/markdown output before ever reaching `render_followup_nudges`.

### Complete anatomy of `render_followup_nudges`

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`, lines 258-385.

The function takes `state`, `dim_scores`, `findings_scoped`, `strict_score`, and `target_strict_score`. Here are ALL the possible nudge blocks it can emit, in order:

---

**Nudge Block 1: "North star" strict score progress** (lines 277-292)

- **Condition**: `strict_score is not None`
- **Two sub-variants**:
  - If `gap > 0` (strict score below target): prints cyan `"North star: strict X/100 -> target Y (+Z needed)"`
  - If `gap <= 0` (target met): prints green `"North star: strict X/100 meets target Y"`
- **Always appears** when strict_score is computed (which it will be after any completed scan).

---

**Nudge Block 2: "Subjective quality followup"** (line 293)

- **Condition**: Delegated to `print_subjective_followup(followup, ...)`. This prints:
  - **2a**: If `followup.low_assessed` is truthy (there are assessed subjective dimensions below the target strict score threshold): prints cyan `"Subjective quality (<X%): dim1 Y%, dim2 Z%"` plus `"Next command to improve subjective scores: ..."`
  - **2b**: If `followup.integrity_lines` is non-empty (subjective integrity notice -- penalized, warn, or at_target status): prints those integrity warning lines (red/yellow/dim depending on severity)
- The `SubjectiveFollowup` dataclass is built by `build_subjective_followup()` in `scan_reporting_subjective.py`. It checks for assessed dimensions below threshold and integrity state (penalized/warn/at_target).

---

**Nudge Block 3: "Subjective integrity gap"** (lines 299-322)

- **Condition**: `unassessed_subjective` is non-empty (dimensions with 0% placeholder scores that have never been assessed) **OR** `holistic_open > 0` (holistic review signals that are stale or missing)
- **Prints**: yellow `"Subjective integrity gap: unassessed subjective dimensions + holistic review stale/missing"` plus dim instructions to run `review --prepare` and rescan
- **Also prints**: If `unassessed_subjective` exists, lists the first 3 unassessed dimension names

---

**Nudge Block 4: "Subjective bottleneck"** (lines 324-335)

- **Condition**: `low_assessed` is truthy (from Block 2's followup) **AND** `open_review` is non-empty (there are open findings from the `"review"` detector)
- **Prints**: yellow `"Subjective bottleneck: prioritize investigating open review findings to move elegance scores."`
- This nudge appears only when both conditions are met simultaneously.

---

**Nudge Block 5: "Subjective coverage debt"** (lines 337-362)

- **Condition**: `coverage_open > 0` (there are open subjective_review findings -- files that are changed or unreviewed)
- **Prints**: cyan `"Subjective coverage debt: N files (M changed, K unreviewed)"`
- **Sub-nudge 5a**: If `holistic_open > 0`, also prints yellow `"Includes N holistic stale/missing signal(s)."`
- **Prints**: dim triage command `"desloppify show subjective_review --status open"`

---

**Nudge Block 6: "Also: open review findings"** (lines 364-384)

- **Two branches** (mutually exclusive):
  - **6a**: If `open_review` is truthy (open findings with `detector == "review"`): prints cyan `"Also: N review findings open (M uninvestigated). Run desloppify show review --status open."`
  - **6b**: Elif `low_assessed` OR `unassessed_subjective` OR `coverage_open > 0` (any subjective concern but no open review findings): prints dim `"Then import review output and rerun desloppify scan to refresh subjective scores."`

---

### How many can appear simultaneously?

**All 6 blocks can appear at the same time.** There is no budget limiting or deduplication mechanism. Each block has an independent condition. A worst-case scenario where all fire simultaneously:

1. North star -- always fires when strict_score exists
2. Subjective quality -- fires when assessed subjective dims are below threshold
3. Subjective integrity gap -- fires when unassessed dims exist or holistic signals are stale
4. Subjective bottleneck -- fires when both low_assessed and open_review exist
5. Subjective coverage debt -- fires when coverage_open > 0
6. Open review findings -- fires when open_review exists (or else the fallback message fires when any subjective concern exists)

The only mutual exclusion is between 6a and 6b (they are an if/elif chain). Everything else is additive.

For a project that has:
- A computed strict score (almost always)
- Assessed subjective dimensions below target
- Unassessed dimensions (placeholder 0%)
- Holistic review stale signals
- Open review findings
- Open subjective_review findings (changed/unreviewed files)

...all 6 blocks will print. That is potentially a LOT of nudge text.

---

## 2. `fix review` vs `review --prepare`

### `fix review` (the `_cmd_fix_review` path)

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/fix/cmd.py`

When `fixer_name == "review"`, `cmd_fix` immediately delegates to `_cmd_fix_review(args)` (line 27-28) and returns. No fixer loading, no detection, no application flow.

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/fix/review_flow.py`

`_cmd_fix_review` calls **`review_mod.prepare_review()`** -- note: this is the **per-file review** preparation function. The key call is at line 36:

```python
data = review_mod.prepare_review(
    path,
    lang_run,
    state,
    options=review_mod.ReviewPrepareOptions(files=found_files or None),
)
```

It then:
- If 0 candidates: prints "All production files have been reviewed" with optional integrity gap diagnostics
- If candidates exist: prints dimension prompts, language guidance, and writes to `query.json`
- Prints an "AGENT PLAN" with instructions to read query.json, evaluate files, save findings, etc.

### `review --prepare` (the `do_prepare` path)

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/entrypoint.py`

When no mode flag is set (no `--import`, `--run-batches`, `--merge`, `--external-start`, `--external-submit`, `--validate-import`), the entrypoint falls through to `do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)` at line 143.

File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prepare.py`

`do_prepare` calls **`review_mod.prepare_holistic_review()`** -- note: this is the **holistic review** preparation function. The key call is at line 64:

```python
data = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
    path,
    lang_run,
    state,
    options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
        dimensions=dimensions,
        files=found_files or None,
        max_files_per_batch=...,
        include_issue_history=retrospective,
        ...
    ),
)
```

### The actual distinction

**They are different things calling different underlying preparation functions:**

| Aspect | `fix review` | `review --prepare` |
|---|---|---|
| Underlying function | `review_mod.prepare_review()` | `review_mod.prepare_holistic_review()` |
| Review type | **Per-file review** -- evaluates individual files against dimension prompts | **Holistic review** -- evaluates the entire codebase architecture, organized into investigation batches |
| Options class | `ReviewPrepareOptions` | `HolisticReviewPrepareOptions` (supports `dimensions`, `max_files_per_batch`, `retrospective`) |
| Output format | Dimension prompts listed individually, agent instructed to evaluate each file | Investigation batches listed, workflow steps, preferred next command is `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` |
| Retrospective support | No | Yes (`--retrospective` flag injects issue history) |
| Batch partitioning | No | Yes (`max_files_per_batch` config) |
| Narrative context | Not computed | Computed with `command="review"` |

**Why do both exist?** `fix review` is the older, simpler per-file review path -- it prepares a packet where an agent evaluates individual files against dimension criteria. `review --prepare` is the newer holistic review path designed for the batch runner pipeline (`--run-batches`). The holistic approach evaluates architectural patterns across the codebase rather than file-by-file, and produces investigation batches that can be run in parallel by automated runners (Codex, Claude).

`fix review` is essentially a legacy/manual path. `review --prepare` is the recommended automation-friendly path that the scan output and nudges consistently point users toward.

---

## 3. The workflow guide and first-scan experience

### What a user sees after their first scan

The full scan output sequence is in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py`, `cmd_scan()`, lines 119-221. Here is the order:

1. **Scan header**: `"Desloppify Scan (python)"` (or whatever language)
2. **Subjective reset notice** (if `--reset-subjective` was passed)
3. **Expired manual-override notice** (if any provisional overrides expired)
4. **Coverage preflight warnings** (if detector coverage is reduced)
5. **Scan execution** (generate findings, merge into state)
6. **"Scan complete" banner**
7. **Diff summary**: `"+N new / -M resolved / K reopened"` -- on first scan this will just show "+N new"
8. **Score delta**: All four scores (overall, objective, strict, verified) with deltas from previous scan (on first scan, no deltas)
9. **Scan visibility hints**: Fast scan warning, noise budget info
10. **Scorecard subjective measures** (`show_scorecard_subjective_measures`)
11. **Score model breakdown** (`show_score_model_breakdown`)
12. **Dimension deltas** (only if both new and previous dim scores exist -- so NOT on first scan)
13. **Low dimension hints** (if dim scores exist and any are low)
14. **Subjective paths section** (the detailed subjective guidance block from `scan_reporting_subjective.py`)
15. **Score integrity** (`show_score_integrity` -- wontfix debt, ignore patterns, confidence warnings; gated on thresholds so may not appear on first scan)
16. **Concern count** (if design concerns exist)
17. **Post-scan analysis** (`show_post_scan_analysis` from `scan_reporting_analysis.py`) -- this is the big one with warnings, narrative, AGENT PLAN, and all the review nudges
18. **Query.json written**
19. **Scorecard badge emitted**
20. **LLM summary** (`_print_llm_summary` -- only in agent environments)

### Does the workflow guide always appear?

**No. The workflow guide is gated on being in an agent environment.**

The workflow guide lives in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_text.py` as the `build_workflow_guide()` function. It is consumed by `_print_llm_summary()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py`.

`_print_llm_summary` (line 228-278) has two gates:

1. **Line 240**: `if not _is_agent_environment(): return` -- checks for env vars `CLAUDE_CODE`, `DESLOPPIFY_AGENT`, `GEMINI_CLI`, `CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED`, `CODEX_SANDBOX`, `CURSOR_TRACE_ID`. If none are set, the entire LLM summary (including the workflow guide) is skipped.

2. **Lines 246-252**: If all four scores are None and dim_scores is empty, it returns early (this handles the edge case of a scan with no detectors producing no scores).

So the workflow guide is **only printed for agent environments**, and only when scores/dimensions exist. A human user running `desloppify scan` directly in their terminal will never see the workflow guide.

### Is the workflow guide gated on whether review findings exist?

**No.** The workflow guide is unconditional once you pass the agent-environment check. It always prints the same static text (the "## Workflow Guide" template from `scan_reporting_text.py`). It does not check for review findings, open findings, or any state.

### What IS gated on review findings in the scan output?

The **post-scan analysis** (`show_post_scan_analysis` in `scan_reporting_analysis.py`) contains several review-finding-dependent nudges:

- **"Review: N findings pending"** (line 278-286): Only printed if `open_review` is non-empty (detector == "review")
- **"Subjective integrity: N holistic stale/missing signals"** (line 287-303): Only if `open_subjective > 0`
- **"N complex files have never been reviewed"** (line 306-326): Only if 3+ complex files lack review cache entries
- **"Subjective scores below 90"** nudge (line 263, calling `_show_subjective_score_nudge`): Gated on subjective dimensions with score < 90 and issues > 0

The **subjective paths section** (from `show_subjective_paths` in `scan_reporting_subjective.py`) is also review-aware -- it shows unassessed dimensions, coverage debt, holistic stale signals, and integrity notices. But this section appears whenever there are dimension scores, regardless of whether a review has been run.

### First-scan experience summary

On a **first scan** (no previous state), a user will see:
- The scan header and "Scan complete" banner
- Diff summary showing all findings as new
- All four scores (first-time baseline)
- Dimension scores if detectors ran
- Subjective path guidance (with all dimensions unassessed at 0% placeholder)
- Post-scan analysis with narrative AGENT PLAN
- Review nudges about unassessed dimensions and holistic gaps
- The workflow guide (only if running inside an agent like Claude Code)

The first scan will have heavy subjective nudging because all subjective dimensions start as unassessed placeholders at 0%, triggering the "integrity gap" and "unassessed" nudges throughout.
