## Problem 1: No single way to see all subjective work

Users need three different commands to see subjective work:
- `show review --status open` — review findings (detector=review)
- `show subjective_review --status open` — coverage debt (detector=subjective_review)  
- `next --tier 4` — T4 items including synthetic subjective dimension items

**Root cause**: "subjective" is a cross-cutting concept spanning multiple detector types and item kinds, but the `show` command filters by exact detector name.

**Existing pattern**: `show concerns` is already a special keyword in `cmd_show()` that renders a custom view. At line 168: `if pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns": _show_concerns(...)`.

**Proposed solution**: Add `show subjective` as a special keyword (same pattern as `concerns`) that renders a **subjective dashboard**:

```
  Subjective Quality
  ─────────────────────────────────
  Dimensions:
    Naming quality         72.5%  (3 review findings open)
    Logic clarity          88.0%  (1 review finding open)
    Mid elegance           [unassessed]
    High elegance          [unassessed]

  Open Review Findings (4):
    [1] [high] processData is too vague
        review::src/foo.ts::naming_quality::processData
    [2] [medium] handleData doesn't indicate return type
        review::src/foo.ts::naming_quality::handleData
    ...

  Coverage Debt: 12 files (8 changed, 4 unreviewed)

  Actions:
    Investigate findings:   desloppify show <finding-id>
    Resolve a finding:      desloppify resolve fixed "<id>" --note "..."
    Run new review:         desloppify review --prepare
    Triage coverage:        desloppify show subjective_review --status open
```

Key design decisions:
- The dashboard shows dimension scores, findings list, coverage summary, and actions — everything in one place
- Writes to query.json with full structured data for agent consumption
- Uses existing functions: `scorecard_subjective_entries()` for dimensions, `unassessed_subjective_dimensions()` for gaps, `subjective_review_open_breakdown()` for coverage
- Files: `desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`

## Problem 2: `fix review` vs `review --prepare` confusion

**Current state**: 
- `fix review` calls `prepare_review()` → per-file review preparation
- `review --prepare` calls `prepare_holistic_review()` → holistic batch review
- These are genuinely different operations
- BUT: the action engine (action_engine.py line 184) suggests `fix review` for `subjective_review` findings, while EVERYWHERE else suggests `review --prepare`

**Root cause**: Inconsistent suggestions. Both commands exist for valid reasons, but `fix review` is the older per-file path and `review --prepare` is the recommended holistic path.

**Solution**: Change the action engine to suggest `review --prepare` instead of `fix review`. Leave `fix review` working but don't actively suggest it in guidance. This makes ALL guidance consistent.

File: `desloppify/intelligence/narrative/action_engine.py` line 184

## Problem 3: `next` followup nudges too chatty

**Current state**: `render_followup_nudges()` (next_parts/render.py lines 258-385) has 6 independent nudge blocks:
1. North star strict score (1 line) — always shows
2. Subjective quality followup (2+ lines) — low dimensions below target
3. Subjective integrity gap (3+ lines) — unassessed dimensions, holistic stale
4. Subjective bottleneck (1 line) — both low dims AND open review findings
5. Subjective coverage debt (2+ lines) — files changed/unreviewed
6. Open review findings (1-2 lines) — review findings to investigate

ALL can fire simultaneously. No budget. No deduplication.

**Root cause**: Blocks 2-6 are all variations of "subjective system needs attention" but each checks its own condition independently.

**Solution**: Keep the north star (it's one informative line). Collapse blocks 2-6 into a single summary line pointing to `show subjective`:

```python
# Build a summary of all subjective concerns
parts = []
if low_dims: parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimensions below target")
if unassessed: parts.append(f"{unassessed} unassessed")
if open_review_count: parts.append(f"{open_review_count} review findings open")
if coverage_count: parts.append(f"{coverage_count} files need review")

if parts:
    print(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.")
    print("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.")
```

15+ lines → 2 lines. The detail lives in `show subjective`, not in `next`.

File: `desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`, `render_followup_nudges()` function

## Problem 4: Workflow guide order

**Current state** (scan_reporting_text.py):
1. Review findings first → `show review --status open`
2. Run auto-fixers → `fix <fixer> --dry-run`
3. Manual fixes → `next` + `resolve`
4. Rescan
5. Reset subjective baseline
6. Check progress → `status`

**Root cause**: Step 1 is irrelevant on first scan (no review findings exist yet). The guide is static — it doesn't adapt to state.

**Solution**: Reorder to put universal steps first:
1. Run auto-fixers (always applicable if findings exist)
2. Manual fixes: `next` → `resolve`
3. Rescan to verify
4. Subjective review: `review --prepare` → import → `show subjective`
5. Reset subjective baseline (when needed)
6. Check progress: `status`

The "Review findings" step becomes step 4, contextualized as part of the subjective review workflow rather than step 1.

File: `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_text.py`

   150→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""

   152→    state = runtime.state
   153→    config = runtime.config
   154→
   155→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   159→    if stale_warning:
   160→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   161→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   162→    if skill_warning:
   163→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   164→
   165→    # Handle "show concerns" as a special view.
   166→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   167→    notes_file = getattr(args, "notes", None)
   168→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   169→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
   170→        _show_concerns(state, lang.name if lang else None)
   171→        return
   172→
   173→    # Numeric shorthand: look up from last query output.
   174→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().isdigit():
   175→        looked_up = _lookup_from_last_query(int(pattern_raw.strip()))
   176→        if looked_up:
   177→            pattern_raw = looked_up
   178→            # Rewrite args.pattern so downstream sees the resolved ID.
   179→            args.pattern = pattern_raw
   180→
   181→    # Single-finding detail mode.
   182→    if pattern_raw and "::" in pattern_raw:
   183→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
   184→        _show_single_finding(
   185→            state,
   186→            runtime,
   187→            pattern_raw,
   188→            lang.name if lang else None,
   189→            notes_file,
   190→        )
   191→        return
   192→
   193→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   194→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   195→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   196→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   197→        return
   198→
   199→    matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
   200→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   201→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   202→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   203→        state,
   204→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   205→    )
   206→
   207→    if not matches:
   208→        print(colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow"))
   209→        write_query(
   210→            {
   211→                "command": "show",
   212→                "query": pattern,
   213→                "status_filter": status_filter,
   214→                "total": 0,
   215→                "findings": [],
   216→                "narrative": narrative,
   217→            }
   218→        )
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    (
   222→        surfaced_matches,
   223→        hidden_by_detector,
   224→        noise_budget,
   225→        global_noise_budget,
   226→        budget_warning,
   227→    ) = resolve_noise(
   228→        config,
   229→        matches,
   230→    )
   231→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   232→
   233→    payload = build_show_payload(
   234→        surfaced_matches,
   235→        pattern,
   236→        status_filter,
   237→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   238→            total_matches=len(matches),
   239→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   240→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   241→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   242→        ),
   243→    )
   244→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   245→
   246→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   247→    if output_file:
   248→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   249→            return
   250→        raise SystemExit(1)
   251→
   252→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   253→    render_findings(
   254→        surfaced_matches,
   255→        pattern=pattern,
   256→        status_filter=status_filter,
   257→        show_code=show_code,
   258→        top=top,
   259→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   260→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   261→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   262→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   263→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   264→    )
   265→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches)
   266→    show_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   269→    )
   270→
   271→
   272→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   273→

   341→        if coverage_reasons.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:

   361→            )

1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(followup: SubjectiveFollowup, *, leading_newline: bool = False) -> bool:
    21→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    22→
    23→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    24→    """
    25→    printed = False
    26→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    27→    if followup.low_assessed:
    28→        print(
    29→            colorize(
    30→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    31→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    32→                "cyan",
    33→            )
    34→        )
    35→        print(
    36→            colorize(
    37→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    38→                "dim",
    39→            )
    40→        )
    41→        printed = True
    42→
    43→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    44→        if printed:
    45→            print()
    46→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    47→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    48→        printed = True
    49→    return printed
    50→
    51→
    52→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]

1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import _format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    18→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    19→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    21→        return 0
    22→
    23→    streak = 0
    24→    for entry in reversed(history):
    25→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    26→            break
    27→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    28→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    29→            break
    30→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    31→            break
    32→        streak += 1
    33→    return streak
    34→
    35→
    36→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    37→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    38→    diff_parts = []
    39→    if diff["new"]:
    40→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    41→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    42→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    43→    if diff["reopened"]:
    44→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    45→    if diff_parts:
    46→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    49→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    50→        print(
    51→            colorize(
    52→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    53→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    54→                "yellow",
    55→            )
    56→        )
    57→
    58→
    59→def show_score_delta(
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    65→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    66→):
    67→    """Print the canonical score trio with deltas."""
    68→    stats = state["stats"]
    69→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    70→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    71→    scoped_open = int(stats.get("open", 0) or 0)
    72→    out_of_scope_open = 0
    73→    global_open = scoped_open
    74→    if isinstance(findings, dict) and findings:
    75→        scope_counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    76→        scoped_open = int(scope_counts.get("in_scope", scoped_open) or 0)
    77→        out_of_scope_open = int(scope_counts.get("out_of_scope", 0) or 0)
    78→        global_open = int(scope_counts.get("global", scoped_open) or 0)
    79→
    80→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
    81→    wontfix_str = f" · {wontfix} wontfix" if wontfix else ""
    82→
    83→    if (
    84→        new.overall is None
    85→        or new.objective is None
    86→        or new.strict is None
    87→        or new.verified is None
    88→    ):
    89→        print(
    90→            colorize(
    91→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            )
    94→        )
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = _format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    98→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = _format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    99→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = _format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
   100→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = _format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
   101→    print(
   102→        "  Scores: "
   103→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
   104→        + colorize(
   105→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
   106→            objective_color,
   107→        )
   108→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   109→        + colorize(
   110→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   111→            verified_color,
   112→        )
   113→        + colorize(
   114→            "  |  "
   115→            f"open (in-scope): {scoped_open} · "
   116→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {out_of_scope_open} · "
   117→            f"open (global): {global_open}"
   118→            f"{wontfix_str} / {stats['total']} in-scope total",
   119→            "dim",
   120→        )
   121→    )
   122→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   123→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   124→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   125→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   126→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   127→        print(
   128→            colorize(
   129→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   130→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   131→                "yellow",
   132→            )
   133→        )
   134→
   135→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   136→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   137→        status = integrity.get("status")
   138→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   139→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   140→        if status == "penalized":
   141→            print(
   142→                colorize(
   143→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   144→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   145→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   146→                    "red",
   147→                )
   148→            )
   149→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   150→            if streak >= 2:
   151→                print(
   152→                    colorize(
   153→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   154→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   155→                        "yellow",
   156→                    )
   157→                )
   158→        elif status == "warn":
   159→            print(
   160→                colorize(
   161→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   162→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   163→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   164→                    "yellow",
   165→                )
   166→            )
   167→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   168→            if streak >= 2:
   169→                print(
   170→                    colorize(
   171→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   172→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   173→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   174→                        "yellow",
   175→                )
   176→            )
   177→
   178→
   179→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   180→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   183→
   184→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   185→        if concerns:
   186→            print(

   188→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   189→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   190→                    "cyan",
   191→                )
   192→            )
   193→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   194→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   195→
   196→
   197→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   198→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   199→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   200→    for message, style in lines:

   202→    return target, gap

1→"""Lightweight integrity helpers for subjective/review scoring.
     2→
     3→This module intentionally lives outside ``desloppify.intelligence.review`` so command/state
     4→paths can import it without loading the heavier review package.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
    10→
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
    13→    matches_target_score,
    14→)
    15→
    16→__all__ = [
    17→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
    18→    "is_holistic_subjective_finding",
    19→    "is_subjective_review_open",
    20→    "matches_target_score",
    21→    "subjective_review_open_breakdown",
    22→    "unassessed_subjective_dimensions",
    23→]
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# Internal iteration helper
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→
    31→def _iter_findings(
    32→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    33→) -> Iterable[tuple[str, dict]]:
    34→    """Yield (finding_id, finding) pairs from mapping or iterable inputs."""
    35→    if isinstance(findings, Mapping):
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
    37→            if isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                yield str(finding_id), finding
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    for index, finding in enumerate(findings):
    42→        if isinstance(finding, dict):
    43→            yield str(index), finding
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Public helpers (formerly in integrity/review.py)
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_subjective_review_open(finding: dict) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when a finding is an open subjective-review signal."""
    53→    return (
    54→        finding.get("status") == "open"
    55→        and finding.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding: dict, *, finding_id: str = "") -> bool:
    60→    """Best-effort check for holistic subjective-review coverage findings."""
    61→    candidate_id = str(finding.get("id") or finding_id or "")
    62→    if "::holistic_unreviewed" in candidate_id or "::holistic_stale" in candidate_id:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    summary = str(finding.get("summary", "") or "").lower()
    66→    if "holistic" in summary and "review" in summary:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    70→    return bool(detail.get("holistic"))
    71→
    72→
    73→def subjective_review_open_breakdown(
    74→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    75→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    76→    """Return open subjective count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    77→    reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    holistic_reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    total = 0
    80→
    81→    for finding_id, finding in _iter_findings(findings):
    82→        if not is_subjective_review_open(finding):
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        total += 1
    86→        reason = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("reason", "other") or "other")
    87→        reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    88→
    89→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding, finding_id=finding_id):
    90→            holistic_reason_counts[reason] = holistic_reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    91→
    92→    return total, reason_counts, holistic_reason_counts
    93→
    94→
    95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []
   101→    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
   102→        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
   103→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   104→            continue
   105→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   106→        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
   107→            unassessed.append(name)
   108→            continue
   109→        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
   110→        issues = int(info.get("issues", 0))
   111→        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
   112→            unassessed.append(name)
   113→
   114→    unassessed.sort()
   115→    return unassessed
   116→
```

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→

1→"""Subjective scan reporting: common helpers, integrity checks, and output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import coerce_target_score
    10→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class SubjectiveFollowup:
    19→    threshold: float
    20→    threshold_label: str
    21→    low_assessed: list[dict]
    22→    rendered: str
    23→    command: str
    24→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None
    25→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    26→
    27→
    28→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    29→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    30→    seen: set[str] = set()
    31→    ordered: list[str] = []
    32→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    33→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    34→            if key in seen:
    35→                continue
    36→            ordered.append(key)
    37→            seen.add(key)
    38→    return ",".join(ordered)
    39→
    40→
    41→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    42→    return ", ".join(
    43→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    44→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    49→    count = len(entries)
    50→    names = ", ".join(
    51→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    52→    )
    53→    if count > max_names:
    54→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    55→    return names
    56→
    57→
    58→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    59→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    60→        return 0
    61→    if isinstance(value, int):
    62→        return value
    63→    if isinstance(value, float):
    64→        return int(value)
    65→    if isinstance(value, str):
    66→        try:
    67→            return int(value.strip())
    68→        except ValueError:
    69→            return 0
    70→    return 0
    71→
    72→
    73→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    74→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    75→        return []
    76→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    77→
    78→
    79→def subtract_reason_counts(
    80→    total_counts: dict[str, int],
    81→    scoped_counts: dict[str, int],
    82→) -> dict[str, int]:
    83→    """Subtract scoped reason counters from global counters."""
    84→    remainder: dict[str, int] = {}
    85→    for reason, total in total_counts.items():
    86→        delta = int(total) - int(scoped_counts.get(reason, 0))
    87→        if delta > 0:
    88→            remainder[reason] = delta
    89→    return remainder
    90→
    91→
    92→def subjective_rerun_command(
    93→    items: list[dict],
    94→    *,
    95→    max_items: int = 5,
    96→    refresh: bool = True,
    97→) -> str:
    98→    _ = refresh
    99→    dim_keys = flatten_cli_keys(items, max_items=max_items)
   100→    command_parts = [
   101→        "desloppify",
   102→        "review",
   103→        "--run-batches",
   104→        "--runner",
   105→        "codex",
   106→        "--parallel",
   107→        "--scan-after-import",
   108→    ]
   109→    if dim_keys:
   110→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   111→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   120→    return scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   121→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   122→    )
   123→
   124→
   125→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   126→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   127→) -> list[dict]:
   128→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   129→    for entry in entries:
   130→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   131→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   132→
   133→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   134→    for key in dimension_keys:
   135→        if key in by_key:
   136→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   137→            continue
   138→        mapped.append(
   139→            {
   140→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   141→                "score": 0.0,
   142→                "strict": 0.0,
   143→                "issues": 0,
   144→                "placeholder": False,
   145→                "cli_keys": [key],
   146→            }
   147→        )
   148→    return mapped
   149→
   150→
   151→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   154→    *,
   155→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   156→    max_items: int = 5,
   157→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   158→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   159→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   160→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   161→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   162→    )
   163→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   164→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   165→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   166→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   167→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   168→
   169→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   170→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   171→            reset_keys, subjective_entries
   172→        )
   173→        return {
   174→            "status": "penalized",
   175→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   176→            "target": target_display,
   177→            "entries": reset_entries,
   178→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   179→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   180→        }
   181→
   182→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   183→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   184→            matched_keys, subjective_entries
   185→        )
   186→        return {
   187→            "status": "warn",
   188→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   189→            "target": target_display,
   190→            "entries": matched_entries,
   191→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   192→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   193→        }
   194→
   195→    at_target = sorted(
   196→        [
   197→            entry
   198→            for entry in subjective_entries
   199→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   200→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   201→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   202→                threshold_value,
   203→            )
   204→        ],
   205→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   206→    )
   207→    if not at_target:
   208→        return None
   209→
   210→    return {
   211→        "status": "at_target",
   212→        "count": len(at_target),
   213→        "target": threshold_value,
   214→        "entries": at_target,
   215→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   216→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   217→    }
   218→
   219→
   220→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   221→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   222→    *,
   223→    fallback_target: float = 95.0,
   224→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   225→    if not integrity_notice:
   226→        return []
   227→
   228→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   229→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   230→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   231→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   232→        fallback=fallback_target,
   233→    )
   234→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   235→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   236→
   237→    if status == "penalized":
   238→        return [
   239→            (
   240→                "red",
   241→                "WARNING: "
   242→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   243→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   244→            ),
   245→            (
   246→                "yellow",
   247→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   248→            ),
   249→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   250→        ]
   251→
   252→    if status == "warn":
   253→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   254→        return [
   255→            (
   256→                "yellow",
   257→                "WARNING: "
   258→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   259→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   260→            ),
   261→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   262→        ]
   263→
   264→    if status == "at_target":
   265→        return [
   266→            (
   267→                "yellow",
   268→                "WARNING: "
   269→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   270→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   271→            ),
   272→        ]
   273→
   274→    return []
   275→
   276→
   277→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   278→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   279→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   280→
   281→
   282→def _subjective_reset_command(state: dict) -> str:
   283→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path")
   284→    if not isinstance(scan_path, str) or not scan_path.strip():
   285→        scan_path = "."
   286→    return f"`desloppify scan --path {scan_path} --reset-subjective`"
   287→
   288→
   289→def build_subjective_followup(
   290→    state: dict,
   291→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   292→    *,
   293→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   294→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   295→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   296→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   297→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   298→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   299→    low_assessed = sorted(
   300→        [
   301→            entry
   302→            for entry in subjective_entries
   303→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   304→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   305→        ],
   306→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   307→    )
   308→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   309→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   310→        low_assessed,
   311→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   312→    )
   313→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   314→        state,
   315→        subjective_entries,
   316→        threshold=threshold_value,
   317→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   318→    )
   319→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   320→        integrity_notice,
   321→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   322→    )
   323→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   324→        threshold=threshold_value,
   325→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   326→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   327→        rendered=rendered,
   328→        command=command,
   329→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   330→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   331→    )
   332→
   333→
   334→def show_subjective_paths(
   335→    state: dict,
   336→    dim_scores: dict,
   337→    *,
   338→    colorize_fn,
   339→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   340→    threshold: float = 95.0,
   341→    target_strict_score: float | None = None,
   342→) -> None:
   343→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold, fallback=95.0)
   344→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   345→    if not subjective_entries:
   346→        return
   347→
   348→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   349→        state,
   350→        subjective_entries,
   351→        threshold=threshold_value,
   352→        max_quality_items=3,
   353→        max_integrity_items=5,
   354→    )
   355→    unassessed = sorted(
   356→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   357→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   358→    )
   359→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   360→
   361→    all_findings = state.get("findings", {})
   362→    if not isinstance(all_findings, dict):
   363→        all_findings = {}
   364→    scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(all_findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   365→    coverage_in_scope, reason_counts_in_scope, holistic_reason_counts_in_scope = (
   366→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(scoped)
   367→    )
   368→    coverage_global, reason_counts_global, holistic_reason_counts_global = (
   369→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_findings)
   370→    )
   371→    if not all_findings and coverage_global == 0 and coverage_in_scope > 0:
   372→        # Test/mocked states may supply only scoped findings.
   373→        coverage_global = coverage_in_scope
   374→        reason_counts_global = dict(reason_counts_in_scope)
   375→        holistic_reason_counts_global = dict(holistic_reason_counts_in_scope)
   376→    coverage_out_of_scope = max(coverage_global - coverage_in_scope, 0)
   377→    reason_counts_out_of_scope = subtract_reason_counts(
   378→        reason_counts_global,
   379→        reason_counts_in_scope,
   380→    )
   381→    holistic_in_scope = sum(holistic_reason_counts_in_scope.values())
   382→    holistic_global = sum(holistic_reason_counts_global.values())
   383→    holistic_out_of_scope = max(holistic_global - holistic_in_scope, 0)
   384→    if (
   385→        not unassessed
   386→        and not low_assessed
   387→        and coverage_global <= 0
   388→        and not followup.integrity_notice
   389→    ):
   390→        return
   391→
   392→    print(colorize_fn("  Subjective path:", "cyan"))
   393→    print(
   394→        colorize_fn(
   395→            f"    Reset baseline from zero: {_subjective_reset_command(state)}",
   396→            "dim",
   397→        )
   398→    )
   399→    if target_strict_score is not None:
   400→        strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   401→        if strict_score is not None:
   402→            gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   403→            if gap > 0:
   404→                print(
   405→                    colorize_fn(
   406→                        f"    North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   407→                        "yellow",
   408→                    )
   409→                )
   410→            else:
   411→                print(
   412→                    colorize_fn(
   413→                        f"    North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   414→                        "green",
   415→                    )
   416→                )
   417→
   418→    if unassessed or holistic_global > 0:
   419→        integrity_bits: list[str] = []
   420→        if unassessed:
   421→            integrity_bits.append("unassessed subjective dimensions")
   422→        if holistic_global > 0:
   423→            integrity_bits.append("holistic review stale/missing")
   424→        integrity_label = " + ".join(integrity_bits)
   425→        print(colorize_fn(f"    High-priority integrity gap: {integrity_label}", "yellow"))
   426→        print(
   427→            colorize_fn(
   428→                "    Refresh baseline (Codex local): `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`",
   429→                "dim",
   430→            )
   431→        )
   432→        print(
   433→            colorize_fn(
   434→                "    Claude cloud durable path: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude`, then run the printed `--external-submit ... --scan-after-import` command",
   435→                "dim",
   436→            )
   437→        )
   438→        print(
   439→            colorize_fn(
   440→                "    Findings-only fallback: `desloppify review --prepare`, then `desloppify review --import findings.json && desloppify scan`",
   441→                "dim",
   442→            )
   443→        )
   444→
   445→    if low_assessed:
   446→        print(
   447→            colorize_fn(
   448→                f"    Quality below target (<{followup.threshold_label}%): {followup.rendered}",
   449→                "yellow",
   450→            )
   451→        )
   452→        print(
   453→            colorize_fn(
   454→                f"    Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
   455→                "dim",
   456→            )
   457→        )
   458→
   459→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   460→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {message}", style))
   461→
   462→    if unassessed:
   463→        rendered = ", ".join(entry["name"] for entry in unassessed[:3])
   464→        if len(unassessed) > 3:
   465→            rendered = f"{rendered}, +{len(unassessed) - 3} more"
   466→        print(colorize_fn(f"    Unassessed (0% placeholder): {rendered}", "yellow"))
   467→        print(
   468→            colorize_fn(
   469→                "    Start with holistic refresh, then tune specific dimensions.", "dim"
   470→            )
   471→        )
   472→
   473→    if coverage_global > 0:
   474→        detail = []
   475→        if reason_counts_in_scope.get("changed", 0) > 0:
   476→            detail.append(f"{reason_counts_in_scope['changed']} changed")
   477→        if reason_counts_in_scope.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:
   478→            detail.append(f"{reason_counts_in_scope['unreviewed']} unreviewed")
   479→        reason_text = ", ".join(detail) if detail else "stale/unreviewed"
   480→        suffix = "file" if coverage_in_scope == 1 else "files"
   481→        print(
   482→            colorize_fn(
   483→                f"    Coverage debt: {coverage_in_scope} {suffix} need review ({reason_text})",
   484→                "yellow",
   485→            )
   486→        )
   487→        print(
   488→            colorize_fn(
   489→                "    Scope split: "
   490→                f"open (in-scope) {coverage_in_scope} · "
   491→                f"open (out-of-scope carried) {coverage_out_of_scope} · "
   492→                f"open (global) {coverage_global}",
   493→                "yellow",
   494→            )
   495→        )
   496→        if reason_counts_out_of_scope:
   497→            out_detail: list[str] = []
   498→            if reason_counts_out_of_scope.get("changed", 0) > 0:
   499→                out_detail.append(f"{reason_counts_out_of_scope['changed']} changed")
   500→            if reason_counts_out_of_scope.get("unreviewed", 0) > 0:
   501→                out_detail.append(
   502→                    f"{reason_counts_out_of_scope['unreviewed']} unreviewed"
   503→                )
   504→            if not out_detail:
   505→                out_detail = [
   506→                    f"{count} {reason}"
   507→                    for reason, count in sorted(reason_counts_out_of_scope.items())
   508→                ]
   509→            print(
   510→                colorize_fn(
   511→                    "    Out-of-scope carried reasons: "
   512→                    + ", ".join(out_detail),
   513→                    "yellow",
   514→                )
   515→            )
   516→        if holistic_global > 0:
   517→            print(
   518→                colorize_fn(
   519→                    f"    Includes {holistic_global} holistic stale/missing signal(s)"
   520→                    f" ({holistic_in_scope} in-scope, {holistic_out_of_scope} out-of-scope carried).",
   521→                    "yellow",
   522→                )
   523→            )
   524→        print(
   525→            colorize_fn(
   526→                "    Triage: `desloppify show subjective_review --status open`", "dim"
   527→            )
   528→        )
   529→
   530→    print()
   531→
   532→

   535→    "build_subjective_followup",

1→"""Query output helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path
    10→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    11→from desloppify.core.query import write_query as _core_write_query
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueryWriter:
    17→    """Command-level query artifact writer with explicit failure policy."""
    18→
    19→    query_file: Path
    20→
    21→    def write(self, data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    22→        return _core_write_query(data, query_file=self.query_file)
    23→
    24→    def write_best_effort(self, data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    25→        result = self.write(data)
    26→        if not result.ok:
    27→            detail = result.message or "unknown write failure"
    28→            print(
    29→                colorize(
    30→                    f"  Warning: {context} succeeded but query artifact was not written ({detail}).",
    31→                    "yellow",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→        return result
    36→
    37→
    38→def query_writer() -> QueryWriter:
    39→    """Construct a writer bound to the active runtime query path."""
    40→    return QueryWriter(query_file=query_file_path())
    41→
    42→
    43→def write_query(data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    44→    """Write structured query output using default best-effort policy."""
    45→    return write_query_best_effort(data, context="query payload update")
    46→
    47→
    48→def write_query_best_effort(data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    49→    """Write query payload and emit a contextual warning on failure."""
    50→    return query_writer().write_best_effort(data, context=context)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "QueryWriter",
    55→    "query_file_path",
    56→    "query_writer",
    57→    "write_query",
    58→    "write_query_best_effort",
    59→]
    60→

1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "
   117→                    f"{noise_budget}/detector{global_label} "
   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )
   134→        )
   135→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   136→        print(
   137→            colorize(f"  {display_path}", "cyan")
   138→            + colorize(f"  ({len(findings)} findings)", "dim")
   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:
   142→            _print_single_finding(finding, show_code=show_code)
   143→        print()
   144→
   145→    if remaining_findings:
   146→        print(
   147→            colorize(
   148→                (
   149→                    f"  ... and {len(remaining_files)} more files "
   150→                    f"({remaining_findings} findings). "
   151→                    f"Use --top {top + 20} to see more.\n"
   152→                ),
   153→                "dim",
   154→            )
   155→        )
   156→
   157→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   158→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   159→    for finding in matches:
   160→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   161→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   164→    print(
   165→        colorize(
   166→            (
   167→                "    By tier:     "
   168→                + ", ".join(
   169→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   170→                )
   171→            ),
   172→            "dim",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    print(
   176→        colorize(
   177→            (
   178→                "    By detector: "
   179→                + ", ".join(
   180→                    f"{detector}:{count}"
   181→                    for detector, count in sorted(
   182→                        by_detector.items(), key=lambda item: -item[1]
   183→                    )
   184→                )
   185→            ),
   186→            "dim",
   187→        )
   188→    )
   189→    if hidden_total:
   190→        print(
   191→            colorize(
   192→                (
   193→                    "    Hidden:      "
   194→                    + ", ".join(
   195→                        f"{detector}:+{count}"
   196→                        for detector, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   197→                    )
   198→                ),
   199→                "dim",
   200→            )
   201→        )
   202→    print()
   203→
   204→
   205→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict, matches: list[dict]) -> None:
   206→    """Render a compact plan from current findings and narrative actions."""
   207→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   208→    if not actions and not matches:
   209→        return
   210→
   211→    print(
   212→        colorize(
   213→            "  AGENT PLAN (use `desloppify next --count 20` to inspect more items):",
   214→            "yellow",
   215→        )
   216→    )
   217→    if actions:
   218→        top = actions[0]
   219→        print(
   220→            colorize(
   221→                f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}",
   222→                "cyan",
   223→            )
   224→        )
   225→    elif matches:
   226→        first = matches[0]
   227→        print(
   228→            colorize(
   229→                "  Agent focus: `desloppify next --count 20` — "
   230→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   231→                "cyan",
   232→            )
   233→        )
   234→
   235→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   236→        print()
   237→
   238→
   239→def show_subjective_followup(state: dict, target_strict_score: float) -> None:
   240→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   241→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   242→    if not dim_scores:
   243→        return
   244→
   245→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   246→        state,
   247→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   248→    )
   249→    if not subjective:
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   253→        state,
   254→        subjective,
   255→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   256→        max_quality_items=3,
   257→        max_integrity_items=5,
   258→    )
   259→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   260→        print()
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "group_matches_by_file",
   265→    "render_findings",
   266→    "show_agent_plan",
   267→    "show_subjective_followup",
   268→    "write_show_output_file",
   269→]
   270→

1→"""Text templates for scan reporting output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from textwrap import dedent
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_workflow_guide(attest_example: str) -> str:
     9→    """Render the scan workflow guide with current attestation text."""
    10→    return dedent(
    11→        f"""
    12→        ## Workflow Guide
    13→
    14→        1. **Review findings first** (if any): `desloppify show review --status open` — high-value subjective findings
    15→        2. **Run auto-fixers** (if available): `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply
    16→        3. **Manual fixes**: `desloppify next` — highest-priority item. Fix it, then:
    17→           `desloppify resolve fixed "<id>" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    18→           Required attestation keywords: 'I have actually' and 'not gaming'.
    19→        4. **Rescan**: `desloppify scan --path <path>` — verify improvements, catch cascading effects
    20→        5. **Reset subjective baseline when needed**:
    21→           `desloppify scan --path <path> --reset-subjective` (then run a fresh review/import cycle)
    22→        6. **Check progress**: `desloppify status` — dimension scores dashboard
    23→
    24→        ### Decision Guide
    25→        - **Tackle**: T1/T2 (high impact), auto-fixable, security findings
    26→        - **Consider skipping**: T4 low-confidence, test/config zone findings (lower impact)
    27→        - **Wontfix**: Intentional patterns, false positives →
    28→          `desloppify resolve wontfix "<id>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    29→        - **Batch wontfix**: Multiple intentional patterns →
    30→          `desloppify resolve wontfix "<detector>::*::<category>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    31→
    32→        ### Understanding Dimensions
    33→        - **Mechanical** (File health, Code quality, etc.): Fix code → rescan
    34→        - **Subjective** (Naming quality, Logic clarity, etc.): Address review findings → re-review
    35→        - **Health vs Strict**: Health ignores wontfix; Strict penalizes it. Focus on Strict.
    36→        """
    37→    ).strip()
    38→
    39→
    40→__all__ = ["build_workflow_guide"]
    41→

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
    14→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
    15→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    16→    pattern = args.pattern
    17→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
    18→    if chronic:
    19→        scope = pattern
    20→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
    21→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
    22→    if not pattern:
    23→        print(
    24→            colorize(
    25→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
    26→                "yellow",
    27→            )
    28→        )
    29→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
    30→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
    31→
    32→
    33→def load_matches(
    34→    state: dict,
    35→    *,
    36→    scope: str | None,
    37→    status_filter: str,
    38→    chronic: bool,
    39→) -> list[dict]:
    40→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
    41→    queue = build_work_queue(
    42→        state,
    43→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    44→            count=None,
    45→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    46→            scope=scope,
    47→            status=status_filter,
    48→            include_subjective=False,
    49→            chronic=chronic,
    50→            no_tier_fallback=True,
    51→        ),
    52→    )
    53→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    54→
    55→
    56→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
    57→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
    58→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
    59→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
    60→    )
    61→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
    62→        matches,
    63→        budget=noise_budget,
    64→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
    65→    )
    66→    return (
    67→        surfaced_matches,
    68→        hidden_by_detector,
    69→        noise_budget,
    70→        global_noise_budget,
    71→        budget_warning,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = ["load_matches", "resolve_noise", "resolve_show_scope"]
    76→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    15→        "id": item.get("id"),
    16→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    17→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    18→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    19→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": item.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    24→        "status": item.get("status"),
    25→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→    }
    27→    explain = item.get("explain")
    28→    if explain is not None:
    29→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    30→    return serialized
    31→
    32→
    33→def build_query_payload(
    34→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    35→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    36→    *,
    37→    command: str,
    38→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    39→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    40→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    41→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    42→    return {
    43→        "command": command,
    44→        "items": serialized,
    45→        "queue": {
    46→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    47→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    48→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    49→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    50→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    51→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    52→        },
    53→        "narrative": narrative,
    54→    }
    55→
    56→
    57→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
    58→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
    59→    lines = [
    60→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
    61→        "",
    62→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
    63→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
    64→    ]
    65→    for item in items:
    66→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    67→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    68→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    69→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
    70→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
    71→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
    72→    lines.append("")
    73→    return "\n".join(lines)
    74→
    75→
    76→def write_output_file(
    77→    output_file: str,
    78→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    79→    item_count: int,
    80→    *,
    81→    safe_write_text_fn,
    82→    colorize_fn,
    83→) -> bool:
    84→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
    85→    try:
    86→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    87→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
    88→    except OSError as exc:
    89→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    90→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
    91→        return False
    92→    return True
    93→
    94→
    95→def emit_non_terminal_output(
    96→    output_format: str,
    97→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    98→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    99→) -> bool:
   100→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   101→    renderers = {
   102→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   103→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   104→    }
   105→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   106→    if renderer is None:
   107→        return False
   108→    renderer()
   109→    return True
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = [
   113→    "build_query_payload",
   114→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   115→    "render_markdown",
   116→    "serialize_item",
   117→    "write_output_file",
   118→]
   119→
```

    23→class TestFormatDetail:

    26→    def test_empty_detail(self):
    27→        assert format_detail({}) == []
    28→
    29→    def test_simple_string_fields(self):
    30→        parts = format_detail({"category": "imports", "kind": "default"})
    31→        assert "category: imports" in parts
    32→        assert "kind: default" in parts
    33→
    34→    def test_line_number(self):
    35→        parts = format_detail({"line": 42})
    36→        assert "line: 42" in parts
    37→
    38→    def test_lines_list_truncated(self):
    39→        parts = format_detail({"lines": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]})
    40→        # Only first 5 should appear
    41→        lines_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("lines:")]
    42→        assert len(lines_part) == 1
    43→        assert "6" not in lines_part[0]
    44→        assert "1" in lines_part[0]
    45→
    46→    def test_signals_list(self):
    47→        parts = format_detail({"signals": ["a", "b", "c", "d"]})
    48→        sig_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("signals:")][0]
    49→        # Only first 3
    50→        assert "a" in sig_part
    51→        assert "c" in sig_part
    52→        assert "d" not in sig_part
    53→
    54→    def test_importers_zero_is_shown(self):
    55→        """importers=0 is meaningful and should be displayed."""
    56→        parts = format_detail({"importers": 0})
    57→        assert "importers: 0" in parts
    58→
    59→    def test_importers_none_is_hidden(self):
